# wow-souls — Handover

**For a fresh chat:** read this file, then `README.md`, then go to the section ">>> START HERE" (line ~354, under Plan and status), which says exactly what to do next (sound check; the menu was built 2026-10-12 in three parts and Dark Souls locomotion the same day, see the first entries of "Next action"; the newest is the enemy footwork and the lively player, 2026-10-13). Then continue at "Next action" below.
Keep this file current after every step (it is the session's memory).

## What this is

A single-player prototype: the World of Warcraft 1.12.1 world (Northshire, from the user's own
install) with Dark Souls Remastered-style third-person action combat. Rust + Bevy 0.18.1 on
benilla's world engine. No server, no login, no WoW UI, no network, no retail client involved.
The user (Swedish, writes English) wants small verified steps; they said "continue" means: take
the next step in the plan below, verify it live, update this file.

## Standing orders from the user (2026-10-09; do not ask again)

- **Model choice is Claude's call, per task.** Go down (Agent tool `model: "haiku"` or `"sonnet"`, or the session
  model) for mechanical work: data transcription, SQL export, quest/NPC tables, running the three gates,
  contact sheets, reading logs, a species row on an existing kit. Go up (`"opus"` / `"fable"`) for design and specs,
  new extractor or retarget work, hard debugging, anything judged by measurement, and review. Nothing needs the
  user's OK to switch either way. Whoever does the work still verifies it (rerun the gates, read the numbers) and
  writes the result here.
- **The data source for spawns and quests is on disk:** `C:\Spel\MaNGOS` is a vMaNGOS repack (1.12 server) with a
  MyISAM world database (`mysql5\data\mangos`, 145 MB: `creature`, `creature_template`, `quest_template`,
  `creature_questrelation`, `creature_involvedrelation`, `gameobject*`, `npc_vendor`, `creature_movement`...).
  No download is needed. Never start the repack's own server against its own data dir: copy the `mangos` and
  `mysql` dirs to a scratch dir and query that copy (see "Quests: data import" below). The WoW client itself has no
  creature, spawn or quest data (the `WDB` caches are empty), that was the earlier dead end.
- **Critters: yes**, as in WoW Classic (they give the valley life); take them from the database like any spawn.
- **Respawn: yes**, with a long timer: the player must be able to walk into a cave and out again without anything
  having respawned behind them (it is a Souls game, difficulty is high). The time is ours to tune; prefer minutes, and
  never respawn a creature while the player is near or in sight of its spot.
- **Order: quests first, XP after.** All Northshire Valley quests exactly as WoW Classic has them, with every NPC (givers,
  receivers, trainers, vendors, quest objects) at its correct place, and every monster at its correct spawn
  position from the database (replacing today's hand-placed `slice.rs` groups), with the right species per spawn.
- **Every mob has its own area where it walks around; none just stands still, as in WoW** (the user, 2026-10-09). The
  database says how: `creature.movement_type` 0 stands (NPC posts, a few thugs), 1 wanders at random within
  `wander_distance` yards of its spawn point (about 190 of the 334 valley spawns: kobolds 5-10 yd, wolves 5-10 and 10+,
  rabbits and young wolves 10+, deer), 2 follows its waypoint path in `creature_movement` (18 spawns: 9 thugs, 5 peasants,
  3 guards, 1 worker; `waittime` ms at each point). G1 must give every spawn that behaviour (pick a random point inside the
  radius, walk there with the creature's walk speed and walk clip, pause a few seconds, repeat; stop and fight when it
  aggroes, walk home after the leash) and critters flee. The importer carries `wander` / `movement` in each `Spawn` and
  (G0b) the paths as `Waypoint` rows.
- **Movement mix and density (the user's correction, 2026-10-09, after G1a):** "vissa står still, vissa ska roama, vissa har liten roaming
  andra större": keep exactly the database's mix (stand / small radius 5-7 yd / large radius 10 yd / waypoint path): do NOT add a default
  radius for the standing ones. And **"vi skulle kunna halvera antalet npcs, just nu blir det lite för mycket"**: thin the hostile and critter
  spawns to about half (default `WOWSOULS_DENSITY=0.5`, 0 to 1). Rules (decided by Claude, to build as step G1c-density in `world.rs`):
  keep friendly NPCs and Garrick whole; thin by alternating guid order inside each entry so every camp stays populated (guids are allocated
  per camp); and **never below what the quests need without waiting for a respawn** (the respawn is 18 minutes): Kobold Laborer (80) needs 12
  kills for Skirmish at Echo Ridge and the valley has only 17, so its floor is 15 (halving would leave 9 and make the quest impossible);
  floors for the others are far below half their count (Vermin 33 -> 17 vs 10 needed, Worker 43 -> 22 vs 10, Thug 77 -> 39 vs 15 at an 80 %
  bandana drop for 12, wolves 73 -> 36 vs 10 at 80 % meat for 8). A thinned spot is `State::Off`. Make the floors a table next to the thinning
  and let G3 compute them from the real quest requirements later.
- **Parry / block:** the read-only survey was completed and reported. The user then asked this chat to build
  player block/parry alongside the ongoing world/quest work and to check whether dodge was fat roll.
  **Built and verified; see the parallel combat note under Next action.**
- **Loot / exploration direction (2026-10-09):** keep WoW quests and baseline loot; add our own secrets and
  fixed rewards (the user's explicit choice). Loot interactions must use the correct contextual animations,
  including the player opening chests and the real lid movement. The latest visual direction supersedes
  the initial original-art idea: use as much real WoW / Dark Souls art as possible. The user supplied
  a DS souls-counter panel as the pickup frame reference, requested real WoW item icons (Broken Fang
  etc.) and WoW's exact blinking loot VFX. They also want ER-style hidden discoveries around Northshire.
  Research and proposed first slice: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-09-loot-ui-research.md`.
  **First loot slice approved with "Kör" and built; see the parallel loot note under Next action.**
  Native DS / WoW art is authorized for the next UI steps. This work does not stop ongoing world/quest work.
  **Chest placement correction:** remove the chest from the chapel passage and put discoveries at
  logical hidden sites that require searching. Built and verified: three authored caches on optional
  detours; see `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-09-treasure-placements-built.md`.

## Build, run, verify

```bash
cd /c/Spel/wow-souls && cargo run -j 4 -p wow-souls
```

- Always `-j 4`: the machine is RAM-tight (other apps hold ~15 GB); full parallel builds hit
  Windows error 1455. Cold build ~10 min, incremental ~30 s.
- Gates every step: `cargo fmt -p wow-souls -p wow-world -p wscl -p dsr-cache -p dsr-formats -- --check` (NOT `cargo fmt --all`: it also checks and, without `--check`, rewrites the path-dependency checkout `C:/Spel/research/benilla-everwood`, whose code is not in our rustfmt style), `cargo clippy -j 4 -p wow-souls --all-targets
  -- -D warnings`, `cargo test -j 4 -p wow-souls` (97 tests at end of milestone 1, 129 after M2 step 5,
  188 before sub-project E, 202 after, 217 after sound, 225 after D2a, 229 after D2b, 238 after D3, 279 after F, 286 after E2-3a (dsr-cache 50), 290 after E2-3 (dsr-cache 51), 386 after G4a with the peers' loot / defense / quest work (wow-world 126 + 4, dsr-cache 53), 394 after G4b, wow-souls 525 + wow-world 145 after the equipment system, wow-souls 687 + wow-world 159 after the menu (parts 1-3); run the same three with `--workspace` to cover the extractor
  crates too: dsr-formats 51, dsr-cache 42, wscl 4). The install tests need
  `DSR_DIR="C:/Spel/Dark Souls Remastered" WOW_DATA="C:/Spel/World of Warcraft 1.12/Data"` in the same
  shell command (without them they skip silently and finish in 0.01 s).
- Live verification is unattended (instrumented runs open unfocused, never grab the cursor,
  ignore live input; see `src/run_mode.rs`). Typical run:
  ```bash
  WOWSOULS_CHECK=8 WOWSOULS_DRIVE=0,1 WOWSOULS_TRACE=1 WOWSOULS_SHOT='C:\path\shot.png' WOWSOULS_SHOT_AT=5 timeout 90 ./target/debug/wow-souls.exe
  ```
  Switches: `WOWSOULS_CHECK[=s]` (exit non-zero on ECS faults), `_SHOT`/`_SHOT_AT`, `_DRIVE=x,y[,jump_s]`,
  `_SPIN=rad/s`, `_LOCK=s` (press lock), `_ATTACK=s[,every]` (R1), `_HEAVY=s[,every]` (R2), `_ROLL=s[,every]` (evade, aimed by `_DRIVE`), `_DODGE=d[,x,y]` (evade d s after each enemy wind-up), `_KOBOLD_AT=x,y`, `_TRACE=1` (body/camera/gait logs), `_OBSERVE[=x,y,z]`
  (overhead debug view of the camera rig), `_AT`, `_KOBOLD_AT`, `_LOOK`, `_WIN`, `_BG`, `_DSVIEW=<clip>`
  (side-by-side of a cached Dark Souls clip, `1` = roll, `a23_3000`/`r1_1` = a sword move, one
  `blade error` line per loop), `_GRIP=rx,ry,rz` (held-sword rotation while DS clips pose the body),
  `_DSR_CACHE=<dir>`; kobold (sub-project E): `_DSVIEW=kobold:<clip>[@<s>]` (the kobold plays a kit clip,
  a move name such as `small_1` or a clip name such as `a00_2202`, beside the ghoul's gold skeleton,
  looping or held at second `<s>`; use `_OBSERVE=7,1.3,-1.5` with `_AT=-8913,-116 _KOBOLD_AT=-8913,-119`
  for a view from the open meadow: the observer offset is in BEVY axes, x east, y up, z south),
  `_MOVE=<name>` (the kobold's next attack, once some band fits), `_SEED=<n>` (its dice); wolves and thieves (E2): `_WOLVES=<0-2>` / `_THIEVES=<0-2>` (default 2 attended, 0 unattended or 1 with `_WOLF_AT` / `_THIEF_AT`), `_WOLF_AT=x,y[,z]`, `_THIEF_AT=x,y[,z]`; valley kinds (E2-3a): `_VERMIN=<0-3>` / `_WORKERS=<0-2>` / `_YOUNG_WOLVES=<0-2>`, `_VERMIN_AT` / `_WORKER_AT` / `_YOUNG_WOLF_AT=x,y[,z]`; extractor look-dev `WOWSOULS_LOOKDEV=1`. Sound: `_AUDIO=0|1|null`, `_VOLUME=<0..1>`.
  HUD and group (F): `_KOBOLDS=<n>` (0-4; default 4 attended, 1 unattended or with `_KOBOLD_AT`), `_RESET=<s>` (the F6 reset), `_SHOT_DEATH=<s>[,<s>...]` (with `_SHOT`: frames after the player's first death), `_HUD=0` (plain HUD), `_HUD_GAIN=<x>` (brighten the art).
- Flat ground for measuring a lunge: `WOWSOULS_AT=-8906,-136 WOWSOULS_KOBOLD_AT=-8960,-136` (the
  default anchor has a 0.8 yd ridge 3 yd north of it, which stops a lunge short).
- Survey a Dark Souls creature (read-only): `./target/debug/wow-souls-dsr.exe survey --chr c3340 --npc 334000` (needs the extractor built: `cargo build -j 4 -p dsr-cache --bin wow-souls-dsr`).
- Install untouched check: snapshot `find "/c/Spel/World of Warcraft 1.12" -type f -not -name realmlist.wtf -printf '%p %s %T@\n' | sort` and the same for `"/c/Spel/Dark Souls Remastered"` before/after and diff.
- Read screenshots with the Read tool; prove numerically with `WOWSOULS_TRACE` before trusting a frame.

## Architecture (crates/wow-souls/src)

| Module | Role |
|---|---|
| `main.rs`, `boot.rs` | bootstrap in benilla worldview order; `WorldPlugins` + our plugins |
| `run_mode.rs`, `check.rs`, `shot.rs` | unattended mode, fault gate, screenshot instrument |
| `slice.rs` | anchor (Abbey doors, wow `-8913,-136,82`), kobold spot (`-8888,-132`) |
| `settle.rs` | `Phase` Streaming→Playing; hold until `WorldLoadProgress::presentable()`; ground snap; `ViewFocus`/`Viewer` |
| `loading.rs`, `hud.rs` | our own loading cover and one-line dev readout (ASCII only) |
| `motor/` | benilla's kinematic mover ported (+48 of its tests); `MoveIntent` in, `Motor` state (velocity = achieved) |
| `controller.rs` | camera-relative input → `MoveIntent`; facing; `PlaySet {Intent, Motor, Camera}` in `WorldStage::Input`; void-out |
| `camera.rs` | Souls orbit cam: `cast_camera` sweep from head, snap in/ease out, smoothed pivot, recentre, `BodyFade`, lock tracking |
| `freecam.rs` | F1 debug camera |
| `avatar.rs` | player body entity (display 49 human male, look), capsule gizmo F2 |
| `actor/` | `ActorModel` → skinned M2 (geosets, CharSections composite, palette rig, parts); a `ModelFrame` child of the body is the rig's `joints_root` and parent of the parts and hand anchors (identity unless the roll tumbles it); `anim.rs` gait FSM (Stand/Walk/Run/Jump*/Fall/Backpedal/Strafe) |
| `npc.rs` | kobold display 365, 60 HP, `Hostile`, `Brain`/`Mind` AI (see step 5), `Home`, `Reaction {None, Flinch, Dead}`, kinematic `BodyCollider`; `react` plays CombatWound 9 / Death 1, on death removes `Lockable` + `Hurtbox` + `BodyCollider`; messages `PlayerHurt`, `Telegraph`; traces `trace_dead`, `trace_body`, `trace_swing` |
| `hurt.rs` | player struck: stagger / death, YOU DIED, respawn, `Respawned` |
| `hitbody.rs` | `BodyHits`: one capsule per skinning bone, fitted once per creature model from its `RenderSubmesh` positions/joints/weights (dominant bone, principal axis, radius covering 85 %), posed by `RigPalettes::world_palette` (the GPU's skinning rows); `strike` tests the swept blade against them, the feet capsule `Hurtbox` is the fallback; F2 draws them orange |
| `lockon.rs` | `Lockable`, `LockOn`, pick/score/break, marker; target switching: `next_target`, `Flick`, `switch`, `WOWSOULS_LOCK_SWITCH` |
| `combat/mod.rs` | `Health`, `Stamina`, the plugin and systems: `act` (PlaySet::Intent after `read_intent`) feeds `step_action` the presses (F / LMB / RB = `Press::Attack`, R / RMB / RT = `Press::Heavy`, Space / B = `Press::Evade`; scripted `_ATTACK` / `_HEAVY` / `_ROLL`), plays the procedural swing's WoW clip via `play_once` (a Dark Souls move poses the rig in `dsclip`), overrides `MoveIntent` with the lunge (`root_step`: a Dark Souls move's root motion frame by frame, recoil included; the procedural swing's `LUNGE_SPEED` rule); `tumble` writes the somersault onto `ModelFrame` (`tumble_transform`); `anim::drive` yields while `Action::owns_body()` |
| `combat/action.rs` | the pure action machine: `Action {state: Idle / Attack(Swing) / Evade(Evade) / Stagger / Dead, buffer}`, `SwingSpec {duration, hit, chain_from, evade_from, move_from, cost, mult}` (f4 chain, f26 evade, f11 movement cancel), `Moveset {r1[3], r2, roll_attack, ds}`, `MoveKind {R1(n), R2(n), RollAttack}`, `Press`, `step_action(a, stamina, StepInput, dt) -> Decision`, `next_move`, `start`; evades `ROLL`/`BACKSTEP` (`EvadeSpec`), `Action::invulnerable()`; the procedural swing is `SwingSpec::procedural(len)`, pinned to the old phase fractions by a test |
| `hits.rs` | `Hurtbox` (vertical capsule), `Hit` message, `BladeTrail`; `strike` sweeps [tip marker, hand] prev->now in `SwingPhase::Active` (pure `segment_distance`, `blade_hits`); damage `hit_damage` = `LIGHT_DAMAGE` 20 x the move's `mult`; a swing's `serial` lets a chained or looped move strike again; `BLADE_RADIUS` 0.18; hit-stop via `Time<Virtual>` relative speed 0.05 for 0.07 s real; `flash_impacts` gizmo burst |
| `hud/mod.rs` | the HUD plugin: dev line, help line, F3; loads `HudArt` at startup (`WOWSOULS_HUD=0` skips it), `UiScale = window height / 720` (the layout is 1280 x 720), `node_at(rect, Anchor)` places a layout rectangle from the left / right / centre edge, `sprite_node`, `clip_sprite`, `WOWSOULS_HUD_GAIN` |
| `hud/art.rs` | `cache/dsr/hud/manifest.txt` as data (`Manifest`: dialogs, places, sprites, rects, gradients, texts, font), `check_sources`, `HudArt` (manifest + one `Handle<Image>` per PNG, read with the `png` crate), `REQUIRED_*` keys checked at load |
| `hud/font.rs` | `layout` (pure: `pen + pre`, `pen += advance`, scale `size / font.size`, align) and `BitmapText` (children = one `ImageNode` with a page rectangle per glyph) |
| `hud/bars.rs` | player bars: plate, `Chip` (the pale-yellow lost-value layer: holds, then drains), fill, ruler, end cap, the bar head and the skull; length = max x 3 layout px |
| `hud/souls.rs` | `Souls` (what the player carries), `Counter` (the rolling count and the gain above it), the widget bottom right, `credit` (`Slain` -> souls) |
| `hud/enemy.rs` | an enemy bar per struck creature (up to 8): `enemy_HP` sprites, name and damage run in the font, `Run`, `Chip`, projected over the head |
| `hud/lock.rs`, `hud/banner.rs` | the lock-on ring (replaces the gizmo dot), YOU DIED (dim, band with soft edges, text, fade to black; `phase`, `ds_alpha`) |
| `hud/plain.rs` | the old rectangle bars, only without `HudArt` |
| `dsclip.rs`, `dsview.rs` | the DS roll and the six sword moves from `cache/dsr/` (manifest: version, `move` lines, `grip`; clips give `EvadeSpec` / `SwingSpec`, root motion `progress`, the `PosePost` pose with blends and a chained clip fading over the last one's end pose; the held sword's blade turned onto DS's blade axis while clips pose the body, `grip_rotation`); `WOWSOULS_DSVIEW=<clip>` side-by-side with the per-loop `blade error` line |
| `monster.rs` | the kobold's Dark Souls kit from `cache/dsr/kobold/` (manifest `monster`/`attack`/`react`/`death`/`think`/`reach` lines): `MonsterClips` (six `AttackSpec`s with hit window, damage `atkPhys x DAMAGE_K` (K = 25/138), poise, level, radius; nine flinch clips with measured head push; two deaths), `MonsterPlay`/`MonsterFade` (the clip on the kobold's `RigPose` in `PosePost`, the player's blend code), `Poise`, `choose_react`/`choose_death` (the clip whose measured push points most like the blow's), `FoeRng`, `forced_move` |
| `pattern.rs` | the kobold's pattern table (hand-made, the ghoul's Lua AI is not read): `wish` (a move while closing in), `choose` (start it once the player is in its band, else what fits), `follow_up` (small_1 -> small_2 40 %, small_2 -> big 30 %); bands in kobold yards, tuned live |
| `foehit.rs` | `touches`: the kobold's hand volume (capsule from its right hand along its forearm, `REACH_LENGTH` 0.55 yd) swept against the player's hurtbox |
| `blood.rs` | blood: `BloodTables`, `is_front` / `is_large`, `Spurt` roots on the victim's attachment 15 / 16 anchor, WoW's `BloodSpurt[Large].m2` emitters via `spawn_emitter`, reaper, `WOWSOULS_BLOOD=0` |
| `reaction.rs` | the player's DS reactions: `PlayerReacts` from the cache (`preact` / `pdeath`), `LEVEL_CLASSES` / `class_for`, `choose` (by blow level and push), `choose_death`, `player_poise` |
| `shove.rs` | knock-back: `Shove` component slid through `MoveIntent` (eased, 0.30 s, `knock` m x 1.0936), `start` (log line), `push_avatar`, `push_hostiles`, respawn clear |
| `shake.rs` | camera shake: `Shake` trauma resource, `trauma_for(level, taken)`, `shake_offset`, applied in `camera::seat`; `WOWSOULS_SHAKE=0` |
| `glide.rs` | (I1) inertia for the player's own walking: `follow` (pure: `ACCEL` 40, `BRAKE` 60 yd/s²), used by `controller::glide_intent`; `WOWSOULS_GLIDE=0` |
| `lively.rs` | (I2) lean (acceleration / turn) and head look as additive rotations on spine, chest, neck, head after the clip layers; `Lively`, `lean_for`, `look_yaw`, `WOWSOULS_LIVELY` |
| `footwork.rs` | (H) what an engaged creature does between swings: `Temper` per species, `Foot` in `Brain`, `step` -> `Plan`, `world_motion`, `MAX_COMMITTED`; `WOWSOULS_FOOTWORK=0` |
| `boss.rs` | a boss's second phase (`phase_for`, `Phase::mods`, `Tempo` read by `npc::think` and `monster::advance`), Pierce Armor (`ArmorShred` resource, +8 % damage taken a stack, 3 stacks, 30 s), the `Engaged` cue the boss theme follows, `Roar`, slam shake, `music_playlist`, `WOWSOULS_BOSS_HP` |
| `trial.rs` | `--trial-character <level> [slot]`: writes a level 6-12 save slot (the same text formats the game reads), never over an existing character |
| `crates/wow-world` | (a library crate, not game code) Northshire from the vMaNGOS DB: `tsv`, `model`, `db`, `valley` (ADT area ids, `EXTRA_VALLEY_AREAS`), `select`, `manifest` (`wsworld 1`); bin `wow-souls-world import|areas|summary`; the game will read `cache/world/northshire.txt` through `manifest::parse` (G1) |
| `world.rs`, `ring.rs`, `roam.rs` | (G1a) the manifest as `Book` / `WorldBook` of `Spot`s with a `Kind` (Foe by species name / Critter / Friendly / Unmapped); `ring_tick` (PreUpdate) spawns records inside 100 yd, despawns beyond 130, replaces killed ones after `max(600 s, x4 DB time)` only with the player 60+ yd away; `roam` is the pure walking rules (`Area::{Stand, Wander(r), Path(nodes)}`, `step`, `home_distance`); `npc::place` builds one creature; `SpawnId` links entity to record |
| `bloodstain.rs` | (G4b) the death rule for souls: `Stain`, `die` / `near` / `recover` (pure), the `wssouls 1` save, `Bloodstain` resource, the systems `drop_on_death` / `pick_up` / `sync_mark` (disc + `LootFX` sparkles) / `save`, `WOWSOULS_SOULS_SCRIPT` |
| `level.rs` | (G4a) `vitals(level)` (HP 100 + 10 per level, stamina 100 + 5), `follow` (pure) and the system that applies `QuestGame.player.level` to the avatar's `Health` / `Stamina` maxima; kill XP itself is `wow_world::quest::xp` + `quests::credit_kills` |
| `actor/held.rs` | `HeldItem` (ItemDisplayInfo → `Item\ObjectComponents\Weapon\` M2) on attachment 1 via `RigPose::anchor_for`; `HeldBuilt { root, blade: [$WTT, $WTB] }` in the item root's frame; `HandGrip` plays `hand_close[0]` at weight 8 |
| `actor/mod.rs` `Dress` | (equipment) a character body's gear as display ids (`worn[10]`, `cloak`); `build` reads it like `NpcWorn`, redoes the parts when it changes (`DressParts`, `WornRoot`) |
| `equip/mod.rs` | (equipment) `Appearance`, `appearance` / `wanted` / `dress_new` / `apply` (what the avatar shows), `Request` / `attempt` / `act` (rules on a copy of the save, then publish), `GearScreen`, `neighbor`, `candidates`, `tooltip`, `WearOverride`, the kit and the debug switches |
| `equip/ui.rs` | (equipment) the character pane page: slots in 1.12's order with WoW's empty-slot art, the choice list, the tooltip panel, mouse / key / scripted input |
| `crates/wow-world/src/equipment.rs` | (equipment, pure) `Slot`, `slots_for`, `Wearer`, `can_wear`, `Gear::{plan, best_slot}`, `Refusal`, `totals`, `start_kit`, the save lines |
| `menu.rs` | the title and character flow before play: `Menu { screen, cursor, existing, sel, .. }`, the `Row` focus ring of the creation screen, `Press` / `MenuPress`, `Outcome::{Enter, Options, Create, Replace, Quit}`, `WOWSOULS_MENU_SCRIPT` |
| `create/` | (menu part 1) WoW's character creation: `rules.rs` (pure `Rules`, `Selection`), `strings.rs` (GlueStrings.lua), `art.rs` (the glue sheets as sprites), `ui.rs` (the screen), `preview.rs` (the 3D body); `character.rs` has `Dials` and the `wschar 2` save |
| `settings/` | (menu part 2) `Settings { sound, video, game, binds }` and `settings-v1.txt`; `binds.rs`: 31 `Action`s, `Chord`, two keys + one pad button, `Controls` / `MenuUp` (every input reader goes through it) |
| `sysmenu/` | (menu part 3) `mod.rs` the pure model (`SysMenu`, `Page`, `Row`, `Kind`, capture), `ui.rs` the panel, the input, the pause, `WOWSOULS_SYSMENU_SCRIPT` |

Engine facts worth remembering:
- WoW coords: **+X north, +Y west**, +Z up. `wow_to_bevy([x,y,z]) = (-y, z, -x)`. Bevy forward −Z = WoW north. The Abbey is EAST of the anchor (lower Y).
- Collision layers (benilla `CollisionLayer`, crate-private): bit0 Default, bit1 Walk (body only), bit2 Camera (camera only), bit3 Liquid. Avian dynamics are disabled: use spatial queries, not contacts.
- Animation: Bevy `AnimationPlayer`/`AnimationTransitions` on the root; benilla poses from it. Clip lookup `ModelAnimations::find(anim_id)`. HumanMale has no roll clip. JumpStart (0.833 s) covers the whole 0.825 s running jump (by design).
- Parts: `MeshTag(spawn_tag(slot, alpha))`, `RigPart(root)`, `ModelPart`, `part_interior_lit`.

## Gotchas

- **`cargo fmt --all` without `--check` rewrites the benilla-everwood checkout** (it formats path dependencies too; 64 files at 22:09 on 2026-10-10, restored with `git checkout`). Use `cargo fmt --all -- --check` as the gate, and `cargo fmt -p <crate>` or `rustfmt --edition 2021 <file>` to fix formatting. A doubled backslash in a Bash-tool heredoc arrives as one, so a Python edit script that writes a Windows path or a literal backslash-r gets control characters (a CR, a backspace): write such text with the Write / Edit tool or `chr(92)`.
- **Never run `cargo fmt --all` without `--check` in this workspace**: it also formats the path dependencies, i.e. the benilla fork checkout `C:\Spel\research\benilla-everwood` (it silently rewrote 64 of its files on 2026-10-10 and forced a rebuild of the benilla crates). Not even `--check` is right there: it also checks the fork, whose code is not in our rustfmt style, so it fails. The gate is package-scoped: `cargo fmt -p wow-souls -p wow-world -p wscl -p dsr-cache -p dsr-formats -- --check`; fix with the same without `--check`.

- Python edit scripts: rustfmt rewraps lines, so an assert-match can fail mid-script (earlier files already written). Re-read and re-apply; prefer `Write` of a script file over bash heredocs with `'''`.
- Strafing right from the anchor runs into the Abbey; west (stick left) is open meadow.
- `WOWSOULS_TRACE` gait lines come from PostUpdate; the body trace from Update (one frame apart).

## Plan and status

Milestone 1 (Northshire slice: world, human, Souls camera, movement, one hostile, lock-on) — **done**.

Hitbox fix (after the user reported "the hit box is not quite 100%", sword hits/misses): measured,
not guessed. (1) The kobold's vertical feet capsule did not match its drawn body (hunched, head and
arms 0.6-0.75 yd forward of its feet, moving with attack/flinch/death clips): hits registered in
the empty capsule top over its back, blows through its head in front of the capsule missed. Fixed
with `hitbody.rs` bone capsules. (2) `strike` read the previous frame's pose in `Update`; it now
runs in `PostUpdate` after `benilla_world::billboard::BillboardPlace` (the final pose), as does the
F2 blade line. (3) With honest hits, the light attack connects from 1.1 to 1.6 yd centre to centre
(best ~1.35), so `LUNGE_STOP_GAP` went 0.9 -> 0.6. Remaining misses in scripted fights are real:
the 1H arc runs high-right to low-left, and a kobold that drifts to the player's front-right or
flinches back is visibly not touched. Not WoW weapon skill: there is no combat roll anywhere.
Instruments: `WOWSOULS_TRACE` `blade ... d= parts= cap=` lines; screenshots `hitbox-*.png`
(old capsule), `hitparts-*.png`, `top-*.png` in the evidence folder.

Milestone 2 — combat (in progress):

1. **Done.** Weapon in hand: rusty short sword (ItemDisplayInfo 1542, `Sword_1H_Short_A_02`) on attachment 1; grip overlay; blade markers verified on the drawn blade (`WOWSOULS_SHOW_BODY=1`).
2. **Done.** Combat core: `Health`/`Stamina`, HUD bars, `Action` machine with 0.35 s buffer; light attack LMB / R1 (Bevy `RightTrigger`) = Attack1H (17) at 1.25x = 0.80 s; active 30-55 %, cancel/chain from 70 %; cost 18; regen 45/s after 0.6 s; verified live: chain every ~0.56 s, 100->82->64->46->28->10->0, then one swing per regen tick (any stamina starts an action, Souls rule).
3. **Done.** Hits verified live: kobold 2 yd ahead (`WOWSOULS_KOBOLD_AT=-8911,-136`, `WOWSOULS_ATTACK=3.0,0.9`): 60 -> 40 -> 20 -> 0, dies, death pose holds. Found by measurement: the human Attack1H crosses a 1.3 yd kobold ~0.5 yd above its head line, so (a) the hitbox runs tip-to-hand with radius 0.18, (b) the lunge stops within 0.9 yd of an enemy ahead (`combat::enemy_in_reach`), or point-blank swings whiff over it.
4. **Done.** Dodge roll: Space / gamepad East (jump moved to Shift+Space; pad South stays jump). Stick pushed (> 0.2) = roll along it, body snaps to face the roll; stick still = backstep (Dodge clip 30, 1.8 yd / 0.55 s, i-frames 0.03-0.25, 15 stamina). Roll: 4 yd over 0.6 s, speed `1.5(1-u²)` x average (fast first), recovery to 0.8 s, i-frames 0.05-0.42 s, 20 stamina, cancel/buffer from 0.6 s; rolls cancel a swing from its recovery (`ACTIVE_TO`), a swing chains from `CANCEL_FROM`. Visual: StealthStand (120) crouch looping, `ModelFrame` turned one forward somersault (smoothstep over the travel) about 0.7 yd over the feet. `face_motion` holds facing during an evade. Verified live: roll 4.00 yd in 0.80-0.82 s (open meadow, `WOWSOULS_DRIVE=-1,0 WOWSOULS_ROLL=4,2`), one roll into an obstacle slid 1.53 yd; backstep 1.80 yd in 0.55 s; locked-on side roll 4.00 yd then back to StrafeLeft facing the kobold; screenshots `m2s4-roll-4.18.png` (~95°, head leading) and `m2s4-roll-4.30.png` (inverted). Known look issue: StealthStand is a crouch, not a tucked ball, so the legs swing wide; the user should judge it in play.
5. **Done.** Enemy AI (`npc.rs`): `Brain { mind: Idle / Chase / Attack{t} / Cooldown{left} / Return }`, pure `think_step(mind, Sense, attack_len, dt) -> Cue`. Aggro 20 yd, leash 30 yd (also on player death) -> walk home (2.5 yd/s), home -> Idle facing its post. Chase 4 yd/s via `motor::step` (the player's capsule shape; its own body collider skipped through `MoverTraceExclusions`, set and cleared around the step); body collider is now `RigidBody::Kinematic`, `follow_bodies` moves it. Swing: Attack1H (17) at 0.7x = 1.43 s; starts within 1.8 yd and facing cos >= 0.85; tracks the player until `STRIKE_FROM`; strike window 0.34-0.48 of the swing (0.49-0.69 s), placed by measuring the kobold's hand (attachment 1, `WOWSOULS_TRACE` `swing t=` lines: hand back/up to 0.28, forward from 0.34, reach 0.86 yd at 0.41); hit test `strike_reaches` (edge within 1.6 yd, cone cos 0.5); 25 damage once per swing; ignored while `Action::invulnerable()` (logged "passes through the roll"); 1.2 s cooldown. A player hit flinches it and breaks its swing. No thinking until its model is built. Player side (`hurt.rs`): `PlayerHurt` -> health, `Action::stagger(0.5)` + CombatWound (9), or `Action::die()` + Death (1), lock released, "YOU DIED" fades in, respawn after 4.5 s real at `SpawnPoint` (full HP/stamina), `Respawned` -> `npc::reset` (home, full health, Lockable/Hurtbox/body collider back). Hit-stop 0.05 s on the player via `HitStop::freeze`. New `ActionState::Stagger{t,duration}` / `Dead` (accept no press; a press late in a stagger buffers). Avatar has a `Hurtbox`. `anim::drive` also yields to `Brain::owns_body` and a non-None `Reaction`. Instrument `WOWSOULS_DODGE=d[,x,y]` (evade d s after each `Telegraph`). Verified live: still player 100->75->50->25->dies, kobold walks home, respawn + reset, loop repeats; forward roll into the kobold at d=0.30: "strike passes through the roll", no damage; side roll at d=0.30 escapes by distance; d=0.45/0.60 rolls get hit (i-frames open 0.05 s in; a hit staggers the roll); player kills it 60->40->20->0 with a swing broken by a hit; leash at 30.04 yd, home reached; screenshot `m2s5-13.5.png` (YOU DIED, kobold walking home). A suspected "roll rides over the kobold" was the Abbey steps edge at wow (-8909..-8907, -135.4) (walking there without the kobold rises the same 0.9 yd).
6. Polish: lock breaks on lost line of sight, camera shake on hit, bonfire.

### Quests: data import (found 2026-10-09; the source of truth for spawns, NPCs and quests)

`C:\Spel\MaNGOS` is a vMaNGOS repack: MySQL 5.5.62, MyISAM, world DB `mangos` (66 025 `creature` rows, 4 725
`quest_template`). **Never run the repack's own `mysqld` on its own data dir.** Query a copy:

```powershell
$s = "<scratchpad>\mangosdb"          # copy once: Copy-Item C:\Spel\MaNGOS\mysql5\data\mangos, ...\mysql -> $s\data\
Start-Process C:\Spel\MaNGOS\mysql5\bin\mysqld.exe -WindowStyle Hidden -RedirectStandardError "$s\mysqld.err" -RedirectStandardOutput "$s\mysqld.out" -ArgumentList "--no-defaults","--basedir=C:/Spel/MaNGOS/mysql5","--datadir=<$s with />/data","--port=3399","--bind-address=127.0.0.1","--skip-grant-tables","--skip-innodb","--default-storage-engine=MyISAM"
C:\Spel\MaNGOS\mysql5\bin\mysql.exe --no-defaults --host=127.0.0.1 --port=3399 --user=root --default-character-set=utf8 -e "<sql>"   # NOT -h127.0.0.1 (PowerShell mangles it)
```
(`performance_schema ... wrong structure` lines in `mysqld.err` are harmless; stop it with `Stop-Process -Name mysqld` when done.
The scratchpad copy of this session is gone with the session: copy again.)

What the tables give (all verified on the copy):
- `creature(guid, id, map, position_x/y/z, orientation, spawntimesecsmin/max, wander_distance, movement_type,
  patch_min, patch_max)` and `creature_template(entry, name, subname, level_min/max, display_id1, ...)`: **filter every
  query with `patch_min <= 10 AND patch_max >= 10`** (vMaNGOS carries every patch; 10 = 1.12). WoW coords as in the
  game (+X north, +Y west), map 0 = Eastern Kingdoms. `quest_template.patch` is the quest's own patch (7961
  "Waskily Wabbits!" is patch 9, 7962 "Wabbit Pelts" patch 4: check what 1.12 has before using them).
- Quest links: `creature_questrelation` (NPC starts quest), `creature_involvedrelation` (NPC ends it); gameobject
  twins `gameobject_questrelation` / `gameobject_involvedrelation`; loot `creature_loot_template`, `item_template`.
- **A coordinate box is NOT the valley.** Box x -9250..-8650, y -450..250 also catches Elwynn Forest (the 41 rabbits,
  deer, Defias Cutpurses y 15..241 and Mangy Wolves are Elwynn's). Decide "in Northshire Valley" with the client's own
  map data: `benilla_formats::area_id_at(&adt.chunks, wow)` (area 9 = Northshire Valley and its sub-areas, via
  `AreaTable.dbc` parent), the same call the engine's `area_id_under` uses.
- Valley quests (`ZoneOrSort = 9`): 783 A Threat Within, 5261 Eagan Peltskinner, 33 Wolves Across the Border, 18 Brotherhood
  of Thieves, 6 Bounty on Garrick Padfoot, 7 Kobold Camp Cleanup, 15 Investigate Echo Ridge, 21 Skirmish at Echo Ridge, 54
  Report to Goldshire, 3903 Milly Osworth, 3904 Milly's Harvest, 3905 Grape Manifest; plus 7961 / 7962 (the wabbit
  quests, patch 9 / 4, to check). Class letters from Marshal McBride (3100-3105, by class), trainer quests (5623 In Favor of the
  Light from Priestess Anetta, 1598 The Stolen Tome from Drusilla La Salle), Brother Paxton's Stormwind chain (344 / 345 /
  346 / 347, level 24), Falkhaan Isenstrider's 2158 Rest and Relaxation, and 5805 Welcome! (Merissa Stilwell). The valley's
  NPCs by entry: McBride 197, Deputy Willem 823, Eagan Peltskinner 196, Milly Osworth 9296, Brother Danil 152, Brother
  Neals 952, Brother Paxton 951, Godric Rothgar 1213, Janos Hammerknuckle 78, Dermot Johns 190, Merissa Stilwell 11940,
  Llane Beshere 911, Jorik Kerridan 915, Brother Sammuel 925, Priestess Anetta 375, Khelden Bremen 198, Drusilla La
  Salle 459, Dane Winslow 6373, Northshire Guard 1642, Northshire Peasant 11260. Hostile spawn counts inside the box
  before the area filter: Defias Thug 76 (38), Kobold Worker 43 (257), Young Wolf 39 (299), Timber Wolf 36 (69), Kobold Vermin
  33 (6), Kobold Laborer 3 (80); critters Rabbit 721, Deer 883, Fawn 890, Cat 6368 (the cat lady's, Donni Anthania 6367), Cow 2442.
  **Garrick Padfoot (103)** spawns at WoW (-9056.48, -460.90, 72.83) (guid 80247, respawn 270 s, no wander), beyond the
  box's y -450 edge: far south-west of the Abbey, nothing like `slice::GARRICK_AT` (-8856,-64), which was a guess.
  `quest_template` holds one row per (entry, patch): use the row with the highest `patch <= 10` for each entry.
- Open decisions for the user (ask before building): the player has no class, so which class letter (3100-3105) does

### Spawn rate and aggression (needs its own look once all creatures are in)

State today: fixed spots, not WoW's (the client has no spawn data): 4 kobolds, 2 wolves, 2 thugs for a person
playing (a scripted run has 1 kobold and no others); every creature aggro 20 yd and leash 30 yd, whatever its
level; **no respawn timer**: a killed creature stays dead until the player dies or presses F6 (the creatures
reset then); no social aggro (one pulled creature does not call its neighbours); everything chases. To decide
with the user: counts and density per area (the classic valley has many more kobolds in the Echo Ridge mine than
this), aggro radius by level difference (WoW scales it: lower-level creatures notice you from nearer), whether
the wolves and the thugs are aggressive on sight, a respawn timer per species (WoW: a few minutes), pack
behaviour, a calm stretch around the Abbey, and how it feels at Dark Souls difficulty when all of them come.

### 1. SOUND WAS BROKEN: ROOT CAUSE FOUND AND FIXED THIS SESSION (verify by ear, then close)

The user: "ljudet tappas hela tiden, särskilt heavy attack. Och vanlig attack låter knappt alls."

**Cause (measured, not guessed):** `audio::listener()` took `cams.iter().next()` of ALL `Camera3d` entities; one of them sits at the world's origin, so the listener was ~8900 yd from the player and every POSITIONAL sound (the sword swish, the blow's impact, creature voices and barks, footsteps) fell off to nothing (`sfx::gains` returns `None` past the kit's cutoff of 45 yd). Non-positional sounds (the player's grunt, music, ambience) kept playing, which is why only "a little" was heard. Which camera came first changed with the entity layout, hence "sometimes" / "always". Found with a null-audio run (`WOWSOULS_AUDIO=null`): the log had `player grunt` lines but no `player swish`; a temporary trace then printed `dist 8910` for every positional kit.

**Fix:** `listener()` and every `cams` query in `audio.rs`, `foot.rs`, `vocal.rs` now use `With<benilla_world::view::WorldCamera>` (the one camera the scene renders through; `freecam.rs` spawns it). A test spawns an origin camera first and checks the listener is the world camera (`the_listener_is_the_world_camera_not_any_other_camera`). A kit that is silent now logs `audio: <what>: kit N is inaudible (listener D yd away ...)` under `WOWSOULS_TRACE`. Verified: the same null run now logs `player swish` (kit 235 for a light blow, 237 for a heavy one), `kobold swish`, `aggro bark`, `attack grunt`, `footstep`, all at 3-6 yd, gain ~0.30.

**Earlier hardening of the same session (kept):** the mixer steals the voice nearest its end at its cap (48), refuses / zeroes NaN, survives a panic, rebuilds a failed device stream every second, and writes anomalies to `%LOCALAPPDATA%\WoWSouls\audio.log` (none was written during the user's play, which fits: the cause was never the mixer). Gates: 589 game tests green.

- **1. The tilt (root cause measured, not guessed).** At the motor's 7 yd/s the gait blend is the DS **sprint** (`a00_0535`, `a10_0510` ...: 7 is its pace). That clip carries the trunk 13 degrees left at the chest, the head 18, hunched 55 forward, the whole loop (the run `a00_0500` and the walk are square: mean lean -0.4 / +0.6). Found by logging the model-space up vector of the head -> root chain in game, then the same numbers offline from `cache/dsr/human_male/a00_0535.wscl`. **Fix:** `dsloco::straighten_of(clip)` (roll about the forward axis that puts the chest's loop-average up vector on the median plane; -22.6 degrees for the sprint because of the hunch) is stored per clip (`Clip::straighten`), blended by the mix's shares into `DsGait::straighten` and applied by `lively.rs` at the gait's weight as part of the lean roll, **whatever `WOWSOULS_LIVELY` is** (a fix, not a flourish). The forward hunch is untouched (it is the sprint's own). Before / after sheet: same frames from behind, head over the hips and the shoulder line level (`scratchpad` only; rerun `WOWSOULS_DRIVE=0,1 WOWSOULS_AT=-8906,-136 WOWSOULS_SHOT_AT=6,6.3,6.6`). Log: `WOWSOULS_TRACE` prints `(straighten -22.6)` in the `lively:` line. Not changed: the pace (7 yd/s is still DS's sprint), so a player who wanted the calmer DS run still has to ask.
- **2. Hogger's back, second pass (the user, after the first: "Saker stretchas som galet och inget ser bra ut. Ser ut som en mardröm"; I had judged from stills and had NOT looked at him moving: a dense frame series of a fight, every 0.2-0.25 s, from the game's camera found it).** Two separate causes, both measured:
  - **(a) The stretch: the hips left the torso on every jump.** The retarget places the WoW hip root at the DS pelvis' absolute height, but a gnoll's spine, arms and head hang from its ground-fixed root bone (10), so a clip where the pelvis travels vertically pulls the legs away from the torso: in the jump slam `a00_3013` the hip root (bone 14) is at y 4.1 while the waist and spine stay at 0.92 / 0.96: a belly three yards long, seen from the front as a thin black column (this was in the first build of Hogger too: the 1.5 scale hid nothing). Fix in the extractor: `bonemap::Hips.body_lift: Option<BodyLift { root, full_to, beyond }>` (gnoll: root 10, 0.6, 0.2): the body hangs on the root and rises / drops by what the DS pelvis moved from its height in the clip's FIRST frame, the hip root gives that much back (legs reach the same places), and a leap is shortened beyond 0.6 model yd to 0.2 of the rest (the demon's pelvis goes ten metres up; the cap was 0.35 first, the contact-frame gate `max_foot_gap` refused it at 0.215 yd, so the lift is kept in full where the soles are near the ground). Only the Hogger kit opts in: re-extracting changes exactly `hogger/*.wscl` (two clips of the 13 differ from the lift without the cap), every other kit, the manifest and the player's clips are byte-identical; `max-foot-gap-all` (information) grew to 1.67 yd from the airborne frames. Test `retarget::a_lift_root_takes_the_body_up_with_the_hips`. Files `bonemap.rs` and `retarget.rs` are CRLF.
  - **(b) The fold at the waist: the demon's chest rolls 70 degrees.** The demon's `Neck` bone (what the gnoll's chest is driven from) carries its shoulder roll, -80 .. +43 degrees sideways on the kit clips, on top of hips that roll 20: one hinge, a pinched torso. First attempt (damping the chest toward the bind pose by shares) left the same column in the overhead wind-up (`TORSO=0` and `1` looked alike in the fight series); the replacement is a chain constraint (`monster::GNOLL_TORSO`: `Link { bone, after, from_bind, from_after, hunch }`, `capped` = a soft tanh cap on lean and twist, `reshape` at runtime in `monster::pose`): the spine base (11) is held within 11 degrees of the waist (12), the chest (13) within 29 of the spine base and 31 of the bind pose, the head (30) within 46 of the chest, a hunch of 0.2 / 0.3 rad forward on top; the waist is anchored (orientation and place), the arm roots (16, 17) keep the clip's own orientation (the axe's arc is the clip's). Chest roll in the overhead wind-up: -70 -> -11 degrees. Switches `WOWSOULS_TORSO=0..1` (0 = the clips as they are: the cap's limits open to a half turn), `WOWSOULS_HUNCH=0..2`.
  - **How it was found (use this):** `WOWSOULS_START=1 WOWSOULS_SLOT=<n> WOWSOULS_WORLD=1 WOWSOULS_DRIVE=0,0.5 WOWSOULS_SHOT_AT=6,6.25,6.5,...` with the three `*_SAVE` envs for a fight series from the player's own camera, and `WOWSOULS_DSVIEW=hogger:<move>@<s>` with `WOWSOULS_HOGGERS=1 WOWSOULS_KOBOLDS=0 WOWSOULS_AT=-8913,-116 WOWSOULS_HOGGER_AT=-8905,-116` for a front view of one pose (the player faces north, Hogger stands 8 yd north of him facing the camera). A temporary system that logs the model-space pitch / roll / height of bones 14 12 11 13 15 30 16 17 18 each half second (`HOGPROBE`, removed) is what showed the 4.1 against 0.92; side views alone had hidden it.

Manifest `attack` / `defattack` lines for both.
- **4. Pattern layer (`pattern.rs`, `npc.rs`, `monster.rs`).** A boss's rhythm is data: `Style { name, then: [(move, percent)], link, cut, feint, opening }` per move (`HOGGER_STYLE`, `HOGGER_TWO_STYLE`, `styles(species, second_phase)`; a species without styles runs on the table's `then` as before). (a) **More than one follow-up**: each move plans its follow-up when it starts (`plan_next`: one of several, by percent; e.g. `overhead` -> backhand 30 / quick 20 / step_smash 10), is held to the player being in that move's band (+0.4 yd) when it ends. (b) **Links**: a follow-up starts `link` clip-seconds into its clip (a combo's blows come closer) and a swing that has a follow-up planned recovers for `CHAIN_RECOVERY` 0.5 s after its last window instead of 1.1. (c) **Feints**: `heavy_swing` 40 %, `overhead` 30 %, `sweep` 25 % (phase two 45 / 40 / 35): the swing's clock stops at the raised axe for 0.3-0.85 s (`Feint { at, hold, chance }`, `brain.hold`), a roll made at the tell is over when the axe comes. The mind owns an attack's clip clock now (`MonsterPlay.clip` time is set from `Mind::Attack.t` each frame; `monster::advance` skips an attacking creature). (d) **Opening**: the fight starts with the charge (`Style.opening`), once per fight (`brain.last` is cleared when he lets go). (e) **Rows**: `charge` band 2.6-7.0 yd weight 2 (3 enraged), `hop_back` band 0-1.8 weight 1 (2): hop back -> charge 75 % -> overhead / step_smash / lunge_smash / quick = the new signature combo. Wind-up log lines now say `, then <move>` and `, held 0.5 s at 1.05`. Live (a level 1, 60 HP default save is not a fair test of the numbers): openings `charge, then overhead|step_smash|lunge_smash`, `step_smash, then sweep|quick`, the charge's body check landing at t=1.55 s, then the step_smash behind it.

**DEFIAS FOOTWORK CHECKED, A SIDESTEP FIX AND A RING BUG (2026-10-13; the user: "Har nu även defias sin egna ai också?", then "sidestep animationen på defias ser lite skevt ut"; nothing committed).** (1) **Yes, the Defias have it**: the Thug (`Temper::THUG`, the undead thief's `slash_1` / `slash_2`) and Garrick (`Temper::GARRICK`, five moves) run the same `footwork` + `pattern` machinery as every species with their own temperament and table; checked live this time (`WOWSOULS_THUGS=1 WOWSOULS_THUG_AT=...`, `WOWSOULS_GARRICKS=1 WOWSOULS_GARRICK_AT=...`: the older E2 group switches are `_THUGS` / `_GARRICKS`, not `_THIEVES`). (2) **A bug found while checking**: a creature with a move whose band reaches out to its ring (Garrick: ring 4.8 yd = the dash's far edge 4.8) started that move the instant it reached the ring, so it never sized the player up; and `choose` could start any move whose band it passed on the way in. Fix (`npc.rs` `think`): an uncommitted creature starts no move while the player is farther than the wished move's band `.1` + `footwork::REACH_SLACK` (0.4 yd); inside that reach it swings at once as before. New test `every_temperament_stops_at_its_ring_on_the_approach`; new trace line `npc: <name> commits at dist=.. (ring .., player open .., was ..)`. Live: Garrick `Close -> Circle` at 4.79 yd, `StrafeLeft`, `Hold`, commits, dash. (3) **Sidestep "crooked"**: measured with a temporary pose probe (removed): during the human's ShuffleLeft / ShuffleRight clip the pelvis, chest and head yaw / roll are 0 and the torso upright, only the thighs swing ±16-20 degrees; so the clip itself is square. What *was* crooked: the circling plan added a radial drift of up to 0.6 of the pace (and 0.6 backwards inside the ring), i.e. a pure sideways clip played on a body that travels 30-45 degrees diagonal. Now (`footwork.rs` `step`, Circle / Hold arm) the drift is at most `SIDE_DRIFT` 0.25 (14 degrees off straight sideways) and a creature inside the reach of its move does not go round at all (it stands; the swing comes). Test `going_round_is_always_nearly_straight_sideways_and_never_inside_reach`. **If it still looks crooked to the user, ask what exactly** (feet crossing / sliding, the torso, the direction): the remaining candidates are WoW's ShuffleL / R legs themselves (thighs turn in and out) and the clips' feet sliding against the ground (the rate is `speed / 2.5`, the clips carry no ground speed). Gates: fmt, clippy workspace, wow-souls 774 tests.

**LOCK-ON TARGET SWITCHING BUILT (2026-10-13; the user: "Kan du också implementera hur man kan cykla mellan lockade targets. Så man inte behöver unlock sen lock igen utan man kan flytta den"; nothing committed).** Locked on, a **flick of the look input** moves the lock to the nearest target on that side of the current one, with no unlock in between (as in Dark Souls). `src/lockon.rs`: `Side`, `next_target` (pure: of the candidates within `LOCK_RANGE` 20 yd the one with the smallest angle round the player from the current target on the flicked side, 3 degrees to `SWITCH_CONE` 100 degrees, distance breaking a near-tie; `None` = nothing on that side, the lock stays), `Flick` (pure detector: the stick / a look key fires on crossing 0.6 and fires again only after falling under 0.25; the mouse is a leaky sum so a quick flick of about 50 px at the default sensitivity fires and a slow drift does not; at least 0.25 s between two; a stick already deflected when the lock is made waits for rest), system `switch` (chained `toggle, switch, keep` in `PlaySet::Intent`). The inputs are the existing look ones, so **no new binding**: right stick, the look keys (arrows by default, `Action::LookLeft/Right`), the mouse while the cursor is captured; the camera already ignored look input while locked, so nothing else changed (`camera::MOUSE_SENSITIVITY` is now `pub(crate)`). The camera, rig and the Dark Souls ring follow `LockOn.target` as before. Instrument `WOWSOULS_LOCK_SWITCH=<s>:<left|right>,...`; log lines `lock-on: switched A -> B (Right)` and `lock-on: nothing to the Left of A`. Verified live (three kobolds, `WOWSOULS_LOCK=4.5 WOWSOULS_LOCK_SWITCH=6:right,...,9:left`): the right flicks found nothing right of the rightmost kobold, the left one moved the lock; 7 new tests (772 in all), fmt / clippy workspace clean. **Open:** a lock whose target dies still just breaks (it does not move on to the nearest foe: say so if the user wants Elden Ring's auto-advance, one rule in `keep`); no dedicated key to switch (flick only; two `Action`s would be the Controls page's two more rows); the pad's flick is untested on a real pad.

**ENEMY FOOTWORK AND A MORE LIVELY PLAYER BUILT (2026-10-13; feedback the user passed on: "Dynamic enemy ai that keep distance, move in, and move away makes combat like this more immersive" and "the player looks stiffer and less lively with Dark Souls animations"; the design was shown in chat and approved with "Kör"; nothing committed).** Two sub-projects, the player's first (I1 + I2), then the enemies' (H). Gates: scoped fmt, clippy `-D warnings` workspace, tests wow-souls 765 (+25), dsr-cache 56, dsr-formats 52, wow-world 159 + 4, wscl 4.
- **Why it looked stiff (read in the code, measured):** (1) the motor takes the stick at face value (`motor::step`: `input_horiz = push * speed`, 0 to 7 yd/s in one frame and back), so every clip started and stopped like a switch; the old stop sheet `loco-sheet-stop.png` shows a full run lean one frame and upright the next; (2) the body turns at 12 rad/s; (3) the DS gait owns spine, arms and hands with no secondary motion on top (the clips themselves are NOT static: measured torso swings 6 to 22 degrees a cycle, probe test removed, so nothing was "amplified"). The enemy side: `Mind` was Idle / Chase / Attack / Cooldown / Return, a Chase was a straight run to `stop_distance`, a Cooldown stood still 0.3 s, `anim::drive` gave only the avatar a strafe gait, and the kobold model has no ShuffleL/R (11/12) or WalkBackwards (13) clips (wolf and human have all three: `benilla-extract m2seq`).
- **I1 inertia (`src/glide.rs` pure, `controller.rs` `glide_intent`):** `read_intent` feeds the motor a velocity that follows the stick at `ACCEL` 40 yd/s² (a run in 0.175 s) and `BRAKE` 60 (a stop in 0.12 s); vector based, so a turn is a curve and a reversal 0.35 s. The intent is encoded as direction (the stick's length) x speed so a roll's "stick pushed > 0.2" and the guard's `speed.min(WALK_SPEED)` read it as before. Anything that carries the body itself (a swing's root motion, a roll, a shove, a charge) overwrites the intent later in the frame as ever and is NOT softened; the next frame the build-up is re-seeded from the achieved velocity (`Walking.written` is how it knows). `WOWSOULS_GLIDE=0` gives the raw stick back. Verified: `trace` shows asked 7.00 within 0.17 s of the push and 3.99 at 0.04 s after the release; `life-start-sheet.png` / `life-stop-sheet.png` (top row = before, bottom = after).
- **I2 life layer (`src/lively.rs`, registered in `boot.rs` after `dsloco`, runs in `PosePost` after `dsclip::ClipLayers`):** additive rotations on the spine (20, 35 %) and chest (22, 65 %) for **lean** (acceleration along the facing pitches forward 6 degrees at a start, back up to 8 on braking; sideways acceleration banks up to 8 degrees toward the inside of a turn; weighted by `DsGait.weight`, so it is gone in any action) and on the neck (48, 40 %) and head (64, 60 %) for **look** (locked: at the target incl. its pitch; moving above 1 yd/s: along the travel; standing: where the camera looks; at most 70 degrees, none past 110 behind the shoulder; eased 6.5/s). Applied in the body's frame through the parent's model rotation (`in_parent`), undone before the next write where the evaluator did not rewrite the bone (`written`), so nothing piles up. Only while `Idle` or a guard and grounded; legs untouched. `WOWSOULS_LIVELY=0` off, a number (max 3) scales; `WOWSOULS_TRACE` prints `lively: lean pitch .. roll .. deg, head yaw .. tip ..` four times a second. Rig axes used (tested): forward -Z, up +Y, left -X; +X rotation tips the top back / the face up, +Z leans the top left, +Y turns left.
- **H footwork (`src/footwork.rs` pure with a `Foot` per creature in `Brain.foot`; hooks in `npc.rs` `think`, `actor/anim.rs`, `species.rs` `temper`, `pattern::reach`):** between swings an engaged creature (Mind `Chase`) closes to its **ring** = the wished move's band `.1` + `Temper.ring`, sizes the player up for `hesitate` seconds by circling (`circle` share) or standing, then **commits** and closes to the band; after its own swing or a blow it may **step back** 1-4 yd first (`back_after_swing` / `back_after_hit`; no swing is started while stepping back). A player who is swinging / reeling / staggered is **punished at once** (commit) unless the creature is stepping back. At most `MAX_COMMITTED` 2 creatures within 10 yd of the player are closing or swinging at once, the rest keep circling. The attack table, damage and swing timing are unchanged and a swing still starts the moment the player is inside its band and square to its nose. Temperaments (`Temper::KOBOLD` ... `GARRICK`): wolf = circler (ring 1.4, hesitate 1.0-2.4 s, circle 0.8), kobold wary (back 0.45 after a swing), vermin skittish, worker plods, thug steady, Garrick a duellist (ring 1.8, back 2-4 yd). **Animation:** an engaged creature gets the strafe-by-direction gait (`strafe` input in `anim::drive`), so wolf and humans play ShuffleL/R and WalkBackwards while facing the player; the kobold has none: it circles by walking along the tangent (`world_motion(.., strafes=false)` faces the way it goes) and steps back with its WALK clip played in reverse (`anim::gait_clip`, negative `set_speed`). Switches: `WOWSOULS_FOOTWORK=0` straight chase, `WOWSOULS_FOOTWORK_BACK=<0..1>` / `_CIRCLE=<0..1>` force the chances (for watching a step); `WOWSOULS_TRACE` logs `npc: <name> footwork A -> B dist= ring= [committed]`.
- **Verified live (unattended, open meadow, `WOWSOULS_AT=-8906,-136`, creature north at `-8891,-136`):** kobold closes to 3.5 yd, circles / holds, commits, `pounce` / `run_strike` / `big`, steps back 2-3 yd after a swing (`Backpedal clip=None` = the reversed walk), sizes up again; wolf circles at 2.7 yd with ShuffleL/R, lunges, steps back with clip 13; four kobolds: two attack at a time; pictures `docs/evidence/fw-wolf-sheet.png`, `fw-kob-back-sheet.png` (the kobold walking away facing the player, from `WOWSOULS_OBSERVE=5,1.6,-3.5`), `life-*-sheet.png`.
- **Open / for the user to judge:** the numbers are first guesses (ring, hesitate, back chances, `ACCEL` / `BRAKE`, lean and look amounts); the reversed walk is a stand-in until the ghoul's own walk-back clips are extracted; a wolf shows a brief `Fall` pose now and then while circling (the same happens in the straight chase, `grounded` flickers over uneven ground; not fixed); the head looks at the camera while standing (turn it off with `WOWSOULS_LIVELY=0` if it is odd); NPC humans' idle and walk are still WoW's. Not done from the plan: turn-in-place (`a00_0602-0605`) and the sprint start / stop clips (`0510` / `0511`) for the player (extractor work); circling / backing for the player's own pets / ranged enemies (none exist).

**CHARACTER SLOTS BUILT (2026-10-13; the user: "Har vi möjlighet för flera character spots? För det måste vi annars", then "1 och gå sedan direkt på 2" = commit first, then slots; the commit `88a379b` is the baseline, the slots are NOT committed).**
- **What there was:** one character, six loose files at fixed paths in `%LOCALAPPDATA%\WoWSouls\`, a new character archived the old one to `backup-<stamp>` and relaunched the game. **What there is:** `characters\<n>\` per character with the same six files (`character`, `quests`, `items`, `souls`, `clock`, `world-state`), up to 10; `settings-v1.txt`, the key bindings and `audio.log` stay at the root (they are the player's). `active-v1.txt` (`wsactive 1` + the number) remembers the slot played last.
- **`src/saves.rs` (new, pure + 9 tests):** `slots`, `next_id` (one past the highest folder, `None` at 10), `migrate_legacy` (the loose files move into slot 1 once; the character file goes last so a cut-short move is finished at the next start; a slot 1 that already has a character is never touched), `resolve_active` (`WOWSOULS_SLOT`, else the pointer if that slot is there, else the first character, else 1), `archive_slot`; process level `active()` (decided once, migrates first) and `file(name)`. The six path resolvers (`character.rs`, `loot/mod.rs`, `quests/mod.rs`, `bloodstain.rs`, `daycycle.rs`, `world_state.rs`) default to `saves::file(...)`; their env overrides and unattended isolation are unchanged.
- **The title (`menu.rs`):** one row per saved character ("Jacob, Human Warrior", the loaded one noted "In the valley now"), then New character (not on a full list), Options, Quit; the cursor opens on the loaded character. Enter on the loaded one plays on; on another it is `Outcome::Switch(id)`: the pointer is set and the game **relaunches** (`WOWSOULS_SLOT` for the child; every save is read at Startup, so a relaunch was the small way, the world streams again). New character beside others is `Outcome::Add`: a slot of its own, the character file written there, relaunch; the very first character is made in this run as before. Delete (key, pad X) on a character asks twice and moves its folder to `backup-<stamp>-character-<n>` (nothing is erased; deleting the loaded one relaunches). `Outcome::Replace`, `REPLACED_SAVES` and `archive_saves` are gone (a new character no longer replaces anything). Unattended runs never relaunch (a child would replay the same script): they log and exit.
- **Verified live on a scratch `LOCALAPPDATA`** (a copy of the user's real saves: the real ones were not touched): the loose saves moved into `characters\1`, the title listed the character (`docs/evidence/slots-title.png`), a script added Garrick as slot 2 (`slots-two.png`: both rows, Garrick loaded after the pointer moved), a switch moved the pointer, a double Delete archived slot 2 to `backup-...-character-2`. Gates: fmt, clippy `-D warnings` workspace, wow-souls 740 tests (saves 9, menu 53 incl. list, switch, add, full list, delete twice, remove).
- **Open / for the user:** the user's real save migrates on the next start of the game (`%LOCALAPPDATA%\WoWSouls\characters\1`; the loose files go away, `settings-v1.txt` stays: a copy of the old folder is not made, the move is a rename). No level or play time in the list (WoW shows level: the quest save has the xp, a later nicety). A character switch costs a relaunch (streaming again). The title scene shows the loaded character only, not the highlighted one. Only a male Human Warrior can be created, as before: the slots are what a second class needs. Old `backup-<stamp>` folders from earlier replaces stay at the root.

**AUTO-EQUIP: ONE RULE FOR EVERYTHING THE CHARACTER IS HANDED (2026-10-13; the user: "Jag såg att vissa saker autoequippas ifall man inte har något medans andra inte gör det. Se till så detta är konsekvent"; nothing committed).** Finding first: the game had **no** auto-equip on pickup at all (read: `Progress::gear` changes only through `equip` / `unequip` / `grant_start_kit`; the loot, quest, shop and world-state paths only add to the pack), so what the user saw was not in the code; asked, they chose "build the rule" (over "never auto-equip").
- **The rule** (`wow-world`, pure): `Gear::free_slot` (the first legal slot, in a quick equip's order, that is EMPTY and takes the piece without taking anything else off) and `Progress::put_on_new(items, got, wearer, keep)`: each unit of `got` (already in the pack) that fits an empty slot goes on, in order; nothing worn is ever moved or replaced (a two-hander is not put on beside a worn shield, a shield not beside a two-hander, a 3rd ring waits); `keep(entry)` leaves what an accepted quest counts in the pack (`shop::needed_by`); what the wearer cannot use (level, proficiency) and non-gear stay carried; only what was just handed over is considered (older pack items are not retroactively worn). 7 tests in `inventory.rs`.
- **Wiring** (`equip/mod.rs`): `put_on_handed` (the game-level helper: wearer + quest guard + "Equipped: X" lines) and the system `auto_equip` (after `act`, before `apply`), which reads `loot::Acquired` = a corpse, a chest (authored or baseline), a quest reward, a quest's source item; and `shop::act`, which applies the same helper to a `Buy` before the pack is published and appends "Equipped: X." to the shop message. A toast ("Equipped: Ensign Cloak") shows the pickup case. Dark Souls' old items are never touched. `GamePrefs.auto_equip` (default on), `game.auto_equip` in `settings-v1.txt`, a row in Options -> Game ("Put new gear on in an empty slot"). Not covered (no `Acquired`): `WOWSOULS_GIVE` / the quest script's `give:`, held-loot at start (`world_state`), a swap-out by the equip button (that is the player's own act and must not re-equip).
- **Tests:** wow-souls 727 (equip: rule helper, the system on a bare world incl. switched off; settings round trip and an older file keeping the default; menu row), wow-world 201 + 4; fmt, clippy `-D warnings` workspace clean. **Not run live:** the baseline Battered Chest (objspawn 2843, loot table 2265 with 7 gear rows) is not interactive in this build (only quest crates and the three authored chests are), and a corpse's gear drop is 2 % or less, so there was no cheap scripted pickup of gear; the system was exercised on a bare ECS world instead.
- **For the user's hands / open:** the user's save has Worn Wooden Shield and Militia Warhammer in the pack: they stay (the main hand holds a two-hander); take the Bastard Sword off and a later pickup of a one-hander goes straight on. If auto-equip in the main hand is unwanted (it replaces the fist moveset), the switch is in the Game menu. Not decided: ammo (arrows go to the Ammo slot, one unit: the slot has no stack count yet), and whether a *better* piece should replace a worn one (it does not: only empty slots).

**THE CAMERA IS NOW DARK SOULS REMASTERED'S (2026-10-13; the user: "Läs handover - innan vi går på nästa klass - borde vi fixa till så kameran är densamma som DSR?", then "Kör"; nothing committed).** Done before the class framework, because every class and move is judged through the camera.
- **Source (read-only, measured):** `param/GameParam/GameParam.parambnd.dcx` has a `LockCamParam` (38 rows, `LOCK_CAM_PARAM_ST`: `camDistTarget`, `rotRangeMinX`, `lockRotXShiftRatio`, `chrOrgOffset_Y`, `chrLockRangeMaxRadius`, `camFovY`). Row 0 (the default): 4.0 m, floor -40 deg, ratio 0.6, pivot 1.42 m, lock radius 15 m, **FOV 43 deg** (every row 41.5-48). Rows 101 / 102 / 103 / 104 run 2.2 / 3.2 / 5.0 / 6.0 m (small to huge; the 103 / 104 floor is -20 deg, ratio 0.4). The free camera's own numbers live in the exe, not in data: row 0 is the nearest thing DSR ships. A dump script exists only as a scratch perl (`/tmp/ws/lockcam.pl`: DCX -> BND3 -> PARAMDEF -> rows); `NpcParam` has no lock-cam id (only `lockDist`), so which creature uses which row is the map's business: our ladder is by target height.
- **Game** (`camera.rs`, `lockon.rs`, one line in `npc.rs`): `DS_METRE = 1.135` yd (the retarget's scale, so the body is framed as DS frames its 1.8 m human: 57 % of the screen height at the default), `FOV_Y` 43 deg (set on the world camera by `seat` while playing; the title / creation screen and the F1 free camera keep the engine's 60 deg spawn FOV until the orbit rig sets it), `Rig { distance, pivot_height, pitch_min, shift_ratio }`, `FREE_RIG` = row 0 = **4.54 yd back, 1.61 yd up** (was 5.5 / 1.75), `LADDER` rows 101 / 102 / 0 / 103 / 104 by target height (0.6 / 1.2 / 1.8 / 3.5 / 6.0 DS m, interpolated, held at the ends), `lock_rig(height)`, `ease_rig` (3/s, about 1 s). `OrbitCam.distance` became `OrbitCam.rig`. `Lockable` gained `height` (npc.rs sets it; `locked_height`). **Lock-on pitch** is now `ratio x angle to the target`, floored at the rig's `rotRangeMinX` (a level target = a level camera), where it was the angle - 17 deg (`LOCK_PITCH_BIAS` is gone).
- **Measured live** (unattended, scratch saves, open meadow `WOWSOULS_AT=-8906,-136`): free camera `boom 4.52/4.54`, pitch -0.25; one kobold locked (`WOWSOULS_KOBOLDS=1 WOWSOULS_KOBOLD_AT=-8894,-136 WOWSOULS_LOCK=4.3`): `lock-on: locked`, boom eases **4.54 -> 3.33 yd** in about a second (the kobold is 1.18 yd tall = 1.04 m, between rows 101 and 102), pitch -0.07 .. -0.22 as it closes. Pictures `docs/evidence/dscam-free.png`, `dscam-locked.png`. Gates: scoped fmt `--check` clean, clippy `-D warnings` workspace clean, tests wow-souls 715 (7 new: rig, ladder, easing, FOV, lock pitch), dsr-cache 58, dsr-formats 52, wow-world 195 + 4, wscl 4.
- **CORRECTION THE SAME DAY (the user: "Är kameran verkligen rätt? När man har låset in så ser man inte fienderna så nästan omöjligt att se deras attack"): the first lock-on port hid the enemy behind the player's body.** Cause: `pitch = 0.6 x the target's angle` (no look-down bias, the old `LOCK_PITCH_BIAS` had been -17 deg) made a near-level camera that looks along the line player -> enemy; with the 43 deg FOV and a 3.3 yd boom (small targets' closer row) the kobold sat behind the legs (`docs/evidence/dscam-locked.png`). Fixed three ways: (1) **sight rule** (`lockon.rs` `sightline_clear` / `clear_pitch` / `LOCK_PITCH_FLOOR` -55 deg = DS row 1103's): the locked pitch goes down one degree at a time from the natural one until the segment camera -> aim point clears the player's capsule (+0.15 yd): about -15 deg for a far target, -50 deg at melee range; a target that cannot be cleared stays at the floor. (2) **`lock_rig` never goes closer or lower than the free rig** (the small rows 101 / 102 and row 104's low pivot were my own reading of the file, unverified, and they made it worse; big targets still push the camera out to 5-6 m). (3) **the view aims part-way to the target** (`view_rotation`, `ease_focus`, `OrbitCam.look` / `.focus`): locked, the camera looks `shift_ratio` (0.6) of the way from the pivot to the aim point, so the player drops low in the frame and the enemy comes to the middle, as DS does; free, the rotation is the orbit's own. Pictures `docs/evidence/dscam-locked3-5.png` (a kobold 8 yd away: clearly seen above the player's head), `dscam-locked3-8.2.png` (melee: a steep, top-down view, the kobold at the middle, its lower body partly behind the player's head). Gates: fmt clean, clippy clean, tests wow-souls 723 (camera 14, lockon 12), the rest as above. **Open for the user's hands:** a melee view this steep is the price of a small enemy 1.8 yd ahead of a 2 yd body; the alternative is a shoulder offset (the camera slides to the right while locked, so the enemy is seen beside the body instead of over it), not built; `LOCK_PITCH_FLOOR` and `SIGHT_MARGIN` are the dials.
- **For the user's hands / tuning:** the numbers are constants at the top of `camera.rs` (`FREE_RIG` row, `LADDER`, `RIG_RATE`, `FOV_Y`). **Open:** `chrLockRangeMaxRadius` is 15 m (16.4 yd) in DS against our `LOCK_RANGE` 20 yd / `LOCK_BREAK` 30 (not changed: a gameplay number, not framing); the free camera's pitch limits (-70 / +30 deg) are ours, DS's are not in the data; the camera collision sphere is unchanged.

**DECISIONS 2026-10-12, a later chat ("Läs handover - vi bör göra klasser?"; nothing built yet, nothing committed).**
- **Classes:** the user wants all of them eventually; **path 2 now = a class framework (a class becomes a data row: resource, abilities, proficiencies, start kit, class letter, trainer) with the Warrior as row 1, plus one more playable class (Claude's pick: Rogue: energy + combo points, melee, daggers `a20`, DS backstab / riposte fit; no cast pipeline)**. The roster of abilities per class is the user's to name (rule from the abilities work). Mage / Priest / Warlock need a cast pipeline (cast bar, projectiles, mana, DS sorcery clips) and come after. Not started: first the gear step below.
- **ATTRIBUTES, ITEM STATS, QUEST ITEMS BEFORE THE QUEST AND BOSS / RARE RULES BUILT (2026-10-12; the user: stats next + "i samma steg" boss / rare drops and no respawn, "B" pure with enemy damage unchanged, "Native"; nothing committed; spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-12-attributes-and-boss-rules-design.md`, plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-12-attributes-and-boss-rules.md`, 10 tasks done).**
  - **Importer:** `item_template` stat columns + `player_levelstats` (race 1, all classes) + `player_classlevelstats` exported (`tools/export-mangos.ps1`), manifest **`wsworld 3`** (`item` gains a trailing stats list; `levelstat` / `classstat` records; the reader still takes 1 and 2), `cache/world/northshire.txt` re-imported (the old one is `northshire.pre-stats.bak`): 360 level rows, 540 class rows; 17 valley items carry fixed stats (the Wandering Boots are +4 Stamina +4 **Intellect**), 30 random-suffix items are not handled.
  - **Rules** (`wow-world/src/attributes.rs`, pure, 10 tests): base attributes per class and level + worn gear's stats; max health = class base HP + `min(sta,20) + 10 x (sta-20)` + `HEALTH_OFFSET` (0): level 1 = **60**, 3 = 98, 5 = 136, 8 = 193; melee attack power `level x 3 + Str x 2 - 20`, ranged `level + Agi - 10`, `ap_factor` normalised so a level 1 warrior with the starting sword is x1.0 (level 5 about x1.5, 8 about x1.85); armor `+ 2 x Agility` (level 1 = 40 + 7); Intellect and Spirit inert. `GearEffect::with_character` feeds the existing damage hooks, `level.rs` takes max health from the attributes (the current value moves with the maximum on a level-up or a gear change; the stamina bar stays 100 + 5 per level; without class tables the old 100 + 10 per level stays and logs why). Enemy damage is **not** scaled.
  - **Display:** tooltips list `+4 Stamina` rows after armor, comparison rows `+4 Stamina (+3)` (Intellect / Spirit shown, not weighed), the equipment page shows `Strength 28 (+3)   Agility 23   Stamina 26 (+4)` / `Intellect 20   Spirit 22   Health 176` and the armor / block / hits line.
  - **Quest items before the quest:** `QuestLog::droppable_items` (+ `rules::takeable_later`, `pre_need`): a corpse may drop a quest item while its quest is not handed in and the player could still take it (class / race ok; level and prerequisites ignored); accept already counts what is carried; the shop refuses to buy / sell what such a quest wants. A fresh start logs `quests: corpses may drop the quest items {182, 745, 750, 752, ...}`.
  - **Ranks:** `SpeciesDef.rank: Rank { Normal, Rare, Miniboss, Boss }` (Garrick = Boss; `WOWSOULS_RANK=<species>:<rank>` overrides for a live check). Boss / miniboss **never respawn**: `ring::NEVER` (a reset and a ring tick leave it; `npc::reset` does not raise a dead one), saved in the new `world-state-v1.txt` (`wsstate 1`: `slain <guid>`, `first <entry>`, `held <guid> <item> <count>...`; an unattended run without `WOWSOULS_WORLD_STATE` keeps it in memory), a dead one's corpse is never despawned by the ring, and its unlooted `held` items go to the pack at the next start. Boss / rare **first kill** adds `loot::best_drop` (gear before anything else, then quality, item level, lower chance; quest rows ignored, references expanded) once per creature entry.
  - **Measured live (unattended, scratch saves):** level 1 `health 60`, armor 47, the kobold's blows 24-33 (was 25-35); Wandering Boots: `health 100` (+40) and armor 73; a vermin made a boss (`WOWSOULS_RANK=vermin:boss`, spot guid 79995) died, `loot: first kill of entry 6: the best of its table is item 1421`, `ring: ... died and stays dead`, `state.txt` = `slain 79995 / first 6 / held 79995 1421 1`; the next run on the same state file: `ring: 1 creature(s) stay dead from an earlier run`, `1 item(s) from an earlier kill are in your pack: [(1421, 1)]` and no `+ vermin ... guid 79995`.
  - **Gates:** scoped fmt `--check`, `cargo clippy -j 4 --workspace --all-targets -- -D warnings`, workspace tests with the installs set: wow-souls 708, wow-world 195 + 4, dsr-cache 58, dsr-formats 52, wscl 4.
  - **Final review (fresh opus reviewer) found a Critical and an Important, both fixed with a failing test first:** (1) *Replace character* archived five saves but not `world-state-v1.txt`, so a new character found the old one's Garrick dead and quest 6 could never finish: `menu::REPLACED_SAVES` now holds six names incl. `world_state::FILE_NAME`; (2) taking stamina gear off at low health floored current HP at 1 and putting it back gave the lost part back (a free, repeatable heal, there is no regeneration): `level::follow` keeps a `debt` that is paid back on the next rise, cleared by a full bar. Deferred minors: an unreadable or BOM-prefixed state file is read as empty and later overwritten; a failed state save is not retried until the next change; a rare's first-kill item is lost if the player dies or presses F6 before looting it (no rare is placed yet; `held` is for never-respawning ranks only); `takeable_later` stops at the first refusal (quests 344-347 are NotPlayable but report Level: no valley loot carries their items yet); a slain spawn stays dead even if its rank override changes (debug path); no game-level system tests for the corpse / held / reset wiring.
  - **For the user's hands / open:** the whole valley now runs 10-40 % lower HP than before (level 1 = 60: a kobold's blow is 2-3 hits), the lever is `HEALTH_OFFSET` in `wow-world/src/attributes.rs` (+40 restores level 1 = 100); the AP formulas are from MaNGOS and not checked against the client; Garrick's guaranteed drop is the Pitted Defias Shortsword (the rest of his table is white food and poor gear: an authored reward would be a later choice); no rare in the valley (nearest: Thuros Lightfingers level 11 and Mother Fang level 10, Elwynn); not built: random-suffix items, resistances, crit / dodge / parry from Agility, mana and Spirit regeneration, class rows other than the Warrior's, a boss killed by hand-placed (non-ring) spawn is not remembered (no spawn guid).
- **GEAR EFFECTS BUILT (2026-10-12; the user: "detta måste fixas så gear gör något", chose "hybrid", then "Native" execution; nothing committed; the user's next step is stats: Str / Stam etc.).** Spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-12-gear-effects-design.md` (approved), plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-12-gear-effects.md` (7 tasks, all done).
  - **Rules** (`wow-world/src/gear_effect.rs`, pure, 9 tests): armor lowers the damage taken by `armor / (armor + 400 + 85 x attacker level)`, capped 75 %; `weapon_factor` = `(weapon dps / Worn Shortsword dps)^0.5` clamped 0.75-2.5 (the starting kit is exactly x1.0; fists x1.0) scales the damage the DS moveset deals (main hand for melee, the ranged slot for projectiles); a shield's block value takes 5 % per point (max 50 %) off a *blocked* blow's chip. Poise, levels and reactions are unchanged (Dark Souls).
  - **Game** (`src/gear_effect.rs`): `GearFx(GearEffect)` resource refreshed from `Owned` + `Data` when the gear changes (log line `gear: armor 195 (23 % off a level 3 blow), melee x1.38, ranged x1.00, block 1`); `WOWSOULS_GEAR_EFFECT=0` makes gear do nothing in a fight. `SpeciesDef.level` (Laborer 4, Vermin 2, Worker 3, Timber Wolf 2, Young Wolf 1, Thug 4, Garrick 5: `creature_template.level_max`). Hooks: `hits.rs` `strike` (`* gear.0.melee_factor`), `projectiles.rs` `release` (`* ranged_factor`), `npc.rs` both `PlayerHurt` writers (`gear.0.taken(damage, species.0.level, blocked)`: applied where the damage is made, so shake / blood / voice see the reduced number; the `die` script's 9999 is untouched). **`hurt.rs` and `defense.rs` were not edited** (deviation from the first spec draft, spec updated). The equipment page's totals line reads `Armor 195 (-23 %)     Block 1     Hits x1.38` (against a level 3 attacker; `docs/evidence/gear-page.png`); WoW's tooltip text is unchanged.
  - **Measured live** (unattended, scratch saves, one kobold at a still player): start kit armor 7 -> kobold `run_strike` 34 / `small_1` 25; leather set + Large Round Shield (armor 195) -> 27 / (big 35 ->) 28, with `WOWSOULS_GEAR_EFFECT=0` back to 34 / 35; Militia Shortsword (same family as the Worn Shortsword, dps 2.0) hits for 28 against 20 with the switch off; a held guard (`WOWSOULS_GUARD=6`) with the same set: chip 13.0 -> 10, 12.5 -> 9, 17.5 -> 13, 9.5 -> 7 (armor x 0.79, shield x 0.95).
  - **Final review (fresh opus reviewer) found one Important, fixed:** a shield put away (a two-handed grip with H, a drawn bow: `equip::apply` hides it) still gave its armor and block while guard stays allowed, so two-handing was a free defence. `GearEffect::from_gear(gear, items, off_hand_in_use)` now drops the off hand's armor and block when the moveset is two-handed or ranged (`crate::gear_effect::off_hand_held`); the equipment page quotes the same stance-aware figure. Measured: `WOWSOULS_WEAPON=straight_sword_2h` with the leather set + Large Round Shield: `armor 140 (18 %), block 0 (shield stowed)` (195 with the shield held), kobold `big` 35 -> 29. If the user would rather keep WoW parity (armor counts while the shield is on the back), pass `true` in `gear_effect.rs` `refresh`. Two numbers in the spec's example list are 0.01 off (Billy Club 1.92, Hunting Bow 2.09; the code and its test are right).

A dagger's shorter lunge did not reach a still kobold at 3.5 yd in the live run (the same family sword did), unrelated to this change.
