**MENU PARTS 2 AND 3 BUILT: SETTINGS, KEY BINDINGS AND THE SYSTEM MENU (2026-10-12, the same request as part 1: "allt en meny behöver med keybindings etc"; the design was shown and approved with "Kör"; nothing committed).** Spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-12-settings-and-system-menu-design.md`. With part 1 below, START HERE job 2 (the menu) is done.
- **Settings** (`src/settings/mod.rs`, new): `Settings { sound, video, game, binds }` (a resource), one text file `settings-v1.txt` beside the other saves in `%LOCALAPPDATA%\WoWSouls`: `wssettings 1`, then `key value` lines (`sound.master|music|ambience|effects|voices|loop`, `video.preset|shadows|ao|bloom|fog|light|sky|wind|water|grading|farclip|grass|fullscreen|vsync`, `game.mouse|stick|invert_y|shake|help|pause`), then `bind <action> <chord> <chord>` and `pad <action> <button>`. Read at start, written the moment something changes (`save_when_changed`, `Last`); a line that cannot be read is skipped and logged, the rest still loads. **The environment switches still win for the run** (`WOWSOULS_VOLUME / _MUSIC / _AMBIENCE / _MUSIC_LOOP / _SHAKE / _GFX`: `Settings::with_env`, `VideoConfig::startup`), and **an unattended run neither reads nor writes the file unless `WOWSOULS_SETTINGS=<file>` names one** (every live run below used a scratch file, so the user's own settings were never touched). Consumers: `mixer.rs` (a live master gain, ramped per buffer), `audio.rs` (`set_levels`: effects and voices), `zone.rs` (`Loudness`: music, ambience, loop), `shake.rs` (scale), `gfx/config.rs` + `gfx/mod.rs` (`VideoConfig::startup`; `from_env` is gone), `boot.rs` (window mode, vsync), `hud/mod.rs` (the help line is built from the bindings, `game.help` hides it). `README.md` has the switch rows.
- **Key bindings** (`src/settings/binds.rs`, new): 31 `Action`s in six groups, per action **two key / mouse chords and one pad button**. Defaults are the keys the game always had (W A S D, Ctrl walk, F / LMB light, R / RMB heavy, Space roll, Shift + Space jump, E block, C parry, Q / MMB lock, X use, V Salve, H two-hand, Y ranged, 1-4 / Z abilities, Tab pack, G equipment, L quest log, T talk, Esc menu, arrows look; pad: A jump, B roll, RB light, RT heavy, LB block, LT parry, R3 lock, Y use, X Salve, L3 cycle, D-pad left ranged / down use ability / right quest log / up talk, Select equipment, **Start menu**). A chord is the action's alone: assigning it takes it from its owner and the menu says so ("F is Roll / backstep now; Light attack lost it."); a chord is active when its source is pressed, its modifiers are held and no more specific chord on the same source is also held (Shift + Space = jump never also rolls); mouse chords count only while the cursor is captured. **Every reader now goes through `Controls`** (a SystemParam: `just_pressed(Action)`, `read(|binds, held| ..)`, `binds().hint(Action)` for prompts): `controller.rs` (`read_pad(binds, ..)`), `camera.rs` (`look_stick`, `orbit_input`; `manage_cursor` frees the cursor while a menu is up), `combat/mod.rs` (`attack_pressed ... guard_held(binds, ..)`; `act` gained `settings` and `menu` and is not `live` while the menu is up), `lockon.rs`, `weapons.rs` (Ranged, TwoHand), `abilities/mod.rs`, `loot/mod.rs`, `quests/mod.rs` (small edits in files co-owned with the other chat: re-read them before touching). `MenuUp(bool)` makes `Controls` answer "nothing pressed" while any menu or capture is up, so a key typed into the name box or a rebinding never also acts in the world.
- **The system menu** (`src/sysmenu/mod.rs` = the pure model with 17 tests, settings 9 and binds 14; `ui.rs` = panel, input, pause, script): **Esc or the pad's Menu / Start** opens it in the world (Tab / G / L still open the pack / equipment / quest log directly). Pages: **Menu** (Resume, Pack, Equipment, Quest log, Options, Quit game), **Options** (Sound, Graphics, Controls: keyboard and mouse, Controls: gamepad, Game), **Sound** (master, music, ambience, sound effects, quest voices, Loop music), **Graphics** (preset Classic / Low / Medium / High / Ultra, then shadows, ambient occlusion, bloom, volumetric fog, volumetric light, sky, foliage wind, water, colour grading, render distance, grass, fullscreen, vsync; the preset row reads "Custom (High)" once a lane differs from it), **Controls** (every action under its group with its two chords or its pad button; "Reset to defaults" resets one device and keeps the other's bindings), **Game** (mouse and stick / arrow look speed, invert Y, camera shake, help line, pause while the menu is open). Input: keyboard (Up / Down, Left / Right change, Enter, Esc back, Delete clears a key), pad (D-pad / stick, A, B, Menu closes), mouse (hover, click, wheel scrolls the 13-row window). A binding row opens a **capture** (`[Press a key]` / `[Press a button]`: the next key, mouse button or pad button; modifiers held then a key make a chord; Esc cancels; a pad capture gives up after 6 s because a pad cannot press Esc). The title's **Options** row opens the same overlay (`SysMenu::show_options`); everything applies live and is saved at once.
- **Pause**: `Time<Virtual>` is paused while the menu is open (`game.pause`, on by default; sound and the screens run on real time). **Verified by pixels:** two shots two seconds apart with the menu open are byte-identical (md5) while the avatar is running before and after (`WOWSOULS_DRIVE=-1,0`, `WOWSOULS_SYSMENU_SCRIPT=16:open,22:close`).
- **Decided without asking (inside the approved design):** the pad's Start was the pack, now it is the menu and the pack is Menu -> Pack (the pad has no direct pack button by default; assign one under Controls: gamepad); Esc used to free the cursor, now it opens the menu and the cursor comes back with it; the HUD help line reads the live bindings (`Shift + G light` after a rebind); sex and class stay choosable in creation (see part 1) but Create takes only a male Human Warrior.
- **Verified live (unattended, `WOWSOULS_MENU=0`, scratch `WOWSOULS_SETTINGS` / `_CHARACTER` / `_ITEMS_SAVE` / `_QUESTS_SAVE` / `_SOULS_SAVE`; script verbs `open close up down left right confirm back delete capture:<chord> pad:<button>`, e.g. `capture:Shift+KeyG`, `capture:Mouse:Back`, `pad:North`)**: the menu opens, every page navigates, sliders / toggles / presets change and persist to the settings file, a keyboard capture with the displacement note and the HUD line updating, the pad capture UI (`[Press a button]`), reset, **a preset change repaints the world** (High -> Classic: grass thinned, bloom, volumetric light and shadows gone: `docs/evidence/sysmenu-preset-before-high.png` / `-after-classic.png`), the title's Options overlay. Pictures `docs/evidence/sysmenu-*.png`, `title-options.png`. Gates: scoped fmt `--check`, `cargo clippy -j 4 --workspace --all-targets -- -D warnings` (exit 0), workspace tests with the installs set: wow-souls 687, dsr-cache 58, dsr-formats 52, wow-world 159 + 4, wscl 4.
- **Open / known:** (1) the options overlay is drawn over the title's list, so the title's footer ("Enter / A Select") is cut by the panel's left edge; (2) no scrollbar art (wheel and cursor only); (3) a pad has no direct pack button by default; (4) the pad's Move rows show "-" (the left stick is fixed; a D-pad binding for a direction works but is untested on a real pad); (5) fullscreen and vsync apply the moment they flip (no confirm step); (6) the sound sliders were checked through the settings file and the mixer's test, not by ear: listen once.

**MENU PART 1 BUILT: WOW'S OWN CHARACTER CREATION, IN SOULS PARCHMENT (2026-10-12; the user: "Gör menyn - jag skulle vilja ha orginal character creation från WoW implementerat. Lägg till en wow souls spin på det dock. Sen grafik etc - allt en meny behöver med keybindings etc"; decided with the user: **show everything, only the Human Warrior (male) is playable**, **the Souls style**; nothing committed).** Spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-12-character-creation-design.md`, plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-12-character-creation.md`. The menu is three parts: **1 = this one (title / select + creation)**, 2 = settings + key bindings, 3 = the in-game system menu with Sound / Graphics / Controls / Game pages (2 and 3 were built the same day: see the entry above).
- **What it is.** `src/create/` (new): `rules.rs` (pure: `Rules` = class lists per race + the five dial ranges from the client's `CharCreateCatalog`, `Selection` = race / sex / class / dials / name / yaw with WoW's rules: a race switch resets the class to the race's first and the facing to -15 degrees, a sex switch re-clamps, dials wrap, `randomize`), `strings.rs` (GlueStrings.lua parser: `RACE_INFO_*`, `ABILITY_INFO_*`, `CLASS_*`, `FACTION_INFO_*`, `HAIR_*`, `FACIAL_HAIR_*`, `CHAR_CUSTOMIZATION*_DESC`), `art.rs` (the `UI-CharacterCreate-{Races,Classes,Gender,Factions,Banners}` sheets as sprites + the Lua texcoord tables; sheet sizes checked against the BLP headers), `ui.rs` (the screen: `CharacterCreate.xml`'s numbers in 1024 x 768 units, one unit = 720/768 layout px under the HUD's `UiScale`; left tower with two race columns, gender pair, class grid, five dial rows, Randomize; right stack with the faction / race / class panels (wheel scrolls); name box, rotate buttons, "Prepare to die" and Back), `preview.rs` (the 3D body: one `Preview` actor from `catalog.body_display(race, sex)` + the look + `start_outfit` mapped to `Dress` slots; a dial or class re-dresses the same body, a new race / sex spawns the next body hidden and swaps when built; idle clip; framed by `body_height(race)`). `menu.rs` keeps the flow: `Menu` embeds `Selection`, a **focus ring** (`Row`: Race, Class, Sex, five dials (facial hair only where the race has it), Randomize, Name, Create, Back) for keyboard / pad (Up / Down move, Left / Right change the focused row, Enter acts, Esc / B back; **letters always type the name**, the pre-filled "Adventurer" goes at the first letter, Backspace clears it at once), `Press::{Race, Sex, ClassSlot, Dial, Randomize, Create}` for the mouse (the screen's buttons send `MenuPress` messages) and for `WOWSOULS_MENU_SCRIPT` (`race:<id> sex:<0|1> class:<slot> dial:<i>:<+1|-1> random create char:<c> up down left right confirm back backspace`), sounds `gsCharacterCreationClass / Look / CreateChar / Cancel`.
- **Souls spin.** WoW's layout and icons in the Dark Souls parchment panels (`soul_param/bg_parchment_0`), Georgia, ivory / gold; the Create button reads **"Prepare to die"**; the menu scene is lit at **dusk** (`daycycle::menu_minute`: 18:40 in the Streaming and Menu phases unless `WOWSOULS_HOUR` / `WOW_CLOCK` names the hour; an unattended run keeps its noon; `WOWSOULS_MENU_HOUR=<h | h:mm>` names it; the game clock itself is untouched).
- **Playable.** **Only the Human race can be chosen: the other seven are drawn grey (dark icon, quiet edge) and ignore clicks and the Left / Right keys (the user, mid-build: "vi fokuserar bara på humans just nu så vi skulle kunna bara göra dom andra raser gråa så man inte kan välja dom").** `Rules::only_races(&[1])` in `create::load`; `WOWSOULS_CREATE_ALL=1` lifts it (a look at every body, and what the instruments of the screen use). Sex and class stay choosable (a Human has both sexes and six classes in 1.12): Create accepts only a male Human Warrior and says so ("Only a male Human Warrior can be made yet; the others are coming."; the same note shows under the name box). The dials apply to the human male body.
- **LATER, IMPORTANT (the user: "varje ras ska ha sin egen bakgrund - för varje ras ska ha sin starter zone"): every race gets its own backdrop = its own start zone.** Today the one scene is the Northshire Valley in front of the Abbey, which IS the Human start, so the only choosable race is right. When another race becomes choosable its backdrop is its start zone (1.12: Human Northshire Valley / Elwynn, Dwarf and Gnome Coldridge Valley / Dun Morogh, Night Elf Shadowglen / Teldrassil, Orc and Troll Valley of Trials / Durotar, Undead Deathknell / Tirisfal Glades, Tauren Red Cloud Mesa / Mulgore). The positions are in the vMaNGOS database (`playercreateinfo`: map, zone, position per race and class; query a COPY, see "Quests: data import"); the engine streams any ADT around the `ViewFocus`, so the preview body and the camera move to the race's start point (`create/preview.rs` places both relative to the avatar today: make it a per-race anchor, and let `settle::publish_focus` follow it while the creation screen is up). The old client's glue backdrops (`Interface\Glues\Models\UI_<Race>`) are the alternative. Each race also needs its own zone's quests, creatures and the Spoken voices before it is playable, so this belongs with sub-project H.
- **Save.** `character-v1.txt` is now `wschar 2` with `skin face hair_style hair_color facial_hair` (a `wschar 1` file loads with the old default look 2 / 1 / 3 / 1 / 1, so an existing save looks as before). `avatar::look_for(env, chosen)`: `WOWSOULS_LOOK`, else the chosen dials on the human male body, else `DEFAULT_LOOK`; `apply_chosen_look` (OnEnter Playing) re-dresses the avatar when the first character is made in this run.
- **Found on the way (fixed): the avatar was never really hidden.** The engine's `exterior_cull` ("body leg") writes `Visibility` of every `WorldUnit` that has a `bound` each frame, so `avatar::hide_when_close` was undone before drawing (first-person included). It now sets the avatar's `bound` aside while hidden (`WorldUnit.bound = None` is "not the cull's to decide") and puts it back; `HideAvatar` (the preview's flag) joins the first-person fade.
- **Verified live** (unattended, `WOWSOULS_MENU=1`, `WOWSOULS_CHARACTER=<scratch>` so the user's save is never touched, `WOWSOULS_MENU_SCRIPT`, `WOWSOULS_SHOT_AT`): the first-run creation screen, eight bodies in one run (human m, night elf m / f, orc f, tauren f, troll f, undead m, dwarf m, gnome m), race / class / dial / sex labels per race (night elf "Markings", tauren "Horn Style / Horn Color / Hair"), the title with a saved character, "New character" opening a fresh creation, typing a name + dials + Create -> `menu: created Jacob the Human Warrior`, the `wschar 2` file, `avatar: takes the chosen look`, the screen gone in the world, a 1024 x 768 window, the sound kits 814 / 817 / 818. Pictures `docs/evidence/create-*.png`. Gates: scoped fmt `--check`, clippy `-D warnings` workspace, tests wow-souls 643 (+ dsr-cache 58, dsr-formats 52, wow-world 159 + 4, wscl 4).
- **Open / known:** the preview holds no weapon (the outfit's weapon / shield types are skipped); the Tauren's mane and tail draw white (the engine's hair-texture lookup for that model: not looked at); the screen has no scrollbar art (wheel only); the title list gained its Options row with part 3 (the options panel is drawn over it and cuts its footer, see the entry above); the sound volumes are the settings' (part 2).

**BATTLE SHOUT'S VFX FIXED: THE EFFECT BUILDER NOW BUILDS WHAT WOW'S EFFECT MODELS CONTAIN (2026-10-12; the user: "Battle Shout är buggigt med vfx'n - ska vara samma som det är i wow"; nothing committed).**
- **Cause (measured, `benilla-extract spellvis 6673`, `m2batch`, `m2anim`, `m2bones`):** spell 6673 -> `SpellVisual` 246, cast stage only: anim 55 (BattleRoar), sound kit 3113, `BattleShout_Cast_Base.mdx` on attachment 0x13 (the base). That model has **no particle emitters**: six additive quads (`Spells\GradientCrescent.blp`, 0.99 yd, flat on the ground plane) on six bones. Bone 0 carries a static +90 deg turn and bone 4 (its child) another +180 deg, so the three crescents under each fly out to the warrior's two sides (translation 1.835 yd, scale 1 -> 3, staggered 0 / 0.13 / 0.23 s start, done 0.67 / 0.8 / 0.9 s), the colour loops white -> red (M2Color) and the alpha loops fade them over the 0.9 s sequence. Our effect path (`loot::vfx` -> `ActorModel` -> `actor::build`) drew them as ordinary depth-tested creature batches with none of that: no alpha loop (so a crescent stayed at full strength, a white one at the end of the clip), no colour loop (white, not red), and the flat quads buried by any slope or step.
- **Fix:** `loot/vfx.rs` rewritten as a port of benilla's spell-effect attach (`entities/spell_fx/mod.rs`: `attach_effect_visuals`, `arm_effect_rig`, `fx_part_material`, `tick_fx_tint`, in the graphics fork; `PROVENANCE.md`): the model's own joint hierarchy under the instance root (`spawn_joints`, one `AnimationPlayer`, palette slot), every batch a skinned part whose **material alpha loops run on the instance's clock** (`MatAnim::driving_tag`) and whose **colour and texture-transform loops run on a per-instance clone of the material** (`FxTints`, `register_fx_uv`), billboard batches as cards on their joint, **flat ground quads as terrain-draping decals** (`benilla_world::ground_fx`: the engine's own replacement for what the 1.12 client draws as depth-tested batches, `GroundQuad`), emitters and **ribbons** on the joints. A one-shot's geometry goes when its sequence ends (`FxVisual`, `settle`), its particles a second later; the effect root no longer carries an `ActorModel`.
- **Verified live** (`WOWSOULS_RAGE=100 WOWSOULS_ABILITY_SCRIPT=6.5:2` on a copy of `cache/ability-check/items.txt`): red crescents flying out left and right of the warrior over the ground, draped over the doorstep and the grass, gone with the sequence (no white leftovers). Same builder, seen working: **Thunder Clap** (rings, the purple ground burst, both hands, the impact lightning), **Charge** (its trail is a ribbon: now drawn, a thin red streak behind the warrior, plus the landing dust), the **level-up flash** (`WOWSOULS_QUESTS_SCRIPT=6.8:xp:60000`: the golden column of light, 12 parts + 5 emitters, was only its emitters before), the corpse's **loot sparkles** (4 emitters, as before). Pictures `docs/evidence/shout-after-sheet.png` (+ `loco-shout*` per frame while they last). Gates: fmt, clippy `-D warnings` workspace, wow-souls 606 tests (one new: `settle`).
- **Not ported / open:** the host alpha chain (`ParentModel`: a unit's fade does not fade its effects: matters for a dying creature's sparkles), the interior light variant (an effect indoors is lit as outdoors), M2 lights on effects, the kit stage logic (Hold / Decay legs: the abilities only use one-shots), `ATTACH_FALLBACKS` (a model lacking its attachment id stays at the owner's origin). The Charge ribbon is as thin as authored (`height_above/below`), look at it before calling it right.

**ELDEN RING AND SHEATHING (the user: "Hur gör Elden Ring det, kan du kolla det?", 2026-10-12; web research: guides, forum threads, the wiki page of the Unsheathe skill; no official source).** Elden Ring has **no automatic sheathing and no sheathe button**: an equipped weapon stays drawn in the hand, with relaxed per-weapon idle animations. The only way to put it away is manual: hold the hand modifier (Triangle / Y / E on PC) and press the hand button (L1 / LB / left click) for an **empty** slot (two-handing the empty hand), which puts the other hand's weapon on the back or hip (a per-weapon sheath setting) until you swing again (an attack draws it; it survives fast travel); a dual-wielder cannot stow both. Katanas have a *deliberate* sheathed stance, the "Unsheathe" Ash of War (blade at the hip, slow walk, almost no stamina regeneration, a quick draw slash on light attack, an overhead draw on strong). Before release an insider claimed "scabbards, no sheathing mechanic". So both Dark Souls 1 and Elden Ring keep the weapon in the hand: what we have (weapon in hand in its passive stance, now with the DS gait) is what they do. **If the user still wants it, WoW's own assets can do it authentically** (checked on `HumanMale.m2`): sheath clips 89 / 90 (1.0 s each, the engine masks them to one arm over the gait), the hip attachments 9 / 10 and the back 12 for where a sheathed one-hander / two-hander rides, and the engine's `arm_nodes`; WoW itself sheathes out of combat. Not built: a design choice for the user (a toggle key, or after N seconds out of combat, or both).

**THE DARK SOULS BACKSTEP (2026-10-12, the same session; the second "missing animation"; nothing committed).** A still stick + the evade key (Space / B) was WoW's Dodge sway (clip 30, 1.8 yd in 0.55 s, i-frames 0.03-0.25 s). It is now DSR's own medium backstep `a00_0690` (the roll's weight class, `a00_0710`): 1.83 s, 2.27 yd backwards after the retarget's scale, four invulnerable frames (0.0-0.133 s), movement / attack / evade cancel from 0.87 s, price 15 stamina as before. The sister clips are the lighter `a00_0680` (cancel 0.60 s) and the heavier `a00_0695` (1.03 s); `0697` is another variant (1.0 s, 8 i-frames, -2.27 m: its meaning unknown).
- **Extractor**: `roll.rs` `MEDIUM_BACKSTEP`, `pick_backstep` (the named clip, four i-frames, backwards travel, no drift, cancel 0.867 s: nothing else is accepted); `cache.rs::extract` writes the pair and a `backstep a00_0690` manifest line (after `roll`). **An older cache has no such line and the WoW sway stays**; re-run `wow-souls-dsr extract` (also rebuilds `weapons-*`: the superseded generations were deleted, 11 of them, 500 MB) to get it.
- **Game**: `DsRoll.back: Option<EvadeClip>` (clips + `EvadeSpec` from `dsclip::backstep_spec`: the roll's reading of the windows, the distance as a positive number, the backstep's price), `ClipId::Backstep` (`playing` names it for a DS backstep, `clips_of`, `WOWSOULS_DSVIEW=backstep`), `StepInput.backstep` / `ds_backstep` (`combat/action.rs`: `start` picks them for `EvadeKind::Backstep`; the roll's and the procedural shapes are unchanged), `combat/mod.rs`: the backstep's root motion runs backwards (`progress(prev) - progress(t)` along `e.dir`, which already points against the facing) and it does not turn the body. Verified live (`WOWSOULS_ROLL=6.5` with no drive): `Backstep moved 1.85 yd in 1.84 s` against the Abbey's steps (the clip's own 2.27 yd without the obstacle), hop back, crouch, recover; `docs/evidence/loco-sheet-back.png`. The roll (`WOWSOULS_DRIVE=-1,0 WOWSOULS_ROLL=6.5`) still moves 4.15 yd until its cancel at 1.10 s. Tests: action machine 1, dsclip load / older cache / `playing` 2 (the whole-cache test and the playing test extended), dsr-cache picker 2. Gates: workspace tests wow-souls 605, dsr-cache 58; fmt and clippy clean.
- **The feel changes**: DS's backstep is slower and longer than the old hop (0.87 s until it can be cancelled, against 0.40 s; the i-frames are short: 0.13 s). Tune by the user's hands: if it feels too long, the lighter class `a00_0680` is one constant (`MEDIUM_BACKSTEP`: cancel 0.60 s, 2.06 m). **The user played it (2026-10-12): "Kändes inte värre": keep `a00_0690`.**

**DARK SOULS LOCOMOTION BUILT: THE REAL WALK, RUN, SPRINT AND LOCKED STEPS (2026-10-12; the user: "Kolla handover - jobba på animationerna som saknas" = START HERE job 3; nothing committed).** The WoW gait (and the carry-pose fade that handed the arms back to WoW's run clip) is replaced, per weapon, by Dark Souls' own locomotion clips, retargeted like the roll and the idle.
- **Which clip is which (measured, `wow-souls-dsr loco [--set a00] [--clips a00_0500,...]`, read-only; the files carry no names).** Every weapon motion set holds the same gait clips with the same timing and root travel, posed for its weapon (`a12` over the shoulder...): `<set>_0200` walk, `0300-0303` locked-on walk (forward, back, left, right), `0500` run, `0550-0553` locked-on run, `0510` sprint (`a00_0535` is a faster sprint in the common set only), all looping. In WoW yards per second after the retarget's scale (about 1.135 yd per DS metre): walk 1.70, locked walk 1.84, run 4.17, locked run 3.84, sprint 5.70 (`0510`) or 7.19 (`a00_0535`). Left unused: `0560-0563` (a slower run, 2.79 m/s: its meaning is unknown; its SpEffect 804 is the run's), `0310-0313` (a walk 20 % slower, with the guard raised?), `0210/0214` (guard walks), `0204-0207` (turning walks), `0800-0807`, `0602-0605` (turns in place). TAE kind 24 = SpEffect (804 run, 511 sprint, 761), kind 112 = foot windows; the foot-down times are NOT read from them (see below).
- **Extractor** (`dsr-cache`): `loco.rs` (`ROLES`: 11 roles, each a list of animation ids, the first the motion set has, the common set otherwise; `clip_for`; the measuring), `cache.rs::extract_locomotion` (also the last step of `extract`; independent of the combat kits like `interaction.txt`): `cache/dsr/locomotion.txt` = `locomotion-version 1`, `source` lines, `loco <set> <role> <clip>` for the sets `a00 a10 a12 a13 a14 a16` (66 clips, shared by name in `human_male/` and `c0000/`, 0.5 s to write). Every clip passes the existing retarget gates (direction drift 0.06 deg, cross drift at most 3 deg, foot gap at most 0.050 yd on contact frames) and carries no hit and no invulnerability. `wow-souls-dsr probe --kit player --clips a00_0200,...` now prints the retarget numbers of the player's own clips (was monster kits only).
- **Game** (`src/dsloco.rs`, new; `dsclip.rs` +30 lines, `foot.rs` +8, `dsview.rs` +9, `shot.rs`): `DsLoco` (every kit by set), `Kit` (11 `Clip`s: wow + ds clip, `stride` yd per loop, `plants` foot-down phases, `closure`), `DsMoves.loco` (the kit of the held weapon's set, from its idle clip's name `a12_0000`, attached by `attach` without marking the moves changed), `DsGait` on the avatar (`phase`, `weight`, `kit`). The gait is a **blend space over the achieved speed**: free (the body turns to its travel) = walk / run / sprint by speed, locked on = the two nearest of the four directions (cosine / sine of the step's angle, so a diagonal is two halves) at the walk or run speed; one stride phase advances by `speed / stride` of the blend (the feet never slide whatever the speed; capped at 1.35 x the fastest clip's pace, so a charge at 24 yd/s is a sprint that cannot keep up, not a flicker). Its weight eases 0.2 s in and out and is 1 only when grounded (a bump keeps the stride for 0.25 s as the WoW gait does, a jump does not), moving (0.6 on / 0.3 off yd/s) and in `Idle` or a raised guard (an action poses the body itself; a `Casting` shout or clap too). It runs in `PosePost` **before** `dsclip::ClipLayers` (the stance and the action clips), as the base layer: the stance's weight is scaled by `1 - gait weight`, `RestPose.legs` / `carry` are bypassed while a kit is in use, the held blade's grip gets the gait as its base layer. Loops that do not close in the source (the polearm's run: 11.7 deg at one shoulder, the worst) are closed by spreading the difference over the cycle (`closure`). **Footsteps** are the gait's own foot-downs (the ankles' tracks over the ground, root motion added, holding still = planted: robust where heights were not), sent as `$FSD` `AnimSound`s so a step sounds when the visible foot lands; `foot::fire` stays quiet for a body whose gait owns the legs. Switches: none new for the gait; `WOWSOULS_DSVIEW=walk|run|sprint|lock_run_l|...` (or a clip name) shows a gait clip beside the DS skeleton; `WOWSOULS_SHOT_AT=6,6.1,6.2` writes `<name>-<s>.png` per time (a gait cycle in one run).
- **Verified live (unattended, open meadow `WOWSOULS_AT=-8906,-136 WOWSOULS_DRIVE=-1,0 WOWSOULS_KOBOLDS=0 WOWSOULS_SPIN=0`; sheets in `docs/evidence/loco-sheet-*.png`)**: walk at push 0.35 (upright, the sword held forward), run at 0.6 (moderate lean, arms pumping), sprint at 1.0 (heavy forward lean, long stride) and a locked-on strafe left / right; the stop (the gait fades into the stance within a few frames: no pop), an attack out of a run, a guard raised while walking (shield up over walking DS legs), the greatsword's own carry (`WOWSOULS_WEAPON=greatsword`: both hands on the weapon over the shoulder) and the polearm's. Footsteps with `WOWSOULS_AUDIO=null`: 13 steps in 3.6 s of sprint = the stride's 3.25 steps/s. **A note on the picture of the gait**: it is a Dark Souls character on a WoW body; the arms/hands are driven (retarget) and the WoW fingers hold the weapon.
- **Tests**: dsloco 14 (blend weights, stride, phase advance, foot-down detection, loop closure, set from the idle name, the built cache loads every set when `WOWSOULS_TEST_LOCOMOTION=<cache/dsr>` is set), dsr-cache 56 (`loco::tests`: roles resolve in every set, direction and pace of every clip). Gates: scoped fmt `--check`, `cargo clippy -j 4 --workspace --all-targets -- -D warnings` clean, workspace tests with the installs set: wow-souls 603, dsr-cache 56, dsr-formats 52, wow-world 159 + 4, wscl 4.
- **Open / for the user**: (1) **the pace**: WoW's run is 7 yd/s, Dark Souls' sprint (7.2 yd/s with `a00_0535`); the DS *run* is 4.2 yd/s. With the motor as it is a free run IS the DS sprint (the lean in `loco-sheet-sprint2.png`); a push of 0.6 (a half-tilted stick) is the DS run. The choice: keep 7 yd/s (the valley's distances and the quests' pacing were tuned on it), or make the run 4.2 yd/s and sprint on a key with stamina as Dark Souls does (one number `RUN_SPEED` plus a sprint key and a stamina cost). **Decided 2026-10-12: the user played it ("känns bra"): keep 7 yd/s, no sprint key, until they say otherwise.** (2) turn in place (`a00_0602-0605`), the sprint's start (`0510`) and stop (`0511`) are not played; the body turns at `TURN_RATE` over the stance. (3) NPC humans (Defias, guards, Garrick's walk) still use WoW's gait; only the avatar has the DS one. (4) the backstep was WoW's Dodge clip: built the same day, see the entry above (`a00_0690`).

**RUN LOOKED ABSURD: THE CARRY POSE NOW FADES AT SPEED (2026-10-11; the user: "Kolla på chatgpts jobb med animationerna. Nu ser det löjligt ut när han springer. Ska också finnas en resting position ... bör weapon sheet? Hur gör dark souls det?"; nothing committed).** Cause (read in `dsclip.rs` `pose`): the weapon's passive stance (`a00_0000` etc.) kept the spine, shoulders, arms and hands (`carry_bone`) at full weight while the WoW gait ran the legs: a frozen upper body, the sword held out like a poker (`docs/evidence/equip-runb-6.0.png`). Fix: `RestPose.carry` / `carry_share(speed)`: full up to 3 yd/s (stand, walk), gone at 4.4 (the locked strafe is 4.5, the run 7), eased over `BLEND_OUT`; the Stance layer's weight (bones and the held blade's grip) is scaled by it, so at a run WoW's own run clip swings the arms (`equip-runc-6.0.png`, `equip-runc-6.2.png`). One test (`the_carry_pose_holds_through_a_walk_and_gives_the_arms_back_at_a_run`). **Not DS locomotion**: the real Dark Souls run is in the install (`c0000_a00_*.anibnd`, "locomotion, rolls, backstep, damage, death") and is not extracted; doing that is the proper next step (clips for walk / run / strafe / back per weapon category, rate matched to the motor's speed, the extractor + retarget + a locomotion layer in `dsclip.rs`). **Sheathing is a design question for the user**: Dark Souls 1 / DSR has no timed sheathing, the weapon stays in the hand in its relaxed passive stance (what the standing breathing loop already is); the c0000 skeleton does carry `L_Wepon_Case`, `R_Wepon_Case`, `B_Wepon_Case` holster bones. Elden Ring and WoW do sheathe.

**ABILITIES REWORKED: CHARGE, BATTLE SHOUT, THUNDER CLAP (2026-10-11; the user: "Heroic strike skulle vi väl inte ha? ... Det var la battle shout, charge och thundercla? Charge är det man lär sig först", then "Charge ska generera rage också"; nothing committed).** The approved set (see the 2026-10-10 entry: "Charge + Battle Shout only") had grown by itself into five; it is three again. **Heroic Strike and Rend are gone** (data, tests, README), the trainer's list is `Charge, Battle Shout, Thunder Clap`, **nothing is known at the start** (the hotbar is empty until the trainer). `wow-world/src/abilities.rs`: Charge level 1 / 400 souls (the DB says level 4: the user wants it first), Battle Shout level 1 / 40, **Thunder Clap level 4 / 400 (the DB says 6, out of reach in the valley: one number to change)**; all `usable`. The user's save has 4850 souls and 3676 XP, so all three can be bought. Game side `abilities/`: `mod.rs` (rage, `Buffs` = shout only, `Cooldowns`, `activate(.., aim)` -> `Cast::{Shout, Charge(e), Clap}`, refusals incl. "Not ready yet (N s)", "Out of range.", "Target too close.", "You have no target to charge."), `moves.rs` (the deeds), `fx.rs` (WoW's own spell models + sound kits from `benilla-extract spellvis`), `ui.rs` (a slot is dim while the rage is short or on cooldown, with the cooldown seconds over it).
- **Charge** (no rage, 12 s): locked enemy, else the nearest hostile within 60 degrees ahead, 5-25 yd (`pick_target`); rushes at 24 yd/s through `MoveIntent` (system `rush` in `PlaySet::Intent` after `combat::act`), stops 1 yd from the body, **+9 rage on landing** (WoW's rank 1; a "+9 rage" toast shows it, `rage_after_charge`), the enemy is **stunned 1.5 s** (`Stun` message -> `abilities::stun`, in npc.rs' chain after `react`: `Reaction::Flinch(secs)` + WoW's Stun clip 14, its mind reset to a short `Cooldown`). An attack, roll, blow, open screen or a wall (stall 0.3 s) ends it. Cannot-be-used-in-combat is NOT kept (every enemy is aggressive here). Verified live: 24 yd in 0.83 s, "the charge lands (rage 9)", pictures `equip-charge2.png`, `equip-charge3.png`.
- **Battle Shout**: 15 % more damage for 120 s as before (`hits.rs` only reads `Buffs::damage_factor()`, the Heroic Strike hook is gone). WoW's BattleRoar clip (55, 1.0 s) holds the body; the glow model has no emitters (built 0, nothing visible, the sound plays).
- **Thunder Clap** (20 rage, 5 s): 30 damage (x the shout) to every hostile within 5 yd of its body, through the same `Hit` a sword makes (flinch, blood, sound, kill, XP all follow), a 0.5 push, `Slowed` for 10 s (`apply_slow`, in npc.rs' chain after `think`: speed x0.6 and the pause between swings 1/0.6 longer), camera trauma 0.4. WoW's cast clip 181 (0.5 s), the ground ring, lightning in both hands, the lightning impact + slow mark on each enemy, sound kits 3090 / 1506. **What an ability deals builds no rage** (`Rage.ability_damage`, `gain_from_blow`) or a clap on three kobolds would pay for itself. Verified live: `thunder clap reaches 1 of 1 enemies, 34 each` (30 x 1.15), pictures `equip-clap1.png`, `equip-clap-6.25.png`, `equip-clap-8.2.png` (a big white-blue ring, WoW's own and bright).
- **Two edits outside the module**: `actor/anim.rs` `drive` yields while the avatar `Has<Casting>`; `npc.rs` plugin chain gained `crate::abilities::stun` and `crate::abilities::apply_slow`. `loot::vfx::effect` is `pub(crate)`. Bevy trap again: a system with more than 16 parameters does not compile as a system (`input` groups its parameters in tuples).
- **Not done / open**: Charge's trail model (`ChargeTrail.mdx`, a ribbon) and the shout's glow draw nothing (no particle emitters in the effect builder, only the dust and the clap's models show); no DS animation (WoW's clips are used); the stun holds the last pose of the Stun clip loop; a charge through a thin fence is stopped by the stall rule, not planned around; hostile only (critters are not clapped). Tests: wow-souls 587, wow-world 159 + 4; fmt, clippy `-D warnings` workspace clean. A copy of the save for live checks: `cache/ability-check/items.txt` (`WOWSOULS_ITEMS_SAVE`, `learn 100 / 6673 / 6343`), with `WOWSOULS_ABILITY_SCRIPT=6:1`, `WOWSOULS_LOCK=4`, `WOWSOULS_RAGE=100`.


**PACK COMPARISON, CHEST SPARKLES, MUSIC LOOP, SOUND HARDENING (2026-10-11; the user: "när man kollar igenom vad man har i sin backpack så kan man också se gear - ifall det är bättre och sämre", "Ibland buggar ljudet när man slår, då försvinner det helt och hållet", "musiken loopar - finns option för det i wow", "samma vfx när man lootar en fiende när man öppnar en chest"; nothing committed).**
- **Pack (Tab) compares gear** (`loot/ui.rs`, `equip/mod.rs`): every gear row has a mark from `ui::mark(&Card)`: **Better / Worse / Equal** (green / red / grey; `equip::verdict` weighs the compare rows) against what is worn in the slot a quick equip would use, **Level N** (red) when the level stops it, "Not yet" for another refusal, nothing for potions and quest items. The detail panel shows WoW's tooltip for gear, the refusal in red, "Compared with X (slot)", the green / red rows (damage per second, armor, block, "Also takes off your ...") and below them the worn piece's own name + tooltip (WoW's "Currently Equipped"). `equip::Card` gained `worn: Option<Worn>`; the quest / shop card ignores it. Layout: the pack panel grew 7 % downward (`bottom` 21 -> 14 %), the detail icon is 56 px, the detail gap 6. **Bevy trap hit and fixed**: the pack's `refresh` now reads `Node` on the labels, so `labels` is `Without<NoticeRow>` and `notice_rows` `Without<Label>` (a B0001 panic that only shows live, never in the unit tests). Pictures `docs/evidence/equip-pack-compare2.png` (Better, +0.5 dps, worn Worn Shortsword), `equip-pack-compare3.png` (Worse -0.9, Level 4 / Level 5, Equal). I read "se gear" as WoW's bag tooltip with the equipped piece beside it; the paper doll stays on G.
- **Open chests glow** (`loot/mod.rs` `chest_glow`, `vfx::spawn_chest`): a chest in `ChestState::Open(items)` with items shows WoW's own `LootFX` sparkles (the corpse's effect, 4 native emitters) 0.35 yd above its origin, in its own frame, from the moment the lid is open (also for a chest opened in an earlier run) until the items are taken or it restocks shut (then `ChestGlow` is despawned). An opened empty chest does not glow. Verified live (`WOWSOULS_LOOT=1 WOWSOULS_AT=-8894,-235 WOWSOULS_INTERACT=5,20 WOWSOULS_SPIN=0.13`): `loot: chest 9000001 glows, it holds something`, `equip-chest-glow2.png` (sparkles over the open lid), `equip-chest-taken.png` (none after the take, 3 Salves in the pouch). The sparkles are small, like the corpse's: the user may want them bigger.
- **Loop Music** (`zone.rs`): WoW 1.12's own option is "Loop Music" = CVar `SoundZoneMusicNoDelay` ("Enable to continuously play background music", `SoundOptionsFrame.lua`, `GlobalStrings.lua` `ENABLE_MUSIC_LOOPING`): no silence between tracks. It is **on by default here** (the user's wish; WoW's default is off); `WOWSOULS_MUSIC_LOOP=0` gives WoW's silences back. The next track starts the moment the last one ends and is another of the zone's tracks when there is one (`FRESH_TRACK_TRIES`); a track that ends within 1 s of starting (mixer refused it, bad file) waits `RETRY` 5 s instead of spinning. Pure `gap_after` + `music_loops` are tested. There is no options screen in the game, so no toggle in the UI. The seam between two tracks is the decoder's start-up (tens of ms), not a crossfade.
- **Sound that "disappears" when hitting: NOT reproduced, hardened.** I could not hear it, so the cause is not proven. Every mechanism I could find is closed and counted: `mixer.rs` (a) a full mixer (cap 48, was 32) retires the voice nearest its end for the new one instead of dropping the new sound, never a loop; (b) a voice with a gain that is not a number is refused and a NaN / inf output sample is zeroed (a NaN reaching a driver's filters can leave it silent for good; `sfx::gains` also returns `None` for non-finite input, which the old fall-off formula turned into NaN); (c) a panic in the mix empties the mixer instead of killing the stream; (d) a stream the device reports as failed (headset pulled, default device changed) is dropped and **rebuilt on the default device every second until it works**, the mixer resampling for a device with another rate (`set_device_rate`, `Resampler` for streams); (e) `Audio::sound` no longer remembers a failed load for good (retry after 5 s). **The next silence will explain itself:** the audio thread appends to `%LOCALAPPDATA%\WoWSouls\audio.log` (and stderr) a line `stolen N refused N non-finite N panics N errors N restarts N` whenever a counter moves, `stream error: ...` and `the output was built again (N Hz)`. If the user reports it again, read that file first; no file = none of these happened and the cause is elsewhere (suspects left: the music decoder thread starving behind the shared chain lock, or Windows). Tests: 573 game tests (mixer 6 new: stealing, loops refused, NaN gain refused, NaN sample zeroed, other device rate, clear).
- **Abilities ("skills") status:** see the ABILITIES REWORKED entry above (Charge, Battle Shout and Thunder Clap work; Heroic Strike and Rend were dropped).
- Gates: fmt (scoped, `--check`) clean, clippy `-D warnings` workspace clean, wow-souls 573 tests (both installs set).

**WARRIOR WEAPON FAMILIES (2026-10-11; user: Souls combat for every warrior weapon, original DSR animation where available; only DSR installed, research-based approximations accepted for missing counterparts).** See `docs/WARRIOR-WEAPONS.md` for the complete source table and explicit fidelity limits. No commits. `dsr-cache weapons` now publishes 19 profiles (15 melee/grips + bow/crossbow/gun/thrown) under a new ignored generation, then updates `cache/dsr/weapons.txt`; ordinary `extract` includes this step. Melee uses original HKX/TAE, special heavy categories (Mace = a59, not a33), alias resolution, per-contact behavior rows, real chain lengths, weapon poise base/correction, weapon guard stats, original idle and block motions. Physical base damage preserves DSR weapon ratios normalized to Shortsword = 20. Runtime `weapons.rs` selects from equipped subclass; H toggles 1H/2H for small melee weapons, Y / D-pad left draws the ranged slot. `projectiles.rs` uses once-per-action source release events, finite ballistic travel, terrain and posed-body sweeps. All ranged physics/damage and gun/staff adaptations are documented estimates, not claimed original other-game assets. Ammunition consumption, whiff states and the previously parked extra attack types are still not implemented.

- **Corrected older guard error:** DS1 `ChrActionFlag` **3** means Guarding; **85 is Light Lantern Weapon**, per the DSAnimStudio template. The sword guard's active window starts at source flag 3 (0 s), not the previously assumed 0.2 s.
- **Cache/runtime safeguards:** missing selected profile disables attacks; family switches wait while an action owns the body; the old family cannot remain under a newly selected weapon. Empty hand has its own Fists behavior values. Both contacts of great-hammer heavies are retained. Full source weapon rotation (including twist) replaces the constant-axis assumption. Freshly spawned avatars also re-apply the selected family's hand/hidden-offhand state, not just later gear changes.
- **Verified:** 832 workspace tests passed: game 560 (including all 19 local caches), extractor 54, formats 52, world 158 + importer 4, wscl 4. Installed-data tests ran with `DSR_DIR` and `WOW_DATA`. Scoped fmt, workspace clippy and the game build pass. The final cache regression also checks two-handed ranged metadata and crossbow/gun barrel direction at release. `cache/warrior-*.log` hold the detailed results. Unattended DSVIEW runs for mace 2H, bow, crossbow and gun exit with `WOWSOULS_CHECK ok`; screenshots/logs are in `docs/evidence/warrior-weapons/` (also copied to the chat workspace's evidence folder). Crossbow/gun have no WoW blade markers: runtime uses a mounting-only barrel axis and source +X, with full authored weapon rotation, instead of leaving the model upright. Both now close the supporting hand.
- **Functional checks:** actual item 2055 equipped at runtime switches `straight_sword` to `mace`; no forced profile. Gun release hits a kobold for the declared estimated 32 damage and triggers its flinch. Bow/crossbow/thrown release events also verified. DSVIEW Mace 2H heavy (`a59_4300`) measured 3.1-degree worst rendered blade-axis error over complete loops; there is no claim of identical joint coordinates across different body proportions. Bow release observed at 0.668 s versus source 0.667 s. Full manual chain/stance coverage and terrain-obstruction scenarios remain untested. Ranged visuals currently use tracers, without bowstring deformation/ammunition meshes; full fidelity limits are in `WARRIOR-WEAPONS.md`.


**ABILITIES, RAGE, TRAINER AND STRONGBOX BUILT (2026-10-11; the user asked "hur gor vi med abilities? auto learn eller trainer? ... spara souls i en bank"; decided via questions: **trainer for the four**, **rage bar + four slots**, **banker in the Abbey with a 10 % tithe**; nothing committed).** `wow-world/src/abilities.rs` (pure, 4 tests): the warrior's five abilities with WoW's data (DB `npc_trainer_template` 22 build 5875, `playercreateinfo_spell`, `spell_template`): Heroic Strike (78, known from the start, 15 rage), Battle Shout (6673, level 1, 10c = 40 souls, 20 rage), Rend (772, lvl 4, 400 souls, 10 rage), Charge (100, lvl 4, 400 souls), Thunder Clap (6343, lvl 6, 400 souls, 20 rage); `usable` says whether the game does anything with it (**only Heroic Strike and Battle Shout**: Rend / Charge / Thunder Clap are listed but `NotYet`, so no souls are wasted); Parry is not gated (the button exists). Learned abilities are saved in the items save as `learn <spell>` lines (`Progress.abilities`); an old save needs nothing (the starting ability is not stored). `wow-world/src/shop.rs` gained the strongbox rules (`TITHE_PERCENT` 10, `tithe`, `deposit`, `withdraw`, `steps`: all / 100 / 1000). **The bank is in the souls save** (`souls-v1.txt`: `banked <n>` beside `carried`, `bloodstain::Banked`; an old file reads as an empty bank; a death takes only the carried souls). Game side: `src/abilities/` (`Rage`, `Buffs`, `Hotbar`, `AbilityArt`, `activate`, `card`; 7 tests incl. every icon exists in the install): rage = 0.5 per point dealt (`Hit`), 0.25 per point taken (`PlayerHurt`, capped 100), fades 2/s after 5 s of quiet, reset by `Respawned`; **keys 1-4 use a slot, Z / L3 cycle, D-pad down uses the selected one** (D-pad left is ChatGPT's ranged stance, `weapons.rs`); Heroic Strike queues `Buffs.strike` (next landed blow x1.5), Battle Shout `Buffs.shout` 120 s (x1.15); **the one hook outside the module is `hits.rs` `strike`: `damage * buffs.damage_factor()` plus a `Res<Buffs>` parameter** (the weapons / animation chat edits that file too: keep it). HUD (`abilities/ui.rs`): red rage bar under the stamina bar (the XP bar moved down, `xp.rs` `GAP` 14), the four slots at the bottom left beside the pouch (selected gold edge, dim when the rage is short). **Talk now has services** (`dialogue::Services {vendor, banker, trainer}`, `Service {Shop, Bank, Train}`; the list ends with "I want to browse your goods" / "...use the Abbey's strongbox" / "I wish to train"; one service and no quests opens it at once; the prompt says Train / Trade): `shop::BANKERS = [152]` (Brother Danil, who also sells), trainer = Llane Beshere (911, flag 16 + a list). `src/shop/` is now the three-in-one screen (`Tab::{Buy, Sell, Bank, Train}`, `Ctx`, `lines`, `attempt` -> `Done {progress, souls, banked, message}`; the card on the left shows an ability's rank, rage and words for Train rows, `Shop.focus: Focus::{Item, Spell}`). Verified live on copies of the user's saves (pictures `docs/evidence/equip-q-train.png` Battle Shout learned for 40 souls, Rend / Charge refused "cannot teach that yet", `equip-r-bank.png` 2000 souls -> 1800 banked + 200 tithe, 100 out; `WOWSOULS_RAGE=60 ... WOWSOULS_ABILITY_SCRIPT=5.0:1` makes the kobold's first hit 30 instead of 20). Gates: fmt, clippy `-D warnings` workspace, tests wow-souls 560, wow-world 158 + 4. **Open**: Rend (needs a bleed on the target; check that a creature dies of `Health` reaching 0 outside `strike`), Charge (a rush with the motor + the stun, wants an animation), Thunder Clap (an area), manual slot assignment (the four slots fill in list order), the pad's L3 / D-pad down are untested on a real pad, the trainer's weapon skills (WoW teaches daggers, two-handers... at the same NPC; not in this DB's list and not gated), more banker places, items in the strongbox, rage numbers are tuning guesses (`DEALT` / `TAKEN` / `FADE` in `abilities/mod.rs`).

**SOULS SHOPS BUILT (2026-10-11; the user: "kanske hur man anvander souls och hela det systemet", then via questions: **vendors first** and **one currency, souls**; nothing committed).** Souls are now spent at the valley's vendors, in WoW's own stock and prices. `wow-world/src/shop.rs` (pure, 5 tests): `SOULS_PER_COPPER = 4` (a kobold = 150 souls, a Cracked Leather Vest 60c = 240), `buy_cost` / `sell_value` (no price = not for sale / cannot be sold), `stock(vendors, npc)` (the DB rows in order), `buy` / `sell` with `Refusal` (not enough souls, not carried, needed by an accepted quest, ...); a refusal changes nothing. Game side `src/shop/` (`mod.rs` state + input + act, `ui.rs` panel, 6 tests): **Talk (X / pad Y) at a vendor** (`npc_flags & 4` and stock rows, `shop::sells`) opens the shop at once when he has no quest business (prompt says `Trade / Wares for souls`), else the dialogue list ends with a row "I want to browse your goods" (`dialogue::Page::Shop`, `Outcome::Shop`, `Dialogue.vendor`; `open` / `rows` got a `vendor` argument). Screen docked right like the dialogue (name, trade, `Souls N`, `[ Buy ] Sell` tabs, 9 rows with icon / name / price, result line), **the card on the left is the quest screens' card** (`rewards::refresh` reads `Shop.focus`): tooltip + comparison with what is worn. Up/Down (D-pad) choose, Left/Right / LB / RB / `[` `]` switch tab, Enter / A buys one or sells the whole sellable stack, Backspace / Esc / B leaves; a purchase publishes the pack first, then takes the souls (`Souls` is saved by `bloodstain::save` as ever); coin sound `LOOTWINDOWCOINSOUND`; walking away, a hit or death closes it (`quests::drive`: `modal` now includes the shop, Talk / Log are blocked while it is open; the tracker hides). **Quest copper became souls**: `complete()` adds `souls_for_copper(reward_money)` to `QuestGame.payout`, `quests::pay_out` credits it to `Souls` + the counter's gain popup; **an older save's `money` is converted once at the next start (the user's 25 copper = 100 souls, log line `quests: 25 copper ... became 100 souls`)**; reward lines and toasts say `N souls`, the log's status line is just the level line (`lines::money` is gone; `vocal::coins` still watches `money`, now always 0). Switches `WOWSOULS_SHOP_SCRIPT=<s>:<up|down|left|right|confirm|back>,...` (+ `WOWSOULS_SOULS_SCRIPT=<s>:give:<n>`, `WOWSOULS_QUESTS_SCRIPT=12:talk`, `WOWSOULS_WORLD=1` for the manifest NPCs in an unattended run). Verified live on copies of the user's saves at Dermot Johns (`WOWSOULS_AT=-8896.5,-115.3`): buying (1100 -> 900 -> 772 souls, items in the pack), a refused buy ("Not enough souls: it costs 144, you carry 50."), the Sell tab (stacks priced whole, `Broken Fang x23 +552`), quest 7 handed in = `souls: +100 from a quest`; pictures `docs/evidence/equip-n-shop.png`, `equip-o-sell.png`.
