**EQUIPMENT SYSTEM BUILT (2026-10-11; the user: "Fixa utrustningssystemet - ska vara samma som WoW alltså samma equipment slots som orginalet"; nothing committed).** The player now has WoW 1.12's paper doll and the cloak hangs off it instead of `WOWSOULS_CLOAK`.
- **Rules** (`wow-world/src/equipment.rs`, pure, 18 tests): `Slot` = the server's 19 equipment slots (head 0, neck, shoulders, shirt 3, chest, waist, legs, feet, wrists, hands, finger 1/2, trinket 1/2, back 14, main hand 15, off hand, ranged, tabard 18) + ammo (the character pane's 20th; the four bag slots are left out, the pack has no capacity). `slots_for(inventory_type)` (a robe = chest, a one-hander = either hand, 14 / 22 / 23 = off hand, bow / thrown / gun = ranged), `Wearer {race, class, level}`, `can_wear` (required level, class / race masks, armor and weapon types: only the warrior's table is written, plate at 40; another class is let through), `Gear::plan` (a two-hander displaces the off hand and shuts it, a one-hander in the off hand needs Dual Wield = warrior 20, `Refusal` with WoW's wording), `Gear::best_slot` (`FindEquipSlot`: first free legal slot, a ring swaps finger 1), `totals` (armor, block), `weapon_dps`, `start_kit` (vMaNGOS `playercreateinfo_item` race 1 class 1 = Worn Shortsword 25 (display 1542, the old starting blade), Worn Wooden Shield 2362, Recruit's Shirt 38 / Pants 39 / Boots 40). **No weapon-skill gating** (trainers are not in the game: a warrior may use every weapon type his class can learn).
- **Save**: `inventory::Progress` gained `gear` and `kit_given`; `items-v1.txt` (`wow-souls-items 1`) gets `kit 1` and `equip <slot key> <item>` lines, `equip` / `unequip` / `grant_start_kit` act on a clone and publish atomically like the salve. An equipped item is not counted in the pack; a swap returns the old piece (and the off hand under a two-hander). **An old save (the user's) has no `kit` line: it is dressed in the kit once at the next start** (`WOWSOULS_KIT=0` skips). The kit's five items were not in the manifest: `select.rs` now adds `HUMAN_WARRIOR_KIT` to the item set and `cache/world/northshire.txt` was re-imported (diff = 5 `item` + 5 `itemx` rows; the old file is `northshire.pre-kit.bak`).
- **What the body shows** (`equip/mod.rs`, `actor/`): `appearance(gear, items)` -> `Appearance { dress: Dress, main, off }` on the avatar (first set by `avatar::dress` through `equip::dress_new`, then `equip::apply` diffs it). `actor::Dress { worn: [u32; 10] (head, shoulder, shirt, chest, belt, pants, boots, wrist, gloves, tabard), cloak }` is read like an NPC's `NpcWorn`: geosets (`equip_geosets`, helm hide mask), body atlas with the armor regions painted (`skin::body_atlas(.., worn)` = `CharSections::composite_plan` with the real displays), head and shoulders as `WornItems` models on attachments 11 / 6 / 5, the back item as the cloth cloak (`cloak_id`). **`actor::build` also runs on a built body whose `Dress` changed** (`Or<(Without<ActorBuilt>, Changed<Dress>)>`): rig, frame and clock stay, the `DressParts` it spawned and the `WornRoot` models are despawned and redone (log `actor .. dressed again`). Main hand = `HeldItem` (+ `HeldBuilt`, hits unchanged); **empty main hand = `Unarmed`: `held::build_fist` makes a `HeldBuilt` with a 0.3 yd blade stub along the sword's blade axis (-Z of the item root; the short sword's markers measure (0,0,-0.89) / (0,0,-0.19)); verified: 3 fist hits kill the kobold like the sword (`WOWSOULS_KOBOLD_AT=-8911,-136 WOWSOULS_ATTACK=6.0,0.9`)**. Off hand = `OffHandItem` (shield: `Item\ObjectComponents\Shield`, attachment 0, left forearm; weapon / holdable: `Weapon`, attachment 2, `LeftGrip` closes the left hand); grips are released when a hand lets go (`release_grips`). Ranged weapon, ammo, neck, rings, trinkets have no visual (a stowed bow / quiver on the back is not drawn).
- **The page** (`equip/ui.rs`; `loot::Screen.page`, `loot::Drive` system set): **G / pad Select** = the equipment page (G again closes, Tab / Start = the pack, Tab closes the pack). The 1.12 character pane: left column head, neck, shoulders, back, chest, shirt, tabard, wrist; right column hands, waist, legs, feet, finger 1/2, trinket 1/2; row under: main hand, off hand, ranged, ammo; WoW's own empty-slot art `Interface/PaperDoll/UI-PaperDoll-Slot-*` (the back reuses the chest's; all of it is in the install, test `the_empty_slot_art_is_in_the_install`); the body stays visible between the panels. Arrows / WASD / D-pad move (`neighbor`), Enter / A / click opens the list for the slot (Take off first when worn, then what the pack holds that fits, usable ones first, refused ones greyed with "Level 4" / "Not skilled" ...), Enter equips, Delete takes off, Esc / B leaves the list (`Screen.back_taken` keeps the screen open), the right panel is WoW's tooltip (`tooltip`: place word + kind, damage / speed / dps, armor, block, "Requires Level N", the name in the quality colour) and the last result. On the pack page Enter / A on a wearable item (or the button, now "Equip") quick-equips (`Screen.equip_requested` -> `equip::act`). Armor / block totals sit under the doll.
- **Switches**: `WOWSOULS_GEAR_AT=<s>` (+ `_GEAR_CURSOR=<slot key>`, `_GEAR_CHOOSE=1`, `_GEAR_SCRIPT=<s>:<up|down|left|right|confirm|back|takeoff>,...` = the hands of an unattended run), `WOWSOULS_EQUIP=<ids>` (+ `_EQUIP_AT=<s>`), `WOWSOULS_UNEQUIP=<slot keys>`, `WOWSOULS_GIVE=<ids>`, `WOWSOULS_ITEMS_EQUIP_AT=<s>`, `WOWSOULS_WEAR=<slot>:<display>,...` (shows displays the valley does not hand out: helms and shoulders; 6895 = `helm_mail_a_02Silver`, 12495 = mail shoulders, 1544 = a shortsword for the left hand). Shot helper `C:/Spel/research/equip/shot.sh <name> ENV=..` -> `docs/evidence/equip-<name>.png`.
- **Verified live** (unattended; evidence `docs/evidence/equip-a-kit.png` the kit on a new character, `equip-b-armor.png` helm + shoulders + leather set + shield from the front, `equip-c-cloak.png` the cloak from behind, `equip-f-page.png` / `equip-g-flow.png` the page with its list and a scripted swap + refusal ("You must reach level 4 to use that item."), `equip-h-items.png` the pack page with Equip and the result line, `equip-i-dual.png` a sword in each hand): the kit, a rebuild of the built body, the cloak after build, the scripted page flow end to end (confirm -> down -> confirm equips the chain vest, the leather vest returns to the pack, armor 43 -> 74). Gates: fmt, clippy `-D warnings` (wow-souls, wow-world), tests **wow-souls 525, wow-world 145 + 4**, dsr-cache 53, dsr-formats 51, wscl 4. Live keys and the mouse on the page were not pressed by a person (the script drives the same `Acts`); the pad's Select mapping is untested.
- **Of the four loose ends the user quoted**: the player's equipment is solved (above); **NPCs still cannot wear a cloak** (1.12's `CreatureDisplayInfoExtra` has no cloak column); **the cloak still passes through arms and sword** (the backstop only keeps it off torso and legs); tabards / robes' skirts as cloth are still open (the same `Cloth` would do).
- **Not built / open (for the user to decide)**: armor and weapon damage do not change combat yet (WoW's armor rule would be `armor / (armor + 400 + 85 x attacker level)`, capped at 75 %; the tooltip already prints armor / dps); the DS moveset is chosen by nothing but the straight sword (a23): maces, staves, two-handers, daggers still swing the sword's moves (roadmap step 3); Show Helm / Show Cloak toggles; bag slots and a quiver; weapon skills from the warrior trainer; vendors do not sell yet; bind on equip / durability; the equipment cross (DS quick slots); the character-creation preview shows the naked body; the starting shield is on the left arm by default (unequip it on the page if it is unwanted).

**NPC interact prompt + character creation menu BUILT (2026-10-10, inline; the user: "Innan vi går vidare med intro, väder osv ...: en indikations-ikon när man kommer nära en NPC; menyn att skapa spelare och klass, vi disablar allt förutom human warrior; plus grafikuppdateringen från pkuzic/benilla-everwood_graphics"; nothing committed).**
- **Interact prompt** (`quests/prompt.rs`, panel in `quests/ui.rs`): near a person worth talking to (a quest mark over them, or any gossip / vendor / trainer flag; within `TALK_RANGE` 3.5 yd) a dark pill at the bottom centre shows a **key icon** (a rounded key cap with the letter), the verb and the name with what they offer
  (`[X]  Talk / Deputy Willem - Quest available`); also `Gather / Grape crate` at a crate a quest still wants. **The interact key is unified: `X` on the keyboard, `Y` on the pad** (the icon follows the device last touched: `PadUsed`),
  and it talks only when nothing to loot has its own prompt (`loot::Screen.prompt` is empty); `T` and the D-pad up still talk. Hidden while a dialogue, the log or the inventory is up, or the player is busy or dead.
  Verified live: picture `docs/evidence/npc-interact-prompt.png`. Not run on a real pad (the icon switch to `Y` is unit-tested only as the letter choice).
- **Character creation menu** (`menu.rs`, `character.rs`, `Phase::Menu` in `settle.rs`): after the valley has streamed in (the loading cover goes), the game stops in `Phase::Menu`: a parchment panel on the left, the character standing in the valley at the right with the camera circling him, WoW's
  valley music and ambience playing behind (the audio opens with the menu). First run (no saved character): the **creation screen** (Race, Class, Sex, Name, Create, Back); with a saved character: the title (Enter the world / New character / Quit). Race, class and sex list **everything, with everything
  but Human, Warrior and Male greyed** ("Not yet: Dwarf, Gnome, ..."; Left / Right on the row says "Only Human can be made as a race yet"); the name is typed (letters only, 2 to 12, WoW's capitalisation: `check_name`). Keys: Up / Down, Left / Right, Enter, Esc, letters + Backspace; pad: D-pad, A, B; the mouse clicks a row.
  The character is saved to `%LOCALAPPDATA%\WoWSouls\character-v1.txt` (`wschar 1`; `WOWSOULS_CHARACTER=<file>` overrides) and its name goes to the quest texts (`QuestGame::set_player_name`; at startup the name is `WOWSOULS_NAME`, else the character file, else the old save, else `Adventurer`).
  **New character with a saved one** warns once ("This replaces Jacob ...", press Create again), then **moves** the old saves (`character-v1`, `quests-v1`, `items-v1`, `souls-v1`) into a `backup-<unix time>` folder next to them (nothing is deleted) and **restarts the game** (it launches its own exe again and exits)
  so the fresh process reads fresh saves. A character file that cannot be read is never written over. `WOWSOULS_MENU=0` skips the menu, `WOWSOULS_MENU=1` shows it even in an unattended run (an unattended run ignores live keys; `WOWSOULS_MENU_SCRIPT=<s>:<press>,...` with `up down left right confirm back backspace char:<c>`
  is its hands). Verified: `docs/evidence/menu-create-character.png`; scripted create -> `menu: created Adventurer the Human Warrior`, the character file written, play starts, the prompt shows. Tests: 19 new (menu logic, character, prompt). Gates: fmt, clippy `-D warnings` workspace, tests **wow-souls 438, wow-world 126 + 4**, dsr-cache 53, dsr-formats 51, wscl 4; installs untouched.
  **Not built:** the 3D preview with look options (face, skin, hair, colours), female / other races and classes, deleting a character without making a new one, naming rules beyond letters (profanity), the pad typing a name (a pad player keeps the default name or types it on the keyboard), gamepad hover on rows.
- **GRAPHICS (2026-10-10, owned by the graphics chat; the user: "jag vill att du tar hand om grafiken ... implementera allt som finns i everwood, sen elden ring / sekiro-gräs"; nothing committed).** The game now runs the renderer of `pkuzic/benilla-everwood_graphics` (MIT / Apache-2.0, cloned at `C:\Spel\research\benilla-everwood`, commit f545081e, 210 commits past the old pin). The workspace `Cargo.toml` path dependencies point at the fork; the pinned `C:\Spel\benilla` @ 2e82d34 stays untouched as the fallback. Our code compiled against the fork unchanged (no dependency or build-script changes).
  - **Layer 1, the engine** (`benilla-world`, already ours): sky tiers, modern fog, water (refraction, caustics, planar mirror, Gerstner), foliage wind, wet surfaces, interior / fire light, moonlight. `gfx/bridge.rs` publishes `gfx::VideoConfig` to the engine resources every frame (the fork does it with cvar observers: `dynamic_interior.rs`, `monkey_gfx.rs`, `sky_quality.rs`, `zone_skybox.rs`). **`WaterDepthPlugin` and `WaterMirrorPlugin` are not in `WorldPlugins`**: the fork's app adds them, so `gfx::plugin` does (AO orders its node against `WaterDepthLabel`; without them the game panics at startup).
  - **Layer 2, the lane drivers** (the fork's `benilla-app`, ported into `crates/wow-souls/src/gfx/`, headers keep the fork's `MONKEY` / `GFX` tags, attribution in `docs/PROVENANCE.md`): `shadow_core` (the one sun rig on render layer 31), `character_shadow`, `world_shadow` (static world, cutout foliage, MCSH steps aside), `torch_shadow` (cube maps, interior and exterior, `carried.rs` stubs the held-light markers: nothing here carries a torch), `volumetric_fog` (+ lamp fog), `volumetric_light`, `ssao`, `post/{bloom,grading,sun_shafts}`. `perf.rs` (`WOWSOULS_FPS=1`), `vantage.rs` (`WOWSOULS_VANTAGE=ex,ey,ez,lx,ly,lz`: the free camera parked at a WoW-coordinate eye looking at a target; set `WOWSOULS_AT` near it). Actor and held-item parts carry `PickMesh(sub.geometry)` (that is what the character lane skins into the sun's map).
  - **Settings**: `WOWSOULS_GFX=classic|low|medium|high|ultra` (default **high**, the fork's shipped column: shadows 2048 map, AO 2, bloom 2, volumetric fog 1 + light 2, lamp fog 2, sky 2, modern fog, wind 2, planar water, farclip 777); `VideoConfig::default()` is the fork's *registered* defaults (opt-in lanes off), which the ported tests assume. Per lane: `WOWSOULS_SHADOWS=0`, `_AO`, `_BLOOM`, `_VOLFOG`, `_VOLLIGHT`, `_SKY`, `_WIND`, `_WIND_STRENGTH`, `_FARCLIP`, `_GRADING=0|1`, `_SHAFTS=0|1`, `_ZONESKY=0|1`. The engine's no-server clock is a constant noon: **`WOW_CLOCK=<minute 0..1439>`** pins the hour (shots use it). Colour grading: the fork's own project data (`MonkeyZoneGrade.dbc` + five LUT strips, MIT / Apache-2.0) is compiled in from `gfx/data/` (nothing is written into the WoW `Data` folder); Elwynn (and so Northshire, whose area 9 is a child of 12) gets `ElwynnWarmGreen` at strength 0.34.
  - **Verified live** (unattended, `C:/Spel/research/gfx/shot.sh <name> ENV=..`, pictures `docs/evidence/gfx-*.png`): High vs `classic` on the same view: tree / cart / fence / player shadows that move with the hour, dusk haze, smooth sky, interiors lit by their own fixtures (dark Abbey nave, candle pools), the Elwynn river with planar reflections and depth tint, lamp bloom at night. Per-lane off/on pixel diffs on the overlook (run-to-run noise 0.03): shadows 9.4, volumetric fog 8.3, AO 3.7, volumetric light 2.7, grading 2.6, sky 2.1; bloom / sun shafts / zone skies / wind read as wind-phase noise (~1.9) in that scene (they need lamps, a low sun, painted-sky data: the Elwynn zone has no painted sky in 1.12 data, the fork's optional builder only adds Burning Steppes / Blasted Lands / Hyjal from a modern install). `WOWSOULS_FPS=1`: High held 60 fps at 1080p in the unattended window (vsync-bound; the unattended window is throttled, so this is a floor, not a benchmark). Gates: fmt, clippy `-D warnings` workspace, tests **wow-souls 512** (49 gfx tests, 12 of daycycle / weather, 13 of cloak), wow-world 126 + 4, dsr-cache 53, dsr-formats 51, wscl 4.
  - **Grass, Elden Ring / Sekiro level (2026-10-10, built and verified live; the user to judge).** The ground cover is the engine's own (`benilla_world::clutter`: per MCNK chunk `frillDensity` cells x the `GroundEffectTexture` density; the tufts are 1.12's grass models). Three knobs, set by the rung in `gfx/config.rs` and bridged to `ClutterConfig` (`gfx/bridge.rs`): **density** (a multiple of the 16 cells per chunk: 2 = the engine default, 16 = its ceiling), **fade** (draw distance, the reference's 70 yd), **height** (a multiple of every tuft's size). **High = 16 / 100 yd / 1.3**, Ultra = 16 / 130 / 1.3, Medium = 6 / 80 / 1.1, Low and Classic = the engine's 2 / 70 / 1.0. Env: `WOWSOULS_GRASS=<0..16>`, `_GRASS_FADE=<10..300>`, `_GRASS_HEIGHT=<0.1..4>`. The engine had no height knob and no way to keep monsters readable, so the **fork carries a local patch** (branch `wow-souls` of `C:\Spel\research\benilla-everwood`, uncommitted working tree; the diff is saved as `docs/patches/benilla-everwood-wow-souls-grass.patch`, re-apply it with `git apply` after a fresh clone): `ClutterConfig::height` (env `WOW_CLUTTER_HEIGHT`; a change re-cuts the built meshes), `CLUTTER_BUILDS_PER_FRAME` 8 -> 3 (a chunk at frill 256 is eight times the work; eight in one frame hitched to 33 ms, three keep it at 22-27 ms), and in `wind_hook.wgsl` a **trample**: each tuft's height in eighths of a yard rides `UV_B.x` above the 0..1 bend fraction (`2 * code + fraction`), and tufts near the player or one of the seven nearest units (the engine's existing benders, `WorldUnit` entities, corpses too) are pressed down. **The user found the first version far too strong when walking (2026-10-11, "För kraftig nedgång av gräset när man går"), so it is now subtle and different for the two kinds**: the player (bender 0) loses at most 22 % of the height, inside 0.5 x its radius, fading to nothing at 1.7 x (about 3 yd); every other unit loses up to 50 %, inside 0.8 x its radius, fading out at 2.2 x, so a kobold or wolf still stands in a lowered patch and stays readable. Constants `TRAMPLE_PLAYER_* / TRAMPLE_UNIT_*` (inner, outer, loss) at the top of `wind_hook.wgsl`; picture `docs/evidence/gfx-n1-orbit-subtle.png` (play camera), `gfx-n2-mon-subtle.png` (monsters). Foliage wind must be on (Classic has it off: no trample there). Pictures: `docs/evidence/gfx-h2-grass{2,16}.png`, `gfx-j1-mon-grass16.png` (monsters in the lowered patches), `gfx-j2-orbit-grass16.png` (the play camera), `gfx-k3-*.png` (fade / height variants). Cost: still 60 fps at 3840x2160 with the High rung (vsync-bound; the GPU is not the limit); worst frame while running across the meadow 22-27 ms (at density 2: 21-25). **Not done:** blade variety beyond 1.12's models (the heights and colours are the vanilla ones x the knobs), grass casting shadows, a per-area density (roads and camps keep the DBC's `no_effect_doodad` mask), monsters farther than the seven nearest units are not cleared, and no grass LOD beyond the fade.
  - **Day and night, weather (2026-10-11, built and verified live; the user to judge: "Du kan sedan hoppa på väder och dag/natt").** Two new modules, the part a server would play, feeding the engine that already draws it all. **`daycycle.rs`**: the game clock, published as `benilla_world::lighting::WorldTime` every frame (`live: true`, so the sun and moon, the moon's phase on the unwrapped day count, stars, the `Light.dbc` colours, the zone music's day / night tracks and the night ambience bed all follow). A game day lasts `WOWSOULS_DAY_MINUTES` real minutes (default **24**, WoW's one game hour a real minute; `0` freezes); a first run starts at 08:00 and later runs resume (`%LOCALAPPDATA%/WoWSouls/clock-v1.txt`, `WOWSOULS_CLOCK_SAVE=<file>` overrides and also enables it in an unattended run; written every 5 s when changed; **a new character starts a new clock**: `clock-v1.txt` is in the archive list of `menu.rs`); the clock only runs in `Phase::Playing`. `WOWSOULS_HOUR=<h | h:mm>` sets the start (ignoring the save); an unattended run neither reads nor writes a save and stands at 12:00 unless `WOWSOULS_HOUR` / `WOWSOULS_DAY_MINUTES` are set, so every older scripted check keeps its noon; `WOW_CLOCK=<minute>` (the engine's lever) still pins the hour and wins. **`weather.rs`**: sends the engine `WeatherMessage`s like `SMSG_WEATHER` (the engine ramps a change over ~10 s: overcast, fog, rain with its mist, wet and dark surfaces, puddles and ripples, sheltered spots stay dry). Rolls follow MaNGOS's `Weather::ReGenerate`: every 5 real minutes of play (first after 2.5), 25 % nothing happens, a fine sky turns to rain by the zone's chance for the real calendar's season (**Elwynn Forest from the vMaNGOS `game_weather` table: 20 % spring, 15 % summer, 20 % fall and winter, no snow or storm**; read from a copy with a throwaway mysqld, `zone 12`), rain clears 55 % of rolls or changes grade. Sound: the server's `Weather::GetSound` kit (8533-8535 rain by grade < 0.4 / < 0.7 / above, 8536-8538 snow, 8556-8558 sand; 8535 is `Sound/Ambience/Weather/RainHeavyLoop.wav`) replaces the zone ambience bed while the player is outdoors, the area's own bed returns indoors (`zone.rs` does the 5 s crossfade; `CameraInteriorClaim` says indoors). `WOWSOULS_WEATHER=<fine | rain | snow | sand>[,<grade>]` fixes it from the moment the player is in the world, `random` rolls it; unattended runs have clear skies unless it is set; the engine's own `WOW_WEATHER` script also works. **Nights are playable on High now**: the fork's `nightGain 0.45` / `moonLight 1` left the valley near black from 22:00, so High and Ultra use **0.85 / 2.0** (Medium, Low and Classic keep the fork's; `WOWSOULS_NIGHT_GAIN` 0.2-1.5, `WOWSOULS_MOONLIGHT` 0-2 tune it): dark and moonlit, the path and the grass read. Pictures: `docs/evidence/gfx-t-<hour>.png` (6, 18, 20, 22, 0, 3 at the old nights), `gfx-u-n*` (night brightness variants), `gfx-w1-rain.png`, `gfx-x1-rain-dusk.png`. Tests: 12 new (`daycycle::`, `weather::`). **Not built:** a bonfire / rest that skips time, weather that follows the area (only Elwynn's table), thunder (1.12 has none), snow / sand in the valley (the chance is 0 there), weather and clock in the HUD, lightning-lit nights, the 1.12 night sky's moon phases tuned by us (the engine's own).
  - **Cloaks as cloth (2026-10-11, a test the user asked for: "testa en cloak ... så det kan följa blåsten"; built and verified on one cloak).** 1.12's cloak is not painted: it is a real cape geoset (group 15, styles 1502-1506 for `ItemDisplayInfo.GeosetGroup[0]` 1-5, with a small AlphaTest collar piece each), but **skinned rigidly to the spine** (probe of `HumanMale.m2`: bones 20-23 and the shoulder / upper-arm bones, no cape bones of its own), so it hugs the back and never moves. `src/cloak.rs` replaces the main cape mesh (the Object-slot batch with 20+ vertices; the collar stays rigid) with **cloth**: the authored mesh and its mirrored UVs, subdivided twice (39 vertices -> ~590), a verlet particle per node (f64, so a body 9 000 yd from the origin keeps its millimetres), gravity, an air term `a = k (n . (wind - v)) n` from the engine's own `WindField` (the grass and the trees' wind; `FoliageWind` 0 = no wind, `FoliageWindStrength` scales it), a spring back to the node's **skinned** position (stiff at the shoulders, loose at the hem), a limit on how far a node may leave it (0.55 yd at the hem), edge constraints, a **backstop** (a node can never end up behind the skinned surface, i.e. inside the body) and the ground. The wearer's own motion is the other wind: running streams the hem out behind and to the side. The targets are the authored vertices skinned through `RigPalettes::rig_rows` + `slot_origin` (the same palette the GPU skins with); the part is a child of the model frame with an unskinned mesh whose positions are written in its own space each frame (`PostUpdate`, after `BillboardPlace`), and a child caster shares the mesh on the shadow layer (`gfx::shadow_core::spawn_solid_caster`). `Cloak(<ItemDisplayInfo id>)` on an actor is the hook (an actor build reads it: cape geoset via `EquipGeosets::cloak`, cape texture `Item/ObjectComponents/Cape/<ModelTextureLeft>.blp` on the Object-slot batches, the cloth part); **`WOWSOULS_CLOAK=<id>` puts one on the player** (the player has no equipment system yet; 15050 = `Cape_Mage_A_01Brown`, style 1, the long cape; ids with `Icon` `INV_Misc_Cape_*` in `ItemDisplayInfo` are cloaks, 683 have a texture; 15029 / 15032 are other styles), `WOWSOULS_CLOTH=0` keeps the rigid 1.12 cape, `WOWSOULS_CLOAK_WIND=<0..4>` is the air gain (default 1). Verified: `docs/evidence/gfx-cloak-run.mp4` (8 s of running with the camera orbiting, cropped to the player), `gfx-cloak-run-frames.png` / `gfx-cloak-stand-frames.png` (frames cut from the 60 Hz recorder, `WOWSOULS_RECORD`), `gfx-k1-cloak-{rigid,cloth}.png`; 13 unit tests in `cloak::` (subdivision, hang, outward normals, still air stays within 0.12 yd of the skinned shape, wind from behind billows the hem, a wind toward the body never enters it, edges keep their length, the hem lags a running body by at most its slack, a teleport resets, the ground holds, NaN / zero / huge steps, f64 skinning). **Not built:** a cloak on NPCs (1.12's `CreatureDisplayInfoExtra` has no cloak slot: 10 equipment columns, head to tabard; a cloaked NPC would need its own table), the equipment / inventory side (a worn cloak item -> `Cloak`), cloak vs. arms and sword collision (the backstop only keeps it off the torso and legs), cloth for other things (tabards, robes' skirts: the same `Cloth` would do), and the cape in the character-creation preview.
  - **Not built / open:** carried torch lights, spell lights (`SpellLight` lives in the fork's `entities/spell_fx`), the options screen (the fork's `benilla-ui` is not ours; settings are env vars), a Souls-style grade of our own (the 0.34 Elwynn strip is the fork's taste), the fork's optional painted skies. **Next, if the user wants it:** a Souls grade of our own, more grass variety, a bonfire that rests time.
  - The throwaway trial build `C:\Spel\research\gfx-trial` (~10 GB, a copy of the source with its own target dir) is deletable. WarcraftXL credit (iThorgrim) is REQUIRED for the grading, clouds, wind and water files at release (`THIRD-PARTY.md` of the fork lists them; copy it into the package).

**RELEASE PLAN: decisions the user took on 2026-10-10 (the game is to be published on the user's own website, players supply their own game installs, like Dusklight does with Zelda: Twilight Princess).**
The game ships code and our own data only; **nothing from the WoW or Dark Souls Remastered installs is ever in the package or the repo** (`/cache` and `/WoW` are gitignored; the DSR cache is generated on the player's machine).
- **Install discovery MUST include a manual option** (the user's explicit requirement). To build, as one first-run setup (a window or a console prompt) plus a `config` file in `%LOCALAPPDATA%\WoWSouls\config.toml`:
  1. *WoW 1.12.1*: environment override (`WOW_DATA`, as the tests use), then the config, then a scan of the usual places (a few Program Files / game-folder spots, the registry's Blizzard keys if any), and **always a manual "Browse..." / typed path** that wins over any guess and is validated (the `Data` folder with the MPQ chain; client build 5875, enUS) with a plain error that names what is wrong;
  2. *Dark Souls Remastered*: environment override (`DSR_DIR`), the config, **automatic Steam detection** (Steam's `libraryfolders.vdf`, app id 570940), and the same **manual path** with validation (the `DATA` archives that `wow-souls-dsr` reads);
  3. the DSR-derived cache (`cache/dsr`, the HUD art, the retargeted clips) is built on the first start with a progress display, into `%LOCALAPPDATA%\WoWSouls\cache` (not the working directory), and rebuilt when the install or the extractor version changes; a clear message and an exit (never a crash) when an install is missing or the wrong version;
  4. every `C:\Spel\...` constant and the `WoW` junction go away: `DEFAULT_DSR` / `DEFAULT_WOW` in `crates/dsr-cache/src/main.rs`, the relative `cache/...` paths (`world::MANIFEST`, `loot-check` saves, `cache/dsr`, `cache/world`), the voices' default root (`quests/voice.rs`, `DEFAULT_ROOT`).
  Until this is built, the `C:\Spel\...` defaults and the environment variables are the developer's setup; **do not add any new hardcoded install path**: new code asks a single `paths` module (to create with this task) or reads the existing env var.
- **Quest data: ship it as built** ("bygg den så som vi har gjort det"): `cache/world/northshire.txt` (the vMaNGOS-derived manifest with WoW's quest texts, spawns, loot) becomes part of the package (move it out of the gitignored `/cache` into a tracked `data/` folder when the release step is built; the importer `wow-souls-world import` and its vMaNGOS source stay the developer's tool). The user is aware the texts are Blizzard's; their call, noted.
- **Spoken Player voices are part of the package for now** ("lägg till det som en del av paketet, för nu"): the four `SpokenQuestsAudio*` packs (12 963 `.ogg`, 1.36 GB unpacked, MIT, by rustykey) are bundled with the release; keep `THIRD_PARTY.md`-style credit and the MIT licence text with them. Revisit if the size is a problem (the Gossip pack, 4 128 files, is not used by the game yet and could be left out).
- **Audio comes from the player's own WoW client by streaming** (already how it works: every `Sound\...` file is read through the MPQ chain on first use; nothing is copied). Music needs a streaming decoder (below).

**Voices and audio (2026-10-10, inline; the user: "Ändra rösterna till ... spoken-player så ta bort det andra vi la in", then "lägg till lvl upp sfx + vfx från wow och lägg in alla ljudpaket från wow också - eller att man streamar därifrån"; nothing committed).**
- **Quest voices are now Spoken Player** (https://www.curseforge.com/wow/addons/spoken-player, by rustykey, MIT, source github.com/rusty-key/spoken-wow; the successor of VoiceOver Redux with newly generated English voices). Downloaded on the user's
  explicit yes (asked with file name, source and size first): `SpokenQuestsAudioAll-2.2.1.zip`, 1 400 731 067 bytes (exactly the size announced), from `github.com/rusty-key/spoken-wow/releases/download/quests-audio/v2.2.1/`, in
  `C:\Spel\research\spoken\pack\`, extracted (no unsafe paths, checked first) to `C:\Spel\research\spoken\x`: four packs `SpokenQuestsAudioAlliance` (2 848 files) / `Horde` (2 389) / `Shared` (3 662) / `Gossip` (4 128), 12 963 **`.ogg`**
  (Vorbis) in all, `generated\sounds\quests\<quest id>-accept.ogg` / `-complete.ogg` (the same keys as VoiceOver), Lua lookup tables beside them (not used). All 9 valley quests we checked (783, 7, 15, 21, 33, 5261, 3100, 18, 6) have both
  lines in the Alliance pack. `quests/voice.rs` now searches the Alliance, then Shared, then Horde quest folders (first hit wins) under `WOWSOULS_SPOKEN=<root>` (`0` = off, default `C:\Spel\research\spoken\x`); the Gossip pack (NPC greetings,
  keyed by text hashes / broadcast text ids) is not used yet. symphonia got the `ogg` + `vorbis` features (two crates fetched from crates.io on the same yes: `symphonia-format-ogg`, `symphonia-codec-vorbis`, 0.6.1; `mp3` stays for WoW's music).
  Verified: unit tests incl. a real `.ogg` decoding to 31.7 s of speech; live (`WOWSOULS_AUDIO=null`): `audio: voice 783 accept: ...Alliance\...\783-accept.ogg (31.7 s, gain 0.59)`. **Not heard on the real device** (the user hears it first;
  loudness `VOICE_SCALE` 1.4 over the master `WOWSOULS_VOLUME`).
- **VoiceOver removed** (the user's choice: "Papperskorgen efter test"): its code is gone, `C:\Spel\research\voiceover` (2.2 GB: the addon and the 1.17 GB pack) was moved to the **Recycle Bin** after the Spoken voices were verified (not
  emptied: restorable until the user empties it); the old HANDOVER entries now say "replaced".
- **Level-up, WoW's own** (`level.rs`, `loot/vfx.rs`): on a rise of the player's level (quest or kill XP; loading a save at some level is not a rise, `level::is_rise`) the client's `"HARDCODED Unit Level Up"` effect
  (`Spells\LevelUp\LevelUp.m2`, id 21, 5 native emitters, a 1.867 s clip that plays once, the same model benilla spawns) flashes on the player's feet and `Sound\interface\LevelUp.wav` plays. `loot::vfx` was generalised for it
  (`Sparkle { attach, once, die_at }`, `spawn_level_up`; the loot sparkles still ride attachment 19 and loop). Picture `docs/evidence/g4c-levelup-flash.png`: the golden flash round the player, the toast `You have reached level 2`.
- **WoW's interface sounds, streamed from the install**: `Audio::play_path(chain path, scale, what)` plays any file of the install's archives (decoded on first use, cached): quest accept `iQuestActivate`, objective progress
  `iQuestUpdate`, hand-in `iQuestComplete`, log open / close `iQuestLogOpenA` / `CloseA` (`Toasts.sounds` queue, `quests::play_chimes`), item pickup `UiLootPickUpItem` (`loot/ui.rs`), level-up above. Checked with `WOWSOULS_AUDIO=null`
  (`audio: quest chime: Sound\interface\iQuestActivate.wav (4.0 s ...)`). Nothing is copied: the game reads the user's own `sound.MPQ` etc. through the same patch chain as everything else.
- **"Alla ljudpaket från WoW" (read as: all of WoW's own game audio, streamed from the install; the user then asked to "fortsätt med resten av ljudet så vi får in hela stämningen") BUILT, 6 of 8 layers** (2026-10-10, inline; rules ported
  from benilla-app's `sound/` and written down in `docs/PROVENANCE.md`; the tables are benilla-formats'). Nothing is copied from the client: every file is read through the patch chain on first use.
  1. **Footsteps** (`foot.rs`): a model event scanner (`AnimSound`: the M2 clip's event keys crossed since the last frame, `(prev, cur]` with the loop wrap) and `$FSD` -> `TerrainType` under the foot (`WorldPoint::terrain_type`) x the creature's
     `FootstepID` -> `FootstepTerrainLookup` -> the `SoundEntries` kit (wading: the splash kit, deeper than 1.5 yd: silent); the player's own steps play at the player (not from the camera), others positioned, at most 4 per frame, only within
     45 yd. Live: walking west from the Abbey `kit 564 ...mFootMediumLargeStone*.wav` -> `560 ...Dirt*` -> `562 ...Grass*`, three steps a second at a run. No steps while an action owns the body (a roll, a swing).
  2. **Zone music + ambience** (`zone.rs`, `mixer.rs`, `stream.rs`): the area under the player -> `AreaTable` -> `ZoneMusic` / `SoundAmbience` (parents inherited), a day and a night kit each; a zone change starts its track at once and fades the old
     one out over 4 s, a track that ends is followed by a silence in the row's range then the next, the ambience bed loops and crossfades over 5 s; day is 05:30-21:00 on `GameClock` (benilla_world, the lighting clock; **the engine's no-server clock was a constant noon, so the music was always the day kit; since 2026-10-10 the graphics chat's `daycycle.rs` drives the day / night cycle and `zone.rs` follows it, to be listened for: the night kits and the dusk change are not heard yet**). The mixer got looped voices, a live `Level` (a ramp across each buffer, no clicks) and **streamed voices** (`StreamVoice`: a decoder thread reads the mp3 from the archive, decodes with
     symphonia, resamples with a stateful linear resampler and feeds 4096-frame chunks through a bounded channel, so a 4-minute track is never held in memory). `Audio` gained `zone_choice`, `pick_file`, `stream_path`, `loop_path`.
     Live (`WOWSOULS_AUDIO=null`): `zone: Northshire Valley (music row 1, ambience row 35, day)`, `Sound\Music\ZoneMusic\Forest\DayForest02.mp3` streams, `Sound\Ambience\ZoneAmbience\ForestNormalDay.wav` loops (60 s); an install test
     decodes three seconds of that mp3 at 48 kHz. `WOWSOULS_MUSIC` / `WOWSOULS_AMBIENCE` (0..1, default 1) set their loudness under the master, `0` turns one off. **Not built:** the zone intro fanfares, interiors (the Abbey uses its zone's
     music), weather / ghost / underwater beds.
  3. **Player voice** (`vocal.rs`): the human male's `CreatureSoundData` row (display 49): a grunt at the start of a swing (70 %), a wound cry per unblocked blow (60 %, the critical one from 30 damage), the death cry (live:
     `HumanMaleMainAttackA/B/C.wav`).
  4. **NPC hello / goodbye** when a dialogue opens / closes (`NPCSounds` by display, positioned at the NPC; live: `HumanMaleStandardNPCGreetings06.wav`, `...Farewell03.wav`).
  5. **Idle creature sounds**: a hostile creature that is idle and within 35 yd grunts or growls now and then (its `Fidget` kits, 14-34 s apart, first one after a few seconds); not run live in this session (no run had one idle in range).
  6. **Coin** when the purse changes (`LOOTWINDOWCOINSOUND`), **bag** open / close with the inventory (`PickUpBag` / `PutDownBag`), plus the quest chimes, item pickup and level-up above. Not run live (no coin or bag in a scripted run).
  Not done: model `$SND` events (doors, torches, bows: the creature models carry few), jump / land vocals, spell sounds (they come with the abilities), the Spoken **Gossip** pack for NPC greetings (4 128 files, unused), loot-window sounds,
  footfall decals / dust, armor foley. **If the user meant the Spoken Zones / Spoken Books audio packs by "ljudpaket", say so**: they are separate downloads (zones ~470 MB, books ~450 MB per language on the releases page; ask first).
  Gates after all this: fmt, clippy `-D warnings` workspace, tests **wow-souls 419, wow-world 126 + 4**, dsr-cache 53, dsr-formats 51, wscl 4 (both installs set); no install file changed; benilla clean. **Not heard on the real device**
  (everything was checked with `WOWSOULS_AUDIO=null` logs and unit / install tests): the levels (master 0.6, footsteps 0.8, music and ambience 1.0 x their kit volumes, voices 0.9) are first guesses for the user's ears.

**UI / controls pass after the user's play-test (2026-10-10, inline; nothing committed). NEXT: the abilities spec + build the user approved ("Ditt förslag låter bra, vi testar det": Charge + Battle Shout only, D-pad left / down direct slots (keys `1` / `2`), talk and loot merged on Y, quest log on Select; see the chat summary below), then G3 leftovers.**
The user's findings and what was done:
- **The `?` over a quest ender was never drawn** ("Visar inte ?"; the yellow `!` was fine). Cause: `TalkToMeQuestionMark.m2` (and the grey / blue `?` models) are all-billboard models, and `actor::build` skipped billboard
  batches ("1 billboard skipped", 0 parts). Now `actor/mod.rs` builds a billboard batch as a world-root `benilla_world::billboard::BillboardCard::following(info, root)` (the way benilla's `quest_markers` and
  `dress::spawn_billboard_part` place it: it turns to the camera every frame, follows the actor, the marker's own anim-0 bob armed at build; the log line is now `built N parts (M billboard cards)`). Verified: a
  run at McBride with 783 complete shows the yellow `?` over his head (`docs/evidence/ui-quest-mark-question.png`), `mark Ready over NPC 197`; hand-in by script: `handed in 783: xp 40`, the toast `Quest handed in: A Threat Within / 40 experience
  gained`, the chain offers Kobold Camp Cleanup at once (`docs/evidence/ui-handin-chain-offer.png`). The grey `?` (quest in progress) uses the same path.
- **The salve pouch and its two help lines overlapped the quest dialogue**: the pouch hides while a quest screen is up (`loot/ui.rs`), and the quest panels were re-laid for any window shape: the **dialogue docks to the
  right edge** (`right 36, top 64, width 520, max height 590`), the log and the toasts are centred with `left: 50 %` and a negative margin instead of fixed pixels (they sat left of centre on the user's ultra-wide window);
  the footers now name the pad (`Enter / A  Accept   Backspace / B  Decline`, `Up/Down  Choose`, log: `Delete  Abandon (twice)`).
- **The player can run and look around while talking.** `loot::Screen::locked()` is now the inventory only (movement, camera, lock-on); a quest screen sets `modal`, which holds actions only
  (`Screen::blocks_actions(now)`: attacks, evade, guard, jump; used by `combat::act`, which no longer zeroes the move intent for a quest screen, and `controller::read_intent` for the jump). **A no longer jumps when it
  confirms** (and B no longer rolls when it goes back): `Screen.swallow_until` keeps the press the screen took for 0.25 s real, whichever order the systems run in. Because WASD / the stick now belong to the player, a screen takes
  the arrows, the D-pad up / down, Enter / A, Backspace / B and Delete (abandon, was `D`). A dialogue still closes when the player walks 7 yd away, is hurt, dies or does a body action. Tested as unit tests only
  (an unattended run ignores live keys, so the A press itself was not run on a real pad); live: the Willem dialogue stays open while the body runs (`Run 7.0 yd/s`, `docs/evidence/ui-dialogue-docked-running.png`).
- **Area loot ("group loot")**: one corpse loot (X / Y) takes **every dead creature's loot within `AREA_LOOT_RADIUS` 10 yd of the player** in one animation, equal items added together (`loot::merged`, `area_corpses`, the prompt reads
  `X / Y   Collect all (N)`, the log `loot: area loot took N corpses`); a living creature, a far corpse and chests are left alone. Unit-tested with the loot fixture (two corpses in range, one far, one alive).
- Gates after all this: fmt, clippy `-D warnings` workspace, tests **wow-souls 397, wow-world 126 + 4**, dsr-cache 53, dsr-formats 51, wscl 4 (both installs set); no install file changed since 07:00; benilla clean.
  Peers' files touched (small): `loot/mod.rs` (`Screen`, area loot), `loot/ui.rs` (pouch visibility), `loot/vfx.rs` / `loot/mod.rs` visibility (G4b).
- **Still open from the user's UI remark ("vi har mycket yta åt kanterna vi kan använda också")**: only the quest panels were re-laid. The HUD layout (1280 x 720 anchored left / right / centre) already uses the edges; the tracker
  (top right, left-aligned text), the pouch (left 5 %, bottom 19 %), the toasts and the help lines (bottom left) are fixed-offset and could use the side space better on a wide window: ask the user what they want moved.

**G4a + G4b BUILT (2026-10-10, inline by the planner; the user: "Kör på, sen får vi diskutera talangträd och abilities"; nothing committed). NEXT: the user's talent-tree / abilities discussion (no building before they decide), then G3 leftovers / the item phase.**
**G4b (souls lost at death) is built, see the entry right after this paragraph's G4a bullets; G4a first:**
Decisions (the user, via questions): **Hybrid**: WoW XP is the level currency (quest XP + kill XP, one total in `QuestGame.xp`), souls stay the Dark Souls currency; **death costs souls**
(the DS bloodstain rule). Spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-10-xp-levels-souls-design.md` (G4a + G4b + non-goals), plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-10-g4a-kill-xp-and-levels.md`.
- **Kill XP** (`wow_world::quest::xp`): vMaNGOS's own `MaNGOS::XP` formula, read from `src/game/Formulas.h` of github.com/vmangos/core: `5 x level + 45`, +5 % per creature level above (cap 4), `(ZD + mob - player) / ZD`
  below, 0 at or under the gray level, elite x2, rounded half to even (done in integers so a tie is the same everywhere); `gray_level`, `zero_difference`, `kill_xp`, `MobXp { level_min, level_max, elite, pays }`
  (`MobXp::of(&Creature)`: elite = rank 1-3, pays = type != 8, so **critters pay nothing**; a spawn's level = `level_min + guid % range`, an old hand-placed creature uses `level_min`; `payout`). The valley has
  no elite (Garrick is rank 0, level 5). `QuestGame` has `mobs: HashMap<entry, MobXp>` and `kill_reward(entry, guid) -> Option<(xp, level, leveled)>`; `credit_kills` calls it (`entry_of` now also returns the spawn guid), toasts
  `N experience gained` and `You have reached level N`, logs `quests: kill xp +N (entry E, level L; total T, you level P)`. Numbers: a level-1 kill of an equal-level creature is 50 XP, 400 XP is level 2 (8 kills);
  a Defias Thug (3-4) at level 1 pays 55 / 58, a level-3 Kobold Laborer at level 1 pays 55, a Young Wolf at level 2 pays 44; the level gate (quest 18 and the other MinLevel-2 quests) now opens by kills alone (8 thugs).
- **The level's effect** (`crates/wow-souls/src/level.rs`, plugin in `boot.rs`): `vitals(level)` = HP `100 + 10 x (level - 1)`, stamina `100 + 5 x (level - 1)` (level 5: 140 / 120; constants at the top of the file,
  tuning values); `follow(level, applied, hp, st)` (pure: the first application fills, later the current values move by the same difference as the maxima, no full heal, a dead player stays dead) and the Update system
  `follow_level` (applies when `QuestGame.player.level` changes, once the avatar exists, so a save at level 3 starts at level 3's maxima). The respawn restores `max`; the HUD bar length is max x 3 layout px, so the bars grow by themselves.
  **Enemy damage and HP are not scaled by the player's level.**
- **Instrument:** `WOWSOULS_QUESTS_SCRIPT` step `xp:<n>` (adds XP as a reward would, toasts, saves).
- **Verified:** unit tests (xp 11 new in wow-world, level 5 + quests 4 new in the game; install tests ran against the real manifest); gates fmt, clippy `-D warnings` workspace, tests **wow-souls 386, wow-world 126 + 4**, dsr-cache 53, dsr-formats 51, wscl 4
  (both installs set); live unattended: `WOWSOULS_WORLD=0 WOWSOULS_KOBOLD_AT=-8911,-136 WOWSOULS_ATTACK=3.0,0.9 WOWSOULS_QUESTS_SCRIPT=1:xp:395 WOWSOULS_CHECK=14 WOWSOULS_SHOT=... WOWSOULS_SHOT_AT=11 WOWSOULS_AUDIO=null`:
  `level: 1 -> health 100, stamina 100`, script +395, the kobold dies, `kill xp +55 (entry 80, level 3; total 450, you level 2)`, `level: 2 -> health 110, stamina 105`, exit 0; picture `docs/evidence/g4a-levelup.png` (the HP bar
  ends at 633 px = 218 + 110 x 3 x 1.25, a level-1 bar would end at 593). No install file changed since the session start; benilla clean. **For the user's eyes:** the toasts at the bottom (not in the picture, they had faded), the
  bar lengths, whether 55 XP per early kill (about 8 kills to level 2, plus quests) feels right: it is WoW's own number, so the valley now levels the player to 4-5 by the time its quests are done.
  **Not built:** WoW's `LevelUp` sound (plan Task 2 left it as optional; the sfx path decodes WAV, so it is a small step if the file is in the MPQ), kill XP for the old groups beyond the species' entry lookup (they use the
  entry's `level_min`), group / rested XP.
- **G4b BUILT (same day, inline; spec section "G4b"): dying loses the carried souls to a bloodstain** (`crates/wow-souls/src/bloodstain.rs`, plugin in `boot.rs`; the peers' `loot/mod.rs` `mod vfx` and `vfx::Art` / `vfx::spawn`
  went from private / `pub(super)` to `pub(crate)`, nothing else of theirs changed). Pure rules (unit-tested): `die(carried, stain, at)` (carried souls -> a `Stain { at, souls }`, count to 0; **an older stain is lost, but only when at
  least 1 soul is carried: with 0 the old stain stays**), `near(stain, feet)` (within `RECOVER_RANGE` 1.5 yd along the ground and `RECOVER_HEIGHT` 2.5 yd up or down), `recover`, `to_text` / `from_text` (`wssouls 1`, `carried <n>`,
  optional `stain <x> <y> <z> <souls>` in Bevy coordinates; strict, an error names the line). Game side: `Bloodstain` resource; `drop_on_death` (the frame `hurt::Death.at` turns `Some`: the stain goes under the body, ground-snapped with
  `settle::snap_to_ground`), `pick_up` (alive, no death pending, on the stain: souls back, the counter shows the gain, toast `Souls recovered: N`), `sync_mark` (one `StainMark` entity per stain: a dark-red translucent disc
  (`PATCH_RADIUS` 0.8 yd, `PATCH_LIFT` 0.08) with WoW's own loot sparkles `LootFX` (`loot::vfx::spawn`) as a child), `save` (atomic write on any change of `Souls` or the stain; a save that cannot be read blocks writing over it).
  Save file `%LOCALAPPDATA%\WoWSouls\souls-v1.txt` (`WOWSOULS_SOULS_SAVE=<file>` overrides; unattended runs write `cache/loot-check/souls-<pid>.txt`); **`Souls` was not saved before, now it is** (carried souls survive a restart).
  F6 (creature reset) leaves souls and the stain alone; XP, level, quests and items are untouched by death. Instrument `WOWSOULS_SOULS_SCRIPT=<s>:give:<n>,<s>:die` (real seconds since process start; **a step whose time has
  already passed when the Playing phase begins (about 3.5 s after start) fires in the first Playing frame, so use times above 4 s**).
  **Verified live (unattended, `WOWSOULS_CHECK` ok):** `WOWSOULS_WORLD=0 WOWSOULS_DRIVE=-1,0 WOWSOULS_DRIVE_FROM=4.0,22 WOWSOULS_SOULS_SCRIPT=5:give:300,7.5:die WOWSOULS_CHECK=22`: `script gave 300`, `died carrying 300: stain at
  113.3 82.1 8913.0` (22.7 yd west of the spawn), the counter reads 0 while dead, `respawns at the spawn point` 4.5 s later, the run west `recovered 300 (300 carried)` 3 s after that, the save ends `carried 300` with no stain; a death
  with the stain right at the spawn point is recovered on respawn the same instant (Dark Souls does the same with a bonfire on the stain); a save with `carried 12` and a stain loads (`souls: save ... carried 12, stain none/450
  souls...`, counter 12, `stain shown`). Pictures `docs/evidence/g4b-died-counter-zero.png` (YOU DIED, counter 0, a few sparkles by the body) and `docs/evidence/g4b-stain-loaded.png` (the stain loaded from a save in the meadow:
  the dark disc seen edge-on at the left, its sparkles above it; a `WOWSOULS_SHOT` exits the game after the picture, so one run gives one picture).
  Gates: fmt, clippy `-D warnings` workspace, tests **wow-souls 394, wow-world 126 + 4**, dsr-cache 53, dsr-formats 51, wscl 4 (both installs set); no install file changed since the session start; benilla clean.
  **For the user's eyes:** the stain's look (the disc is plain dark red, the DS bloodstain has more glow; the sparkles are WoW's loot ones, small), whether 1.5 yd / the instant recovery at the spawn feels right, the toast.
  **Not built / known:** a death in the void or off the map leaves a stain in the void (no rescue), stains do not snap to ground when loaded from a hand-written save, souls have no sink yet (the item phase), the plain WoW death
  (no DS kit) uses the same hook.
- **Then, the user's request: talent trees and abilities** (the open sub-project H "WoW abilities" of the spec decomposition; the player has no class abilities yet, the class is Warrior): a discussion first, nothing decided.
  Inputs to bring: level now matters (vitals), the class quests are Warrior-only (3100), the DS moveset is the only attack set, parry / block exist, souls have no sink yet.

**Parallel combat work (2026-10-09): player block/parry BUILT; dodge confirmed as medium roll.**
Read `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-09-player-defense-built.md` for sources, prototype rules and live evidence;
plan `docs/plans/2026-10-09-player-defense.md`. Nothing committed. World/quest work continues in its existing order.
- Hold **E / LB** to guard the Shortsword (DS two-hand raise/hold/lower); **C / LT** parries with the empty left hand.
  Guard activates at 0.200 s, physical cut 50%, stability 32; real collision spends guardAtkRate × 0.68 stamina.
  Block keeps guard, applies chip damage, metal feedback and a small shove. Walking is allowed at 2.5 yd/s;
  guard pauses stamina regeneration. At zero stamina, directional `a00_2080..2083` breaks guard without i-frames.
- Bare-hand `a42_5100`: judge 450, 14 stamina, 0.55 m swept left-hand volume, active 0–0.233 s, no invulnerability.
  Kobold variants, thug and Garrick play their `a00_2100` and lose control for its flag-41 window (1.833 s).
  Wolves cannot be parried; throws bypass defense. Front half-plane, stamina formula and wind-up-through-final-hit
  parry eligibility are explicit prototype rules (including later combo hits). Riposte, shields, enemy guard/parry and
  additive guard-hit overlays remain follow-ups.
- Extractor: `wow-souls-dsr defense` extends the version-6 cache with optional defense records and the creature
  parried clips; ordinary `extract` includes them too. All 11 retargeted pairs passed the existing drift/foot gates.
  Older caches omit defense safely; numbers/windows/bone counts and source size/mtime are checked on load.
- **Roll audit:** both the old and current manifest name **a00_0710 (medium)**, cancel 1.100 s, 11 i-frames (0–0.367 s).
  Fat is a00_0720, cancel 1.600 s. Live medium roll: 4.13 yd in 1.10 s with movement held. The incomplete-list
  extractor fallback could pick fat; it now requires the medium clip and fails if it is missing (regression tested).
  A roll returning to held guard also uses the 1.10 s cancel (final trace 4.16 yd / 1.12 s, one-frame quantization);
  action tracing now closes at that transition, so it does
  not count the later guard walking as roll travel (which misleadingly logged 1.60 s). Equip-load selection is not built.
- Gates: fmt check, workspace clippy `-D warnings`, **510 workspace tests** with both installs set (dsr-cache 53,
  dsr-formats 51, game 347, wow-world 53+2, wscl 4), game/extractor builds. Live checks: front/back guard, throw
  bypass, stamina break and exposed next hit, successful parries of all three kits, late parry and wolf failure,
  guard walking/release/R1/backstep, medium-roll travel/cancel. Every run passed the ECS fault gate.
  Later combo verified too: Garrick double's first hit blocks (20 -> 10), then its second wind-up is parried
  at t=0.962 s (stamina 59.2 -> 45.2), repeated at t=0.973 s; the second hit never lands.
  Logs/scenarios `cache/defense-work/`; pose evidence `docs/evidence/defense-{guard,parry,break}.png` in the session
  folder. Install metadata comparison: 0 changes in 175 WoW files (excluding realmlist.wtf) and 6038 DSR files.

Checked: every species alone against a still player strikes within its first moves (kobold 25, vermin 18, worker 23, Timber Wolf 18-19, Young Wolf 13, thug 27, Garrick 20+20). Lesson for scripted checks: run each new creature once against a player who does nothing.

Retarget: drift 0.06 deg, cross drift up to 3.2 deg, foot gap up to 0.06 yd, scale 1.1347. The extractor refuses a move whose windows carry different judge ids. `wow-souls-dsr hud` now also exports `F20_boss_HP` (re-run: `cargo run -j 4 -p dsr-cache --bin wow-souls-dsr -- hud`; the game skips the boss bar with one log line if the HUD cache is older); `extract` was re-run too.

**E2-3a (2026-10-09, after the user's "Läs handover och fortsätt köra"; inline, nothing committed): the kobold Vermin, the kobold Worker and the Young Wolf are in, and the player's blade is as fat as Dark Souls'.**
- **Species rows on shared kits.** `SpeciesDef` has `kit` (the manifest's monster name; kobold / wolf / thug) apart from `name` (the species: `vermin`, `worker`, `young_wolf`), `damage_scale` (a blow does the kit's damage x this, rounded: `npc::strike`, also in the wind-up log) and `hit_floor`. Every kit lookup (`audio`, `monster::pose`, `npc` spawn / react / think / strike, `dsview`'s `SpeciesView.kit`) goes through `species.0.kit`. `species::VERMIN` (display 10913 at 0.7, chase 3.3 = 4.0 x 0.7 / 0.85, 40 HP, x0.7), `WORKER` (10912 albino at 0.85, 60 HP, x0.9), `YOUNG_WOLF` (447 at 0.4, chase 2.8 = the run clip's 6.94 x 0.4, 30 HP, x0.7, `Blow::Bone`). Tables in `pattern.rs`: `VERMIN` (small_1 band 0-1.65 then small_2 30 %), `WORKER` (the kobold's small_1 / small_2 / big without the charges), `YOUNG_WOLF` (bite 0-0.95; the Timber Wolf's band x 0.4 / 0.55). The bands scale with the entity scale because the root-motion lunge does. HP / damage scales are tuning values judged against the sources' levels and HP (WotLK-era wowclassicdb numbers, ratios only: Vermin 42-55, Worker 71, Laborer 71-86, Young Wolf 42), not data. The sources also name a **Gug Fatcandle** (a named kobold at the Echo Ridge Mine; unverified, not built) and say Young Wolves travel with a Mangy Wolf (not built).
- **Slice groups** (`slice.rs`, now six): vermin 3 at WoW -8860,-160 (+8,+6 / -4,+12), workers 2 at -8850,-125 (+8,+8), young wolves 2 at -8930,-70 (-5,+8) for a person playing, none in a scripted run unless `WOWSOULS_VERMIN=<0-3>` / `_WORKERS=<0-2>` / `_YOUNG_WOLVES=<0-2>` or `WOWSOULS_VERMIN_AT` / `_WORKER_AT` / `_YOUNG_WOLF_AT=x,y[,z]`. The spots are CANDIDATES: ground snaps (80.0-80.9 for the vermin and workers, 89.9 for the young wolves, a wooded rise) and the groups stand 20 yd clear (a test), but nobody has looked at them in a picture (the overhead observer landed on the Abbey roof; creatures this small are a few pixels from above). The vermin stand just behind the Abbey's east wing. Counts and density are the open spawn-rate question below.
- **Why the blade changed (the user: small enemies, swinging over them feels wrong, how does Dark Souls handle it?).** Measured on the Young Wolf: in the R1-1 hit window the blade tip runs 2.6 -> 1.0 yd over the ground and the pup is 0.5 yd tall, so the swing never touched it (the nearest it came to the bone capsules was 0.17-0.3 yd). Dark Souls' answer, from the install: attack hit volumes are fat. `AtkParam_Pc.hit0_Radius` of the straight sword is 0.4 m on R1 (0.3 / 0.35 on R2, 0.35 on the rolling attack), and the game used a hand-picked 0.18 yd. Now: the extractor reads `hit0_Radius` / `hit1_Radius` (`params::Behavior.radius`, the larger goes on the move line as `radius <m>`), manifest `wscl-cache 6` (re-extract: `cargo run -j 4 -p dsr-cache --bin wow-souls-dsr -- extract`), `SwingSpec.radius` (yd, x 1.0936) and `blade_hits(..., blade_r)` use it; `BLADE_RADIUS` 0.18 stays for the no-cache procedural swing. That alone is not enough for a 0.5 yd target (the blade is still 1.0 yd up when the window ends), so `hits::HitFloor` (set by `SpeciesDef.hit_floor`, 0.35 yd only for the Young Wolf) makes each of its body capsules at least that thick for the player's blade. Live: the Young Wolf now dies to two R1 (30 -> 10 -> 0). Not read: how DS's own enemy hurt volumes are sized (the creatures' HKX), so "DS makes small enemies easy to hit" is only half shown: DS's blades are fat, which is measured; DS has almost no enemy as small as 0.5 yd, which is not. Open for the user: whether the thicker blade makes every fight too generous (it is DS's number), whether the pup's floor reads right, and whether other small creatures later (critters) need a floor too. The hit radius of the kobold's and the others' blows was already the DS data.
- **Verified**: gates fmt, clippy `-D warnings` workspace, tests (installs set) dsr-cache 50 / dsr-formats 51 / wow-souls 286 / wscl 4 (new: `install_the_swords_blows_are_as_fat_as_measured`, species / kit tests); live (unattended, one creature each at WoW -8911,-136, `WOWSOULS_KOBOLDS=0 WOWSOULS_SEED=7`): the Vermin's `small_1` lands 18 (kit 25 x 0.7), 40 -> 20 on a hit; the Worker's lands 23 (x 0.9) and dies 60 -> 40 -> 20 -> 0; the Young Wolf's bite lands 13 (19 x 0.7) and it dies in two blows. No pictures of the new creatures yet (they use the kobold and Timber Wolf models the user has seen; only the scale, the albino skin and the pup's size are new): ask the user to look at them live (default run, no env).
- NEXT: E2-3 Garrick (`c2400`, Elite, multi-window attacks, a boss bar), then quests and XP; first the user's eyes on the three new creatures, the thicker blade and their spots.

**E2-2b (2026-10-09, the user: "Kan vi inte bara använda defias fienderna från wow? ... Du behöver bara använda human, det är allt som finns i northshire. Se till att det är rätt defias. Du kan kolla wowhead"): the thief became WoW's own Defias Thug, and the actors can now wear a character NPC's gear.** What was researched (classicdb.ch, a vanilla DB that prints each NPC's model = `CreatureDisplayInfo` id as `/models/<id>.gif`; wowhead's pages give no ids to WebFetch; the client's `WDB/*.wdb` caches are empty, 28 bytes): Northshire Valley's Defias are the **Defias Thug** (npc 38, display **5035**, level 3-4, `CreatureDisplayInfoExtra` 883: human male, bandana helm item display 15308, chest 13636, belt 8551, legs 8552, boots 12197, baked atlas) and **Garrick Padfoot** (npc 103, display **2073**, level 5, extra 676: red outfit, helm 15308, shirt 8128, belt 4592, legs 8129, boots 8130, gloves 8131), not a "Defias Thief" (the Defias Bandit npc 116 = display 2357 is Elwynn's). The wolf inside the valley is the **Timber Wolf** (npc 69, display **604**, scale 0.55), not the Young Wolf (npc 299, display 447, outside the gate): `species::WOLF` is now display 604, title "Timber Wolf", chase 3.8 yd/s (6.94 x 0.55), bands bite 0-1.3, lunge 1.6-2.4 (rescaled from 0.65). The kobold (display 365) is the Kobold Laborer (npc 80); the valley's other kobolds are Vermin (npc 6, display 10913, 0.7) and Worker (npc 257, display 10912, albino 0.85). Sources also say the **Kobold Geomancer** lives in the Jasperlode Mine in Elwynn Forest, not in Northshire (warcraft.wiki.gg, one source: unverified against the 1.12 spawn data, which the client does not have): the geomancer is the user's call. Renamed: species / kit `thug` (title "Defias Thug", env `WOWSOULS_THUG_AT`, `WOWSOULS_THUGS`; `WOWSOULS_<SPECIES>_DISPLAY=<id>` looks at another display for any species), `pattern::THUG`, `slice::THUG_AT`; the DS-side names keep "thief" (`bonemap::THIEF` / `THIEF_RIG` = c2520, the undead thief). The actor builder: `ActorModel.worn: Option<NpcWorn {bake_name, equipment[10]}>` is filled from a display's `npc_appearance` when no `CharLook` is given (the player's avatar still passes one); `build` loads the baked atlas `mpq://textures/bakednpctextures/<name>` as the body (never composited), builds `EquipGeosets` from the equipment rows (bodyslots 2-9 by `geoset_groups`, `forearm_dressed`, the helm's `worn_helm_vis` hiding hair) as benilla's `equip_geosets` does, and inserts `held::WornItems` (helm: `Item\ObjectComponents\Head\<model>_<Race><M|F>.m2` on attachment 11; shoulders left / right on 6 / 5 from `Shoulder\`), built by the new `held::build_worn` once their models are in (`spawn_item_parts` is the part-spawning code shared with the weapon). Pictures: `docs/evidence/thug-sheet-*.png` (the contact sheets again, now with the real Defias look), `wolf-sheet-a00_3000` / `-3004` / `-2205` (the Timber Wolf). Gates: fmt, clippy `-D warnings`, tests 49 / 51 / 285 / 4, installs untouched. NEXT: see the Roadmap above (Garrick, the kobold Vermin and Worker, the Young Wolf, then quests and XP, then items). The geomancer is dropped: not a Northshire creature.

**E2-1 BUILT (2026-10-09, the user said "kör" to the refactor and then the wolf with contact sheets to judge; inline, nothing committed): the wolf, in a Dark Souls black dog's moves.** Extractor (`dsr-cache`): `monster::WOLF` (`Tier::Common`, chr `c3340`, NpcParam 334000, variation 33400): attacks `bite` = `a00_3000` (judge 100, hit 0.633-0.867 s of 1.667, phys 104 = 19 damage) and `lunge` = `a00_3004` (judge 130, 0.333-0.700 of 1.500, 2.07 m, phys 100 = 18), flinches level 1 `a00_2000`-`2002` and level 2 `a00_2004`-`2007`, ONE death `a00_2205` (knocked onto its side, ends lying; the dog's `2200` and `2209` sit up on the haunches with the forepaws raised, `2207` is thrown 5.9 m: seen in the contact sheets), `lying: LYING_QUADRUPED` (body under 0.65 of standing, pelvis settling under 0.08 yd; the biped's 0.5 / 0.02 stay for the kobold). The retarget now handles a quadruped: `Legs` are slices (`bonemap::Legs { ds: &[[..;3]], wow: &[[..;3]] }`), `DsNames.facing` is `Facing::FeetToToes(..)` or `Facing::Along(pelvis, head)`, `Checks` gained `relative_soles` (foot heights as drift from the clip's first frame, not an absolute), `ground_lock` (each frame the body is raised or lowered so the lowest WoW paw stands where the lowest DS digit does: the WoW wolf's legs are uniform (0.416 x 3) where the dog's are not (0.37 / 0.20 / 0.34), so paws would sink or hover; the foot gap then reports the lift) and `max_foot_gap` (per rig: 0.08 biped, 0.45 wolf; the wolf's worst is 0.30 yd). Tables: `BLACK_DOG` (`DsNames`: pelvis, facing along pelvis -> head, soles the four first digit joints, hand `Jaw`, forearm `Head`), `WOLF` (23 pairs, the doc comment lists the wolf's 64 bones: 0 root, 19 -> 22 -> 26 rear, 20 front legs, 21 -> 25 -> 29 head -> 47 jaw, hind 36 -> 55 -> 60 -> 62, front 23 -> 27 -> 43 -> 57, tail 38-42), `WOLF_RIG` (the hind legs set the scale: 1.274; hip root bone 0; cross check only the hind hip line, because the shoulders ride on moving collarbones and the WoW front hangs off the root). New tools: `wow-souls-dsr probe --kit wolf --clips a00_2205,..` (retarget numbers without the gates: drift, foot gap, body and pelvis heights, how far the pelvis still moves at the end, head push) and `WOWSOULS_LOOKDEV=1` (a death that does not lie only warns, to look at a candidate clip in the game). Game: `species::WOLF` (display 372 = the timber wolf at 0.65, health 40, collision 1.3 x 0.45, chase 4.5 yd/s = the run clip's 6.94 x 0.65, `Blow::Bone` with `reach_length` 0.35: the bite volume starts at the jaw bone and runs along head -> jaw, the radius is AtkParam's 0.5 x 1.274 x 0.65), `pattern::WOLF` (bite band 0-1.5 weight 3, lunge band 1.9-2.8 weight 2: the lunge carries it 1.2 yd by the time its window is open, 1.7 in all), `slice::wolf_spots()` (first at WoW -8898,-94, the second at -8904,-80; both open meadow checked by picture; 2 for a person playing, 0 unattended unless `WOWSOULS_WOLVES=<0-2>` or `WOWSOULS_WOLF_AT=<x>,<y>[,<z>]` asks), the swish is only for a swung blow (`Blow::Hand`). Sound, blood and the HUD name needed no code: the wolf's own aggro bark (kit 390 mWolfAggro), attack grunt (391 mWolfAttack), blow on the player (3176 BiteMedium), blood on attachments 15/16, "Wolf" over its bar. Verified: gates fmt, clippy `-D warnings` workspace, tests dsr-cache 48 / dsr-formats 51 / wow-souls 284 / wscl 4 (install tests included: `install_the_wolfs_moves_match_the_data_table`), both installs untouched; the old kobold / player blocks of `cache/dsr` byte-identical; live: a wolf against a still player (aggro bark, lunge from 2.7 yd lands 18 at t=0.63, bite from 1.4 yd lands 19 at t=0.63-0.65, no swish for a bite), R1 x2 kills it (40 -> 20 -> 0, shove 0.79 yd, flinch level 1 0.67 s, `wolf dies`, it lies on its side in the grass), the species view `WOWSOULS_DSVIEW=wolf:<clip>@<s>`. Pictures (the user judges the look first): `docs/evidence/wolf-sheet-a00_3000.png` (bite), `-a00_3004` (lunge), `-a00_2000` / `-2004` / `-2007` (flinches), `-a00_2200` / `-2205` / `-2209` (the three deaths tried), `wolf-sheet-death-live.png` (the real death in the game), four stills each beside the DS skeleton in gold; `scratchpad/species_sheets.sh` + `compose_sheet.py` made them (one game run per frame: `WOWSOULS_DSVIEW=<species>:<clip>@<s>`, observer view, `WOWSOULS_KOBOLDS=0`). **Previously (2026-10-09): E2 specified, the enemy roster (wolf, Defias thief, Garrick, kobold geomancer)** (spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-09-enemy-roster-design.md`, mirror `docs/enemy-roster-design.md` in the session folder; waiting for the user's go, then E2-0). The user asked to map similar Dark Souls creatures onto ours, picked the black dog for the wolf, and set **rule R1: a creature that borrows from a tougher DS enemy gets only one or two of its attacks** (so nothing ordinary is "helt galet svår"); the spec makes it a `Tier` check in the extractor (`Common` max 2 attacks, level 1-2, one hit window; `Elite` max 5; the kobold's 6 are `Legacy`). Surveyed with the new read-only `wow-souls-dsr survey --chr cNNNN [--npc ID]` (bones, NpcParam row, think bands, every clip's length / root travel / TAE kinds / hit windows with their AtkParam_Npc rows / bullet events). Chr id -> enemy: NpcParam row names + every `map/MapStudio/*.msb` Enemy part (model name; NpcParam id at int 3 of the type data; verified `c2060` = 206000); throwaway Python in the session scratchpad, not in the repo. The mapping: wolf = `c3340` black dog (NpcParam 334000, an ordinary enemy: HP 84, souls 80; quadruped, 58 bones, 6 attacks, full flinch + 4 deaths; moves picked `a00_3000` bite + `a00_3004` lunge), Defias thief = `c2520` undead thief (252000; moves `a00_3003`, `a00_3004`; its `3000`-`3002` have `dmgLevel` 8, which `class_for` would read as a knockdown, so they are skipped until 8 is understood), Garrick = `c2400` assassin (240000, `Elite`, multi-hit windows, needs a boss bar), kobold geomancer = `c4160` Oolacile sorcerer (416000; single bullet `a00_3003` bullet 200 + staff poke `a00_3000`; needs bolts: TAE kind 2 event + `BulletParam`). Non-obvious: the LLM docs' `c2260` "Undead Attack Dog" is the Lance Demon; WoW's client has no creature templates, so display ids (wolf 372/380, geomancer 373) are guesses to judge by eye; the survey's `NpcParam` row names are Shift-JIS; `dmgLevel` 8 appears on the first moves of the thief and the assassin. Build order: E2-0 roster refactor (kobold behaviour unchanged), E2-1 wolf first (the only new retarget problem: a quadruped, needs `Legs` as slices and a `Facing::PelvisToHead`), E2-2 thief, E2-3 Garrick, E2-4 geomancer.

**Latest (2026-10-08, night): F is built, the Dark Souls HUD, plus two things the user asked for on the way: more kobolds, and corpses that lie right** (spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-08-ds-hud-design.md` with its "Built" note, plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-08-ds-hud.md` with "Deviations found while executing"; nothing committed). The user was away ("kör på", no questions), so every choice below is Claude's and open to their eyes.
- **The HUD is Dark Souls' own, layout and art read from the install.** `menu/menu.drb.dcx` is DS's whole UI layout (638 dialogs of named objects in a 1280 x 720 space; the HUD is `F20-00`, `F20-02`, `soul_param`, `enemy_HP`, `F20_LockCursor`, `F23_YouDied`); the art is in `menu/menu_N.tpf.dcx` + `menu/ENGLISH/menu_local.tpf.dcx` (DXT1/3/5); the font is `font/english/DSFont24`. New readers in `dsr-formats`: `tpf.rs`, `drb.rs` (sections, dialogs, `Sprite` / `MonoRect` / `GouraudRect` / `Text` records; the layout of each is in the file's header comment, found by decoding the install), `ccm.rs`; in `dsr-cache`: `dds.rs` (DXT via `texpresso`, PNG via `png`, both already in `Cargo.lock`) and `hud.rs` (`PARTS` table, `wow-souls-dsr hud` -> `cache/dsr/hud/`: one PNG per sprite, an additive sprite on black becomes straight alpha, two font pages, `manifest.txt` `wscl-hud 1` with `dialog` / `place` / `sprite` / `rect` / `gradient` / `text` / `font` / `page` / `glyph` lines). `getSoul` rides in the main manifest (`souls kobold 150`).
- **Game (`hud/`, see the architecture rows)**: the layout is 1280 x 720 and `UiScale` = window height / 720 (default window 1600 x 900 = 1.25); left items hang from the left edge, the souls counter from the right. Player bars: black plate, pale-yellow chip that holds 0.55 s then drains, red / green fill, ruler cropped to the length (max x 3 layout px), end cap, the fixed head and the skull (`ring_BG`, `icon_ring_1` = the black ring, `icon_stone`). Souls counter bottom right: a kill gives 150 (`NpcParam.getSoul`), the count rolls up, the gain shows above and fades (`Souls`, `Counter`; dying does not lose them, that and the bloodstain are G). An enemy bar per struck creature (up to 8) with its name and damage run in the DS font. The lock-on ring replaces the gizmo dot. YOU DIED: dim, a band with soft edges, the red text, black from 3.4 s (the layout's own animation is not read; timing is ours); DS blends in gamma space so black overlays use `ds_alpha`. Without the cache or with `WOWSOULS_HUD=0` the old plain HUD stays (`hud/plain.rs`). `WOWSOULS_HUD_GAIN=<x>` brightens the art (DS's bars are dark: red 66,24,24; shown as shipped).
- **More kobolds (the user's request)**: `slice::kobold_spots`: the first at `kobold_at()`, three more at fixed offsets (8-15 yd apart, open meadow, ground heights 80.8-81.8); default 4 for a person playing, 1 for an unattended run or with `WOWSOULS_KOBOLD_AT` (the scripted checks in this file assume a lone kobold), `WOWSOULS_KOBOLDS=<0-4>` overrides. **F6** (or `WOWSOULS_RESET=<s>`) sends every creature home, whole, to fight again without dying. Soak: 4 kobolds against a still player, 70 s, exit 0, no warnings, five death / respawn cycles.
- **Corpses (the user's request) were a bone-map error**: `KOBOLD_RIG.hips.wow_hip_root` was bone 18 (the legs' root) but the spine hangs from bone 13, so when the pelvis dropped to the ground the spine, head and arms stayed standing (at rest the corpse's bone capsules reached 1.21 yd over its feet; the DS joints end within 0.18). Bone 13 now (`bonemap.rs`), the extractor refuses a death that ends more than half as tall as it began (`Retargeted.body_height`, `check_death`; with the old map it fails: "a00_2202: the body ends 1.03 yd tall from 1.39"), `EXTRACTOR_VERSION` 5 (re-extract: `cargo run -j 4 -p dsr-cache --bin wow-souls-dsr -- extract`, 0.5 s; the HUD cache is the separate `-- hud`). Corpses now end at most 0.55 yd over the feet (the candle hat); attacks and flinches checked unchanged. `WOWSOULS_TRACE` prints the corpse's bone extent (`dead[n] ... bone centres (n, lowest, highest)`); the cloth chain under the pelvis still sinks 0.3 yd into the ground (undriven bones keep WoW's death pose).
- **Instruments added**: `WOWSOULS_SHOT_DEATH=<s>[,<s>...]` (frames after the player's first death), `WOWSOULS_RESET`, `WOWSOULS_KOBOLDS`, `WOWSOULS_HUD`, `WOWSOULS_HUD_GAIN`.
- **Verified live (unattended)**: idle at 1280 x 720, 1600 x 900 and 1920 x 1080 (`docs/evidence/hud-idle-*.png`, `hud-sheet.png`), a kill with the counter rolling and the stamina chip (`hud-souls-5.9.png`), the enemy bar (`hud-enemy-4.9.png`), YOU DIED 0.6 / 1.5 / 3.2 / 4.1 s after the death (`hud-died-*.png`), the plain fallback (`hud-plain-1280.png`), the kobold group from above (`kobolds-group-top.png`), corpses before / after (`corpse-sheet.png`).
