**Latest (2026-10-08, night): D3 is built, the player's Dark Souls reactions and poise** (spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-08-player-reactions-design.md` with its "Built" note, plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-08-player-reactions.md`; nothing committed). A creature's blow on the player now plays the player's own DS clip, held for its own TAE control windows: the kobold's level 1 blows (`small_1`, `small_2`, `pounce`) a small flinch (`a00_2000`-`2003`, control back at 0.60 s), level 2 (`big`, `run_strike`) a medium one (`2004`-`2007`, 1.03 s), level 3-4 a large one (`2010`-`2013`, 1.45 s), level 5 (`body_check`) a knockdown (`2026`/`2027`: thrown 3.2-3.7 yd straight back or forward, invulnerable while down (`f8`), up again at 3.0 s). Which clip of a class: a flinch by its head's measured push, a knockdown by its travel. At zero health a knockdown clip is held on the lying frame (`a00_2026` / `2027`, from 1.13 / 1.40 s). The `a00_2200`-`2213` "death" clips do not retarget (no ordinary pose). Player poise: `Poise` on the avatar (`monster::Poise`), `reaction::player_poise()` 0 by default (every blow flinches), `WOWSOULS_POISE=<n>`; a blow that does not break it takes health, shows blood and shake, and no flinch. A flinch keeps the D2a shove, a knockdown is thrown by its clip.
- Extractor: `dsr-cache/src/reacts.rs` (the hand table: 16 reactions in classes s/m/l/down/down2, deaths `a00_2026`/`2027`), `cache.rs` `extract_player_reacts` (retarget, head push, flag times, `f8` window, lying time), manifest `wscl-cache 5` with `preact` / `pdeath` lines (rebuild: `cargo run -j 4 -p dsr-cache --bin wow-souls-dsr -- extract`). Game: `reaction.rs` (`PlayerReacts`, `LEVEL_CLASSES` = the tuning table level -> class, `choose`, `choose_death`), `ActionState::Reel(Reel{t, clip, spec: ReelSpec})` (attack from `chain_from`, evade from `evade_from`, a pushed stick ends it at `move_from`, else at the clip's end; `Action::invulnerable()` inside `spec.invuln`), `Action.death` / `dead_t`, `dsclip` `ClipId::React` / `Death` (a death clamps to its hold time), `combat::reel_step`, `hurt::suffer`, `PlayerHurt.poise`, `WOWSOULS_DSVIEW=<clip>@<s>` (a still of a player clip). Without the cache lines the old stagger and WoW death stay (one warning).
- Verified live: see the spec's "Built" note (forced `small_1` / `big` / `body_check`, death after three `big`, poise 100, no cache). Gates: fmt, clippy `-D warnings` clean, tests wow-souls 238, dsr-cache 28, dsr-formats 29, wscl 4 (install tests included); both installs identical before and after. Pictures in `docs/evidence/react-*.png`.
- Open for the user's eyes and hands: the look of the flinches and the knockdown, which blow level answers which class (`LEVEL_CLASSES` in `reaction.rs`: e.g. level 5 as `down` = 6.4 yd instead of 3.2), and the feel: the player is helpless 0.6 s / 1 s / 1.5 s / 3 s (it was 0.5 s). Not done: the sideways knockdowns (root yaw), armor-style poise, hyper-armor frames in the player's moves, guard / parry reactions, ragdoll. NEXT: sub-project F, the Dark Souls HUD (its own spec).

**Latest (2026-10-08, evening): D2b is built, WoW's own blood spurt** (spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-08-blood-design.md` with its "Built" note, plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-08-blood.md`; nothing committed). A landed blow, either way, plays the client's melee blood spurt: the victim's blood row (kobold and human male both `BloodID` 1, red) -> `UnitBlood` -> `Particles\BloodSpurts\BloodSpurt[Large].m2` (a particle-only M2 with 4 emitters: red droplet streaks and a white-orange flash, the "spark"; 1.12 has no other spark effect). `blood.rs`: `BloodTables` (blood + spell-visual catalogs, loaded on entering `Playing`), `is_front` (flat dot of the victim's forward and the way to the attacker -> attachment 15 front / 16 back), `is_large` (level >= 2), a `Spurt` root under the victim's attachment anchor (`RigPose::anchor_for`, the held-item pattern), `build_spurts` (when the M2 has loaded: `spawn_emitter` per `ModelEmitter`, owner = the root, `OwnerLoss::Free`, `EmitClock::Effect(None)`), `reap_spurts` (`SPURT_LIFE` 1.6 s), a new spurt on the same body and side replaces the old. Triggers: `Hit` (player's blow) and `PlayerHurt` (a creature's). `ActorModel.display` is new. `hits::flash_impacts` (the gizmo cross) draws only when there are no `BloodTables`. `WOWSOULS_BLOOD=<x>` (0 = off, 1 default, a number scales the copies) ; **turned up at the user's request**: the droplet (alpha) emitters are spawned `DROPLET_COPIES` 3 times with their own seeds at `DROPLET_SIZE` 1.6x, the additive flash once (peak live particles about 40 -> about 80; pictures `docs/evidence/blood2-r1-*.png`); `WOWSOULS_TRACE=1` logs `blood: N spurt(s), M live particles` every 0.1 s.
- Verified live (unattended): R1 on a kobold: `blood: creature front small ...BloodSpurt.mdl`, `spurt built, 4 of 4 emitters`, live particles rise to about 40 and drain to 0 in about 0.7 s, two hits on the same side keep one spurt; R2: `front large ...BloodSpurtLarge.mdl`; the kobold's `small_1` on the player: `player front small`, the kobold behind: `player back small`; `WOWSOULS_BLOOD=0`: `blood: off`, no spurt. Pictures `docs/evidence/blood-r1-3.85*.png`, `blood-r1-3.92*.png` (`-zoom` 4x on the kobold): flash and red streaks visible. Gates: fmt, clippy `-D warnings` clean, tests wow-souls 229, dsr-cache 26, dsr-formats 29, wscl 4 (install tests included); both installs identical before and after.
- Open for the user's eyes: the look on the kobold and on the player (size, brightness, chest placement; WoW's red is brighter and thinner than Dark Souls' dark blood), whether the flash reads as the spark. Not done: ground splats (1.12.1 never reads them), a Dark Souls blood (FFX in the install, a separate extractor job), the effect model's own bone motion (the emitters ride the root, the model is static), the player's DS reactions and poise, footsteps, DS sounds.

**Latest (2026-10-08, later): D2a is built, impact feel from the data** (spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-08-impact-feel-design.md`, plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-08-impact-feel.md`; nothing committed). The extractor reads `hitStopTime` and `knockbackDist` from `AtkParam_Pc` / `AtkParam_Npc` onto every `move` / `attack` manifest line (`stop <s> knock <m>`; manifest `wscl-cache 4`, the game's `CACHE_VERSION` "4"; rebuild with `cargo run -j 4 -p dsr-cache --bin wow-souls-dsr -- extract`). The data: the sword's hit-stop is 0.08 s on every move (no "by weight" scale exists), the kobold's blows have 0.0; knock-back sword 0.72 / 0.84 / 0.72 (R1), 0.95 / 0.90 (R2), 0.80 (rolling attack), kobold 0.45 / 0.45 / 1.0 / 1.0 / 1.37 (body check) / 1.0 m; the kobold's flinch clips have zero root motion, so the knock-back is their only push.
- `SwingSpec` / `AttackSpec` carry `stop` and `knock`, `Hit` carries `knock`, `PlayerHurt` carries `level`, `stop`, `knock`. `strike` freezes the world for the move's own `stop` (0.08 s) and not at all for 0; `suffer` for the creature's (0, so being hit no longer freezes). Without a cache the old values stay (swing freeze 0.07 s, hurt freeze 0.05 s, no knock-back).
- `shove.rs`: `Shove` component (flat unit direction, `knock x 1.0936` yards, `SHOVE_TIME` 0.30 s, eased `1-(1-u)^2`, fastest first) driven through `MoveIntent` / the motor (`root_step`), so walls stop it; `push_avatar` after `PlaySet::Intent` and before `PlaySet::Motor`, `push_hostiles` between `think` and `exclude_bodies` in the creatures' chain; started in `npc::react` (non-lethal, poise broken) and `hurt::suffer` (non-lethal); `face_motion` skips a shoved body; a respawn clears every shove; the slide ends one frame after its last step with an idle intent (see the spec's "Built" note).
- `shake.rs`: `Shake { trauma }` kicked per blow (taken: level 1 = 0.35, 2-4 = 0.55, 5+ = 0.9; dealt: level 1 = 0, 2-4 = 0.25, 5+ = 0.4), decays 2.5/s in real time, `shake_offset` = sines x trauma^2 (1.2 deg, 0.04 yd at full), applied in `camera::seat` on the seated pose each frame; `WOWSOULS_SHAKE=0` turns it off. Tuning values, not data.
- Verified live (unattended): R1-1 on a kobold 2 yd away logs `hit: ... (stop 0.08 s, knock 0.72 m)`, `shove: kobold 0.79 yd`, and its feet trace moves 0.78 yd (R1-2: 0.91 of 0.92); R2 `knock 0.95` -> `shove: kobold 1.04 yd`, `shake: kick 0.25 (level 2, dealt)`; the kobold's `body_check` -> `shove: player 1.50 yd` (trace -8906.00 -> -8907.50), `shake: kick 0.90`, `stop 0.00 s`; `small_1` -> 0.49 yd, kick 0.35; the player's yaw stays +0.00 through both; `WOWSOULS_SHAKE=0` logs no `shake:` line and still shoves; `WOWSOULS_DSR_CACHE=/nonexistent` runs the old fight (stop 0.05 / 0.07, knock 0.00). Gates: fmt, clippy `-D warnings` clean, tests wow-souls 225, dsr-cache 26, dsr-formats 29, wscl 4 (install tests included); both installs identical before and after.
- Open for the user's hands and eyes: how far and how fast a blow pushes (`SHOVE_TIME`, `METRES_TO_YARDS` in `shove.rs`), whether being hit without any freeze reads right (`HURT_STOP` only remains for the no-cache kobold), and the shake (strength in `shake.rs`, or off with `WOWSOULS_SHAKE=0`). Not done: D2b blood and sparks (WoW's own particle effects are the likely source; a survey of the install first), shoves on death, the player's DS reactions and poise, footsteps, DS sounds.

**Latest (2026-10-08, night): sound is in (sub-project D1)**, with WoW's own sounds (spec
`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-08-fight-sound-design.md`; nothing committed). `audio.rs` (the Bevy side:
`Audio` resource with the five client tables, a decoded-sound cache, the triggers), `mixer.rs` (a pure
`Mixer` and `Output`, a thread that owns the cpal 0.17 stream), `sfx.rs` (decode with symphonia, weighted
variation, gain, pan). New workspace dependencies `cpal = 0.17` and `symphonia = 0.6` (`wav`, `pcm`,
`adpcm`), both in the local cargo cache. Triggers: the player's swing plays the sword's swish (Sword1H is
medium, kit 235) 0.08 s before the hit window; a landed blow plays the sword's flesh impact (kit 143) at the
hit point and the kobold's wound vocal (60 %, kit 315) or death cry (kit 314); the kobold's aggro bark (694),
its attack grunt at the telegraph (70 %, kit 312, `KoboldAttack` A-C), its swish before its hit window, and
its blow on the player (the unarmed flesh impact, kit 1014: the kobold row has no `CustomAttack`). Unattended
runs are silent unless `WOWSOULS_AUDIO=1` or `null` (see README). Verified: 23 sound files of the fight's
kits decode (install test), a null run through a whole fight logs every trigger in order, one real-device run
(48 kHz, volume 0.12) played them without an error; mixer and sfx arithmetic are unit-tested. Gates: fmt and
clippy `-D warnings` clean, tests dsr-cache 26, dsr-formats 29, wow-souls 217, wscl 4; both installs
identical before and after. **Disk:** C: was full while building (the incremental cache and old duplicate
rlibs in `target/debug` were removed to make 11 GB room; `cargo build` is slower until the incremental cache is
back). Open for the user's ears: the volumes (`SCALE_*` and the master in `audio.rs`), which sound for
which event, the swish timing (`SWISH_LEAD`). Not done (D2 and later): hit-stop by weight, camera shake,
knock-back from `knockbackDist`, sparks and blood, the player's own wound voice and Dark Souls reactions and
poise, footsteps, ambience, Dark Souls' own sounds (FMOD FSB4 + FEV: a separate extractor job).

The decision history for E follows (kept for its reasoning).

Why E: the player's side is Dark Souls' own now (the roll, the three-move R1 chain, R2 with its
follow-up and the rolling attack, all from the install's TAE and params). The kobold is still the
stand-in from milestone 2 step 5: WoW's `Attack1H` clip (17) at 0.7x, one attack, a strike window
measured by hand (0.34-0.48 of a 1.43 s swing), 25 damage, WoW's wound (9) and death (1) clips as
reactions. Next to the new player moves the duel is lopsided (the showcase video shows it).

The decomposition (spec intro): A data reader and B retarget are done, C player movesets is done, then
D impact, E enemy movesets, F DS HUD, G playable Northshire, H WoW abilities. Take D's enemy half
(flinch, stagger and death clips, poise as a number) into E: it uses the same creature rig and the same
data. Leave the rest of D (hit-stop tuning, sparks, camera shake, player poise) until after E.

How to start: the workflow used for A-C. Brainstorm with the user, write the spec
(`docs/superpowers/specs/<date>-enemy-movesets-design.md`), write the plan, execute it inline unless the
user asks for subagents. Show the user the look early with a side-by-side like `WOWSOULS_DSVIEW`: the user
judges the look by eye.

Suggested order:
1. **Survey, no game code.** `chr/` in the DSR install holds about 170 `cNNNN.anibnd.dcx` rigs (c1000,
   c12xx, c2060, c2210-c2960, c3090-c3530, c4090-c4531, c5200-c5401, c9999). `wow-souls-dsr list` only
   lists the player's roll candidates, and `dsr-cache/src/dsr.rs` loads the player only (`c0000`, its
   animations split over `c0000_a0x` ...), so the loader needs a `chr` argument. An enemy's archive is
   probably one `cNNNN.anibnd.dcx` with its own `Skeleton.hkx`; check. Print per enemy: bone names,
   animation ids with their TAE attack judge ids, and the `NpcParam` / `AtkParam_Npc` rows (names come
   from the FMGs under `msg/ENGLISH`). Pick the DS enemy that fits the kobold (small, hunched, club or
   pick) from that data and a picture, not from `docs/enemy-*.md`. Those LLM-written Swedish docs name
   `c2100` (kobold), `c2200` (Defias) and `c2260` (wolf); `c2100` and `c2200` do not exist in the install,
   so treat every id, number and bone in them as unchecked.
2. **Retarget onto the kobold rig.** Only HumanMale exists today: `dsr-cache/src/wow.rs` loads one M2
   (`HUMAN_MALE_M2`), `bonemap.rs` is the hand-made table of DS bone to M2 bone index (`benilla-extract
   m2bones <model>` prints the indices), `retarget.rs` does the work (bind alignment, root motion scaled
   by the body-height ratio, the 15 degree direction check). The kobold (display 365) is hunched with
   short limbs and the DS enemy has its own skeleton, so expect a new table per pair and a decision on
   posture (keep WoW's rest posture or follow DS's). Judge it side by side before wiring anything.
3. **Attack data from the same sources as the player's.** A hit window is a TAE type-1 event whose
   judge id picks a param row (the player uses `BehaviorParam_PC` and `AtkParam_Pc`; check which rows
   enemies use, probably `AtkParam_Npc`); root motion gives the lunge; the clip's length replaces the
   hand-measured swing. Swap `npc.rs`'s `Attack1H` timings (`STRIKE_FROM`, the 0.34-0.48 window, the
   1.43 s swing) for the clip's. `think_step`, `Telegraph`, `PlayerHurt`, `strike_reaches` and the
   `WOWSOULS_DODGE` instrument must keep working, and a strike still passes through a roll's i-frames.
4. **Patterns.** DS's enemy choice logic is Lua AI (`script/*.luabnd.dcx`, `aiCommon.luabnd.dcx`:
   compiled Lua, not read yet). Start with a hand-written table (move, distance band, weight, cooldown)
   built from the real moves. Decompiling the Lua is a separate decision for the user.
5. **Reactions.** Flinch, stagger and death from the same chr, and poise as a number, replacing `react`'s
   WoW clips 9, 10 and 1.
6. **Other mobs.** The Defias thief and the wolf are not in the slice, only the kobold. A wolf is a
   quadruped, so its retarget is a different problem. G (playable Northshire) decides when they arrive.

Still waiting on the user (their eyes and hands, not code): the look and feel of the sword moves, and
the pad with RT = R2 (live, awake pad, focused window). The roll look is approved.

Rules that still hold: always `-j 4`; never write inside the DSR or WoW installs; DSR-derived data stays
local (`/cache` is gitignored, the GPL extractor stays apart from the MIT/Apache game); benilla is never
modified; the user commits (nothing is committed); live runs are unattended and never grab the user's
mouse; the user wants everything keyboard-testable; downloads and anything public need the user's
explicit yes.

**Uncommitted recorder.** `crates/wow-souls/src/record.rs` (new, with edits in `main.rs`, `boot.rs`,
`run_mode.rs`, `hud.rs`): `WOWSOULS_RECORD=<file.mp4>,<secs>[,<fps>]` records a deterministic video: a
fixed 60 Hz clock (`TimeUpdateStrategy::ManualDuration`, held until `Phase::Playing` plus a 3 s hold and 60
warm-up ticks), `Screenshot::primary_window()` readback piped to an `ffmpeg` child, dev line and help
line hidden, counted as an unattended run (`UNATTENDED_ENVS` has five entries). Gates on 2026-10-08, after
two lint fixes in this file: fmt clean, `cargo clippy -j 4 --workspace --all-targets -- -D warnings`
clean, tests wow-souls 188 (181 plus 7 for the recorder), dsr-formats 29, dsr-cache 17, wscl 4. It is
not in the README or the switch list above. The user has not said keep or delete, and the finished
videos do not use it (the user recorded the screen). Ask before building on it; if kept, document it.

a new download yes; no rain exists in the recording (the night shot is the weather); the shot list is `STORY` / `REDDIT` at the top
of the script if the user wants other moments; the YouTube community post of 2026-10-08 is still not posted.

The user confirmed the vertical slice (2026-10-07) and unlocked Dark Souls integration. Goals:
DS-exact weapon movesets, DS animations on WoW models, each Northshire mob with one fitting DS
enemy's pattern, DS impact, DS-style HUD, levels, a fully playable Northshire with quests; WoW
abilities undecided. Decomposed into sub-projects A-H (spec intro). Milestone 2 step 6 (polish)
is parked.

Built (plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-07-dsr-data-and-roll-retarget.md`, spec
`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-07-dsr-data-and-roll-retarget-design.md`; nothing committed yet):
- Crates: `wscl` (MIT/Apache, `.wscl` clip format: frames, parents, driven mask, root motion,
  TAE flag windows), `dsr-formats` (GPL-3.0-or-later: DCX, BND3, TAE, Havok 2015 tagfile,
  spline-compressed animation, ported from SoulsFormatsNEXT / SoulsAssetPipeline / HavokLib),
  `dsr-cache` (GPL-3.0-or-later, bin `wow-souls-dsr list|extract`: retargets the DS medium roll
  `a00_0710` onto HumanMale, writes `cache/dsr/{manifest.txt,human_male/,c0000/}`). Licence
  split: GPL stays in the extractor; the game reads only `.wscl`. `/cache` is gitignored.
- Game: `dsclip.rs` loads the cache at startup (manifest version + source size/mtime checked;
  missing/stale -> one warning, procedural roll), turns the clip into the roll's `EvadeSpec`
  (`roll_spec`), drives the body by the clip's root motion (`progress`) and poses the rig in
  `PosePost` (blend in 0.1 s, out 0.2 s). `dsview.rs` (`WOWSOULS_DSVIEW=1`): DS skeleton as gold
  gizmo bones 1.5 yd right of the avatar beside the human, lockstep, looping in place, logs
  `dsview t=` every 0.1 s. `WOWSOULS_DSR_CACHE=<dir>` overrides `cache/dsr`.
- Numbers (success criterion 2). Extractor: 62 frames at 30 fps = 2.033 s, root motion 4.47 yd
  (4.16 at the 1.1 s cancel), invulnerable 0.000-0.367 s (TAE flag), cancel from 1.100 s, max
  direction drift 0.06 deg (limit 15; measured as drift from each pair's bind angle), max cross
  drift 2.97 deg, max foot gap 0.038 yd on contact frames (0.078 over all). Live, full roll
  (`WOWSOULS_CHECK=15 WOWSOULS_KOBOLD_AT=-8908,-136 WOWSOULS_DODGE=0.3,-1,0 WOWSOULS_TRACE=1`,
  stick released after the press): `Roll moved 4.47 yd in 2.03 s` / `2.05 s` / `2.04 s` (three
  rolls; real-time frame quantisation, within one 30 fps frame); 4 Hz trace `invuln=true` at
  t=0.03/0.29/0.30, `false` at 0.50/0.54/0.57 (bounds the 0.367 close; the exact window is
  `Evade::invulnerable` on the clip's window, unit test `the_spec_comes_from_the_clip`). Stick
  held: movement cancel at 1.1 s, `Roll moved 4.16 yd in 1.10 s`.
- Fallback (criterion 3): `WOWSOULS_DSR_CACHE=/nonexistent WOWSOULS_CHECK=6 WOWSOULS_ROLL=4
  WOWSOULS_DRIVE=-1,0 WOWSOULS_TRACE=1` -> one `dsclip: no Dark Souls roll (no manifest ...)`
  warning, procedural `Roll moved 4.00 yd in 0.60 s`, tumble 81/307 deg.
- Look (criterion 1, the user's call): evidence folder `dsview-4.10/4.25/4.40/4.55.png` (plan's
  side-on observer `WOWSOULS_OBSERVE=-4,1.2,0`: the DS skeleton stands 1.5 yd behind the human
  along the view, so they overlap) and `dsview-34-4.10/4.25/4.40/4.55.png` (3/4 observer
  `-3.5,1.6,4`, separated). Clip time per shot (+-1 frame): 2.006, 0.132, 0.177, 0.405 and
  2.030, 0.092, 0.267, 0.445 s. Same phase in every pair (upright at the end, head-down dive at
  0.1-0.2 s, inverted legs-up at 0.4 s); no visibly broken limbs or twisted torso.
- Install check: DSR install identical before/after. WoW install: only `realmlist.wtf` differs
  (`set realmlist 127.0.0.1`, written 2026-10-07 11:28 local, during the separate
  `C:\Spel\MaNGOS` setup; nothing in wow-souls writes it).
- Gates: fmt clean; `cargo clippy -j 4 --workspace --all-targets -- -D warnings` clean;
  `cargo test -j 4 --workspace`: wow-souls 139, dsr-formats 20, dsr-cache 13, wscl 3; with
  `DSR_DIR`/`WOW_DATA` set, dsr-formats 20 + dsr-cache 13 pass against the installs.

Open: the look (user judges `WOWSOULS_DSVIEW` and a live roll); DSView's side-on observer
overlaps the two skeletons (offset along the view); the roll is in place in DSVIEW only; C: had
under 1 GB free (a 1 GB PDB failed to link; stale test PDBs in `target/debug/deps` removed).

The user judged the roll look good (2026-10-07). **Plan C (straight-sword moveset) is built**
(plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-07-straight-sword-moveset.md`, spec
`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-07-straight-sword-moveset-design.md`; nothing committed yet). The
light attack is Dark Souls' own, from the install's data: the one-handed R1 chain (`a23_3000`,
`a23_3001`, `a23_3002`, round again after three), R2 (`a23_3300`, then `a23_3310`) and the rolling
attack (`a23_3900`: a light press in a roll or backstep once its cancel time has passed),
retargeted onto the human, timed by their TAE windows, priced by `GameParam`. Without a cache the
old procedural swing and roll stay. Keys: F / LMB / pad RB light, R / RMB / pad RT heavy.
- Extractor: `dsr-formats/param.rs` (PARAMDEF v104 + PARAM), the TAE attack judge, `wscl` v2 (hit
  windows), `dsr-cache` `params.rs` + `moveset.rs` (the hand-made class table `straight_sword`,
  variation 2300) and `cache.rs` (manifest: `class`, six `move` lines, `grip`).
  `EXTRACTOR_VERSION` 3, `wscl-cache` 2. Rebuild: `cargo run -j 4 -p dsr-cache --bin wow-souls-dsr -- extract`.
- Game: see the `combat/` and `dsclip.rs` rows above. Flags read as: f4 attack input (next move),
  f26 evade, f11 movement (inferred from the data pattern, not from the player's ESD).
- Numbers, live (table = the spec's; lunge = net travel on flat ground, the chain pressed at 2.2 s
  so each move runs its whole root motion):

  | move | clip | length s | hit window s | f4 / f26 / f11 s | lunge yd, table / live | stamina | damage, live |
  |---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
  | R1-1 | a23_3000 | 2.500 | 0.533-0.667 | 0.767 / 0.900 / 1.033 | 1.98 / 1.97 | 20 | 20 |
  | R1-2 | a23_3001 | 2.233 | 0.467-0.567 | 0.733 / 0.900 / 1.000 | 1.42 / 1.42 | 20 | 20 |
  | R1-3 | a23_3002 | 2.500 | 0.467-0.600 | 0.767 / 0.900 / 1.033 | 1.98 / 1.98 | 20 | 20 |
  | R2 | a23_3300 | 2.533 | 0.733-0.867 | 1.300 / 1.300 / 1.367 | 1.98 / 1.98 | 45 | 27 |
  | R2 second | a23_3310 | 1.900 | 0.333-0.467 | 0.933 / 0.933 / 1.200 | 0.94 / 0.93 | 35 | 25 |
  | rolling attack | a23_3900 | 2.100 | 0.233-0.333 | 0.700 / 0.767 / 0.867 | 1.42 / 1.42 | 20 | 18 (unit test; the live hit is timing-sensitive) |

  Chain rules seen live: an R1 pressed before f4 waits in the 0.35 s buffer and chains at f4 (0.78 s
  after the swing began); the chain loops after three; R2 -> R2 second -> R2; an evade from f26
  (pressed at 0.70 s it starts at 0.90 s); a held stick ends a swing at f11 (`roll_attack moved
  1.10 yd in 0.88 s`); a light press in a roll after 1.10 s or in a backstep after 0.40 s is the
  rolling attack; a stagger ends the swing; no stamina, no swing. The root motion's recoil
  (R1-2 steps back 0.16 yd after its thrust) is applied: `MoveIntent.speed` may be negative.
- The look, measured: the drawn WoW sword's blade against Dark Souls' blade, both in the body's
  frame, every frame of every clip (`WOWSOULS_DSVIEW=<clip>`, `blade error` per loop): mean
  3.0-3.2 deg, worst 4.5-12.2 deg (limit 15) on all six moves and the roll; unfitted
  (`WOWSOULS_GRIP=0,0,0`) mean 25, worst 32. The 3 deg mean is WoW's own pose of the bones the
  clips do not drive (0 while the body hangs in its spawn pose). The worst frame sits between two
  keyframes in the fastest part of R1-1 (interpolating local rotations is not model-space
  interpolation). Pictures: `blade-<clip>.png` in the evidence folder.
- Gates at the end: fmt, `cargo clippy -j 4 --workspace --all-targets -- -D warnings` clean,
  `cargo test -j 4 --workspace`: wow-souls 181, dsr-formats 29, dsr-cache 17, wscl 4 (with
  `DSR_DIR`/`WOW_DATA` set the install tests run too). Installs: before/after listings identical
  (the WoW listing leaves out `realmlist.wtf`, see above).
- Open: the user's eye on the look (grip, swing, the blends at a move's start and end; the flat
  of the blade is only judged by eye) and on the feel (Dark Souls' timing is slower than the
  prototype's: R1 hits 0.5 s in, chains from 0.77 s, evades from 0.9 s; a kobold at 2 yd strikes
  first, a dodge such as `WOWSOULS_DODGE=0.3` lets an R2 land); the pad with RT = R2 (live,
  awake pad, focused window); parked: running attack (needs a sprint), two-handing and the 4000
  series, jump and plunge, backstab and riposte, left hand, guard and parry, DS locomotion, other
  weapon classes (data-only additions to `moveset.rs`), DS impact (D), poise.
Findings:
- `a20` is the DAGGER set (`EquipParamWeapon.wepmotionCategory` 20). Straight swords are `a23`
  (Shortsword row 200000, `behaviorVariationId` 2300, model `WP_A_0207`). `docs/animations.md` and
  `docs/attacks-swords.md` (LLM-written) still call a20 the sword: wrong, do not trust their ids.
- Hit windows are TAE type-1 events (judge id = third param int); stamina is `BehaviorParam_PC`
  and the damage multiplier `AtkParam_Pc.atkPhysCorrection / 100` (both keyed by variation + judge).
- The controller never worked: benilla's `tuned_default_plugins` disables `GilrsPlugin`, so no
  `Gamepad` entity existed. **Fixed 2026-10-07** in our `boot.rs` (`default_plugins`:
  `.enable::<bevy::gilrs::GilrsPlugin>()`, benilla untouched; test `the_gamepad_plugin_is_on`
  fails without it). New `pad.rs`: `PadStatus` (first pad's name, last button), a connect/disconnect
  log line `gamepad: connected '<name>'`, `describe` for the new bottom-left help line in `hud.rs`
  (keys + `pad: <name> (last RB)` or `pad: none`; F3 hides it and the dev line). Keyboard
  aliases: F = light attack, arrow keys = camera (`camera::look_stick`); the lock button is
  `lockon::lock_pressed`. The pad code paths (move, jump, RB, B, RS click, right stick) are now
  covered by tests on mocked `Gamepad` components (`pad::testkit`). Gates: fmt, clippy `-D
  warnings`, 157 wow-souls tests. R2 (R / RMB / RT) arrived with plan C's R2 move.
  Evidence: gilrs alone saw the user's pad (Bluetooth Xbox, vid 045E pid 02E0, mapping Driver,
  battery 70 %) before it went to sleep; an unattended run then showed `pad: none` on screen
  (`docs/evidence/pad-help.png`) and no gilrs error. Not yet seen end to end with the pad awake:
  WGI gives button input to a focused window only, so the user's live run is the proof (look at
  the bottom-left line).

Clip ids (logged at build): Human attack clip ids present: 16 (AttackUnarmed), 17 (Attack1H), 18 (Attack2H), 19, 20, 21, 23, 24 (no 22);
also 30 (Dodge), 50 (Loot), 96-98 (sit ground), 119/120 (StealthWalk/Stand).
kobold: 0, 4, 5, 16, 17, wounds 9/10, death 1, 62/136, 106, 48, 49, 110, 32.
